### 1010101 · The Opportunist and The Sticky Gum

???：Now those little brats are knocked out. You noticed that old gum sticking to the bottom of their shoes, like crushed leeches.

???：Having been chewed for long, it took on the light amber color that the carbohydrate gum should have. Some folds were cyan. It's most likely mint-flavored.

???：The sales champion of mint gum is the bubble gum machine near Wrigley Field. When a crazy game ends, there's always someone who needs something to chew in the mouth in order to take out the anger over the defeat.

???：But ... Hey. Haven't you noticed yet? That wonderful mix of particles in the chewing gum base that's been squeezed into lumps?

???：The off-white sand. The synthetic waxed elastic fiber. In the South Bank, there is only one place where such a politically symbolic floor is laid—St. Pavlov Foundation.

???：Tragedy. A sympathetic encounter. It has fulfilled its mission cautiously and conscientiously. But clearly, no one cares.

???：I dare say it's the most popular mint gum now. When the Chicagoans lost their rights to get drunk, they could only turn to this nasty upstart stuff.

???：The sales champion is the bubble gum machine near Wrigley Field. The old gum on the kids' soles also comes from there.

???：Some gravel mixed into the squeezed gum base. The off-white sand. The synthetic waxed elastic fiber. In the South Bank, there is only one place where such a politically symbolic floor is laid—St. Pavlov Foundation.

???：Who does? I'm very glad that we are on the same page on this.

???：Describing an abandoned carbohydrate gum won't give anyone pleasure. So, let's skip to the next subject—Have you noticed the gravel mixed into the gum base?

???：The off-white sand. The synthetic waxed elastic fiber. In the South Bank, there is only one place where such a politically symbolic floor is laid—St. Pavlov Foundation.

???：It's very lively here in the square, just like every day in the past. Speculators, who advocate mankind supremacy, are trying to get the attention of the Foundation through demonstrations.

???：They are on the same side as the Foundation, but the Foundation has no intention to treat these protesters as allies and give them what they want. They even sent a little girl to go through the motions as a perfunctory response.

???：Yes. Yes. No one will ever ignore them. They are honey-colored. They are the morning sun in California. They are the dazzling spot on the doe that you'll never catch.

???：For those hypocritical politicians, the little girls are the delicacies that they would drool over. Luckily, this little girl is not one of their prey.

???：She is nervous but not panicked. She doesn't seem to be good at dealing with these slick "politicians." The mission capsule of the Foundation grew hot in her tightly clutched fist.

???：And on the other side of the square, another little girl in a black suit is looking over here gloomily. She stood by the Foundation's air outlet, the best place for her.

???：The position that is too marginal to be further marginalized. That's the best portrayal of the second-generation immigrants in the 20th century.

???：She is the good girl you already know. Orange hair. When she's deep in thought, she turns her pen like a nimble ball in a magician's hand.

???：She is puzzled at the guys shouting around her. But there's light in her eyes, as if she's expecting something nice.

???：Is it because of the next task packed in the mission capsule? Or the people she is going to meet?

???：Her cheeks are a little rosy now. The thing in her hands was loosened too.

???：She's someone you will know in the future.

???：Now she shudders and shivers, like a cricket desperate for shelter. She's looking for an eave or a piece of rubble in the "Storm."

???：Her figure might be frail, but her eyes are filled with cold anger. Maybe she had been rejected just now, or even insulted. She walked up to the square center with firm step, like a warrior.

???：She had to perform another task she didn't like.

???：At this moment, the capsule in the hand of the ad hoc representative of the Foundation dropped under the push of the crowd.

???：—This is a good chance.

???：She feels a hot wind from Africa blow towards Trapani. Then, the whole of Sicily becomes dry, warm, and comes alive. The air smells like tropical corals and wild oranges.

???：That wind is never supposed to be part of this cold winter. But the warm vibe lingering and dancing in her mind reminds her to live on.

???：She used to be desperate.

???：Though all she has been through was failure after failure, her vigorous vitality won't fade away, at least not now.

### 1010501 · Intermission

???：What are you doing? Why did you pick up that cold, opaque, opalescent glass wine bottle from the ground—at this most critical moment?

???：The scream outside the parking lot, the friction between the brake pads and the wheel drums, or the story unfolding like Giallo films. Obviously, none of them are as impressive as this bottle.

???：I can tell that you got good taste. You know it's not easy to get this stuff these days.

???：But, why are you staring at the bottom and licking your lips? Tell me, you are not going to take a sip, are you?

???：This is not a wise choice. You can't see the color of the liquid inside the glass. Maybe it's not the ice wine, or beer, or any homemade alcohol.

???：It's probably—very likely—just a critter's snot. Even so, do you still wanna give it a try?

???：Every Carbuncle you've ever met will appreciate your courage.

???：Now, some cold liquid runs through your throat. It trickles slowly, most likely because it's thick. But the taste is much smoother than you think.

???：Then, a faint scent of absinthe greeted your nostril, and maltose, and lemon thyme, and coconut sugar. It's as delicious as a present from the arcanist from New Zealand.

???：And at the same time, your heart swells, and you feel a scorching energy fill your windpipe.

???：Your vision begins to blur. You kinda see the rough letter on the bottom: The Walden Potion Bar.

???：It's anything but alcohol! "Alcohol" is a taboo. But it will empower you to face the next damn reality.

???：Me? Ha, I am no one.

???：But I can also be anyone—a drunken, slovenly old man, a cleaner in a run-down hotel, a fund manager greedy for more, or a cute spinning crystal skull.

???：Now I'm a drunkard with good taste, just like you.

???：And the day when we formally meet each other is coming soon.

???：Now, lift your big ass and continue your wonderful journey. I will appear at any time, any moment, no matter you expect or not.

???：Right. It only took one moment for you to decide to click, while you had to stand so much nonsense for no reason.

???：My apologies if the story didn't unfold as you expected. But luckily, you still have your sanity that you are always proud of.

???：Come here. Let's put down the bottle. Before Sonetto and that floating APPLe notice your unusual behavior, you still have a chance to restore your image.

???：After me, say "Hi" to them. Pretend you've never been tempted by the bottle.

???：Oh. Hi! How are you doing?

???：Hahahahaha. Excellent. Now we all know how effective the training of the Foundation is. Good news: everyone likes good kids.

???：It's so nice talking to you. You're better than your ... Oops, I should stop here.

???：Me? Ha, I am no one.

???：But I can also be anyone—a drunken, slovenly old man, a cleaner in a run-down hotel, a fund manager greedy for more, or a cute spinning crystal skull.

???：Now I am a nice and friendly adventurer, just like you.

???：Don't worry. The day when we formally meet each other is coming soon.

???：Now, lift your big ass and continue your wonderful journey. I will appear at any time, any moment, no matter you expect or not.

???：...

???：I really wanna find out what you're composed of! I'll chew and swallow all the gene analysis down to my stomach, so I can figure out ... how did you keep your sanity!

???：No arcanist is ever as rational and restrained as you. They should, they should ... If there is really one in the world, that should be ...

???：And I know so much about you. Too much about you! Even I cannot deny what you are. I can't!

???：Me? Ha, I am no one.

???：But I can also be anyone—a drunken, slovenly old man, a cleaner in a run-down hotel, a fund manager greedy for more, or a cute spinning crystal skull.

???：But now I'm only a rubber asshole whose mischief just failed. A desperate loser. My wrath can't even burn a weed in the Pacific Ocean.

???：I'm looking forward to the day we formally meet each other. Only then …

???：Now, lift your big ass and continue your wonderful journey. I will appear at any time, any moment, no matter you expect or not!

**On Bankrupt Companies and Newly Registered Companies: February**

Public Notice  
(1929) IL 17-14000 BD59-3

Great Lakes Media Company, located in Roosevelt Road, the parent company of the Moon Post of Arcanum, does not possess any distributable assets. The debtors Theodore Jackson (Human), Emilio Robert Henger (Human), and manager Steven Sheridan (Human) have applied for the termination of the bankruptcy proceedings following negotiations.  
The Moon Post of Arcanum specializes in reporting news of the arcanist world with a circulation of over 500,000 copies. Renowned articles include Conspiracy: Human Organ Trade and the Witch's Curse. Its parent company Great Lakes Media Company was forced to stop its publication in September 1928 due to anonymous reports to relevant authorities and the emergence of radio stations.  
Pursuant to the provisions of The Nelson Act, this Court has reached the verdict of terminating the bankruptcy proceedings of the Great Lakes Media Company in February 14, 1929.

### 1010901 · 小径① 到另一座城堡

> Trail ① To Another Castle

???：Mrs. Greco puts down the phone. She is now as calm as any Italian mother you've ever met. You know, they're usually making a yummy pie before they pull out their guns.

???：There are enough people in black outside, enough to prevent any eavesdropping or violent conflict.

???：Mrs. Greco glanced blandly over the one, two, three, four ... nine, ten, eleven children in front of her. This is a big family, and it has grown bigger and bigger over the past few years because of Schneider.

???：They're Schneider's sisters. It's hard for the Grecos to tell them apart. But Schneider is different.

???：But Schneider is different. She was born in an earthquake, all covered in blood, and didn't let out her first cry until two hours later. It wasn't until she was one year old that her father noticed that she had never been baptized.

???：She's Schneider's biological mother. You see her furrowed face, like a hard stone wall, while inside the wall is all about a pious farmer's life.

???：The Grecos are not good at dealing with gang affairs. They can't even figure out when their daughter has grown—grown into the backbone of the whole family.

???：When Schneider took out 500 dollars from her pocket and put them on the table, they were completely shocked, and that's just the beginning.

???：Schneider ... Schneider, the youngest daughter they seldom cared about. It's impossible to keep every child well-fed. Schneider could not even get a piece of bread in the Eucharist.

???：But a good daughter would not let anyone worry about her. She sat on the bench outside the church and hummed. She found a way out for herself. She walked to the underground market, fascinated by what she saw …

???：She announced her "new identity" one day.

???：The identity you know now.

???：In the process, she lost something old but gained something new, no matter if she wished for it or not.

???：The bread provided in some sacred religious ceremonies. According to different teachings, it's sometimes flatbread, sometimes fermented bread, or sometimes any kind of bread.

???："Now," Mrs. Greco said, "we live, or we die. If Schneider comes back safely tonight ... we will leave when the moon rises."

???："The doors are closing in front of us, one after another. But the benevolent Maria will give us the ultimate shelter. My children, remember today forever."

???：Her words speak for her status. But Mrs. Greco's eyes never moved away from that small Madonna on the table. The hem of her clothes were soaked in tears.

???：4 p.m. A family is determined to start a new journey.

一名重塑之手的成员徘徊在瓦尔登湖门口。他有一封信，你可以通过一些手段获得它。

**A Letter with Black Mucus**

"Chaos begins where the butterfly flutters its wings."

—— Institutum Lorentz ——  
  
Founded after the "Storm," Institutum Lorentz is a mysterious organization hidden within but independent of the Laplace Scientific Computing Center. Its primary area of research is the chaos energy of Arcanum. Its members distrust the tech of human civilization and would rather discard them. They're active in various sites of arcane phenomena, secretly searching for the "Original Butterfly."  
While all of its members' whereabouts remain unknown, Lorentz still has branches all over the world. Our vanguard squad has fought their investigators several times, yet we still haven't found a way into any of their branches.

### 1011301 · 小径① 诗歌

> Trail ① Poem

一首无名诗。纸张褶皱且带有汗渍。

**Unnamed Poem**

Surrounded by imposing barriers,  
Epics never sing of those about to die.  
Look up, keep looking up,  
A broken tile is the only shelter in the tempest.

People always knew how they took the wrong path,  
So they regret the unregrettable nights, laugh at the laughable fools.  
At the bottom of the cliff, a river always flows.

## 章节 102

### 1020101 · 小径① 黑色星期四

> Trail ① Black Thursday

送奶工曾路过此地，留下些许模糊片段。

**Black Thursday**

"Are we now witnesses of history?"  
The milkman who knew nothing about finance was laughing at the news.  
"Hah, finally a hard time for those Wallstreet gentlemen in suits!"  
"Gotta go, John."  
"Fine, fine." John responded with impatience and tried hard to move those heavy legs. Unlike the rich, he had to deliver the milk for them before sun rose and being late was not allowed. He dropped the newspaper and put out the cigarette. Suddenly he frowned and stopped to ask "Did you hear that? Someone's playing the pipe. But at this time? He must be nuts!"  
"John, I said, stop lying ... Seriously, if you're late again, they really got the reason to fire you this time."  
"Don't worry, man. They've said that for years. People need a guy to deliver the milk anyway. There's always a job for the lazy bones like us. I-I mean ... they gotta drink milk, right?"

### 1020201 · 小径① 重塑之手

> Trail ① Manus Vindictae

有关于你的敌人，有关于重塑之手，大部分人都知道的公开信息。

**Manus Vindictae**

Manus Vindictae is an infamous extremist organization.  
Holding a grudge against humans and contempt for the mixed, these extreme racists only recognize the bodies ruled by arcanist's blood as "mankind."  
They call themselves Manus Vindictae and believe they have the ability and power to change everything. They are dedicated to building a so-called proper world where arcanists will stand neck and neck with gods and enjoy the status they believe they deserve. In that case, humans, arcanists on the human side, and the mixed who tolerate the pollution of arcanist's blood will pay a huge price for their existence.  
There is no doubt they are lunatic.  
One of the two reasons for their madness is their brains: they have a strange prefrontal cortex or some unknown neurons; the other reason is hatred: in those dark ages, arcanists were discriminated, expelled, marginalized, and even slaughtered.  
The tragedies that happen every day on the fringes of the society breed their revenge and keep strengthening this army of darkness.  
Now they have come onto the stage. What they want is power, freedom, the one and only supreme status, and an overthrown world.

### 1020301 · The Door with Wet Paint

**An Unpopular Children's Song**

Who killed the Snowy Dove?  
I, said the cricket.  
With my heart and musket I killed the Snowy Dove.

Who saw her die?  
I, said the owner of the suitcase,  
With my vision and sight,  
I saw her die.

Who dug her grave?  
I, said the cricket.  
With my pick and spade,  
I dug her grave.

Who'll make her shroud?  
I, said the owner of the suitcase,  
With my little suitcase,  
I'll make her shroud.

### 1020603 · A Door on the Trail to the Garden

**Outside the Garden Walls**

Running from one crowd to another  
Hopping from one ship to another  
The green bud finally breaks the dark winter  
Let the snow melt and the fires die  
Push open the narrow door to snow covered garden  
Let bygones be bygones  
Let the future be itself  
The sparrows of spring celebrate after a long wait  
Whispers of the oak forest sound like faint breaths

### 1020701 · 小径① 致小卡文迪许

> Trail ① To Cavendish Jr.

告别

???：What an intriguing plot. Cavendish Sr. must be surprised.

???：Did you see it too? Did you see that woman's face eroded in the dark? The ecstasy on Cavendish Jr.'s face when he turned away from the others?

???：He was once very sensitive and very talented. He was a real f*****g genius when he made potions! But now ... He has been swallowed and digested, and yet he knew nothing about it.

???：Oh, it's a surname buried in dust. It has been shot dead in history. Even Cavendish Jr., who was still alive and once sat in front of you, has taken off his family crown.

???："Forget Me Not," what a hilarious, stupid name. That young master with a pale face died, and in his place, something sinister took shape. He battled hunger and suicide countless times, but in the end, he sank into decadence.

???：That woman gave him a promise. She said a few words to Forget Me Not, and then traveled north, and disappeared in the fog. The Windy City was an unwanted sarcoma which she left behind.

???：Only when the gun was pointed at his throat could he see clearly what had been on his mind. Therefore, he allows himself to revenge, revenge, re-re-re-revenge, and to die.

???：Now, the sarcoma belongs to him. He swears to build a magnificent stage there. He is patiently waiting ... to put his meanness, craziness, and quivers under the sun.

### 1020801 · 小径① 漫漫长夜旅行

> Trail ① Long Night Trip

一场天主教的葬礼。

???：Let's rewind the time to a few years ago.

???：It's for your own good. Lying in front of you is a girl full of mysteries. She makes your mind spin like a top. You scratch your ears, trying to understand what happened exactly.

???：And all these damn things can be traced back to a long, long time ago, when the monkeys decided to stand on their feet …

???：Awesome! Your lofty ambition that a boxer would have is exactly what we need. But unfortunately, I can't take you to the future. Oops, I could, but now I can't.

???：Now, another person is going to do this. But before we solve this big mystery, I think it's necessary for us to sort out the principal contradiction—

???：Trust me, you won't regret listening to this story.

???：I will never be offended by your humor. Okay. The past is waiting for us to look back.

???：But I will never be tired of you, baby.

???：I respect your decision. When you're ready for a story, go find your friends. They will tell you exactly what happened.

???：... While I will wait for you at the next destination, humbly and patiently.

???：It's winter in the early 20th century. The gloomy rain never stops. The square on West Jackson Avenue is alive with people. There is an Italian Renaissance-styled basilica. You rarely see so many people get together without making a sound.

???：This is a black requiem mass. The priest is chanting the requiem, and the people mark a cross on their chests.

???："He was a father. He dedicated to a sinner of ... Lord shall give him eternal peace." A woman in a black robe turns to you and whispers.

???："What is power?" A solemn lady shakes her head at you, "Only Lord can forgive us for our sins and judge the right and the wrong, but he does not want power."

???："Neither does the father." "He was our father. He dedicated to a sinner of ... Lord shall give him eternal peace."

???：She turns back. Explaining this makes her unpleased. More and more people are queuing for the funeral. The square is alive with chants here and there.

???：The Grecos are among them. They're covered by the dark cloud of long-handled umbrellas. Soon, their voices are replaced by whimpers. You can hardly tell whether they are pleased or sorrowful. But you can't find Schneider.

???：Look in the direction of Mr. Greco's broken left palm. Yes. Look from the bandage-wrapped end at his cuff.

???：3390 feet away from the crowd, in the shadow of the church, stands the girl you want to see.

???：She is pale and thin, as if she has just recovered from a serious, long illness.

???：But her fist clenches, her eyes burn, and her figure is an open defiance of the ubiquitous chants. You don't know why she looks so—so furious. Is she ... what, 11 years old?

???：And the mass is about to usher in the ultimate climax. It rains heavier. The priest opens his arms to embrace the sky, "The Lord be with you."

???："And also with you." Schneider responds in a voice that could hardly be heard. She puts her hand on her heart. This is the first time she responds to the Lord. And it will be the last.

### 1021104 · 小径④ 斗犬之隔

> Trail ④ Fight of the Bulldogs

???：You look nervous, and you all know something's gonna happen.

???：With your—that ... the brains full of weird ideas—somehow you can feel it. It's your special talent. The talent comes together with your sensitive nerves and rich emotions.

???：Things are getting messy. New friends and old friends showed up one after another. And, here you are.

???：Fog rises. It separates the black and the white, just like separating two groups of bulldogs. They're turning around and around, revealing their fangs as if they're trying to tear their own tails apart.

???：Good question, my dear. Let's take a look here, right under your feet—

???：You walked in the area controlled by Manus Vindictae. You stole Schneider under their noses—that little girl who bathed in red feathers and blood. And you whispered to that pretty Druid.

???：And your rivals are no better people than you are. Look at those wooden supply boxes. People are starving, but the only thing inside is cotton, and cotton, and cotton.

???：Butter, flour, sugar, and everything sweet were intercepted and sent to Chicago, thanks to those black dogs. But that's alright, cotton will feed you too.

???：Come on. Follow me and look. Look at the place where the air becomes humid because tears are shed. Here's a sobbing mother, with an infant in her arms. Her child is dying, while those nobles are worrying about their silk clothing, checking if their jewelry gets stained with mud.

???：Yes, of course you'd wonder. You haven't been out there for so long that you know nothing about them or their stories. I'd be as curious if I were you.

???：The smarties in the Foundation also noticed what Manus Vindictae has done. They stopped the transportation team while approaching the fog.

???：The girl in the black suit has returned to where you asked her to be. They take good care of her.

???：And your loyal, beautiful friend is fighting for you, together with that easily startled rocking girl, the quick-thinking boy, and a red fruit.

???：She's on her way to save you. She overcomes one obstacle after another, like a prince crossing the thorny land of a sleeping castle, like a legend puppy gripping its owner's clothes and pulling him from a flaming fire.

???：You picked an interesting question, as interesting as yourself, little freak.

???：You do have a distinguished brain, with so many distinguished things inside. It's not bad. Keep that. Use it more often.

???：Let's go back to your question. The fog—no doubt it's created by the barkeeper. It's like bartending, put this in, put that in, stir them in a crucible, and you get what you want.

???：It works like real fog; it blocks eyesight, hides secrets, and slows down those who wanna get in or get out. It also works differently from the real fog, for it is hallucinating, intoxicating, and getting people forever lost at the mysterious fork roads.

???：He is shrewd and insidious. But smart guys are everywhere, especially now and here.

???：What we have to do is wait. Wait. Wait for it.  
But not for too long, that'd be stupid. You know, a proper wait—wait for it!

???：The show is about to begin. You'll see the most magnificent performance of all time! Oh, of course, you can join it, stand in the center, bring your pretty suitcase, hold your umbrella, and give us a badass fight.

???：Wait and see. The fog between black dogs and white dogs won't last for long. You can't turn away from those biting, tearing, and bleeding.

???：Grab your popcorns, my love.  
Then sit tight, hold still. You may cry, or may not. I don't give a f**k.

### 1021201 · 小径① 八卦杂志

> Trail ① Gossip Magazine

一份随处可见的八卦杂志。记载着这座城市里人们关心的一切。其中包括瓦尔登湖酒吧与勿忘我先生。

**■■■ Formula: 1920s**

I am so sick of him! An "arcanist" acting like the upper class! He even attended the parties in the lords' mansions. He is nothing like those who hide their identities: they know how to suck up to humans and cover up the ugly side. Look at him! A drink that turns into alcohol only when you drink it? He should be hung, and shot, and burned! Get out of our street!  
—Big Mouth Bulletin: From Readers, 1927, Issue 1224-1

He must be a weird monster! I heard a woman married a snake and bore a horrible child. That must be him! That's why he hides everything about himself. The other day I saw with my own eyes he had scales under those long sleeves, one next to another embedded in his flesh. On the fourteenth night after that, I woke up with a start from the nightmare to find a woman with a strange head staring at me from the darkness outside the window. She didn't breathe or say a word, and just put her face against the window like some dead animal ... That must be his underling ...  
—Big Mouth Bulletin: From Readers, 1927, Issue 1224-1

He gave me a hand when I needed help.  
—Big Mouth Bulletin: From Readers, 1927, Issue 1224-1

His alcohol has bewitched too many people. That green stuff can open anyone's mouth: secrets of the Anti-Saloon League, plans of the Synagogue, and even rumors of the Congress, he knew them all. There's someone different behind him. I don't know who that is, but they must be unbelievably powerful. No one's going to believe my words. I don't know why he is still alive now. That's horrible. I mean it.  
—Big Mouth Bulletin: From Readers, 1927, Issue 1224-1

Forget Me Not is the most celebrated mixologist of the early 20th century and a perfect embodiment of the American Dream. His real name is unknown to the public, and rumors about him abound. Some claim he's a wicked drug dealer, Satan reincarnate, a political spy, or even the love child of an evil serpent and a human. Although his background remains shrouded in mystery, it doesn't stop us from tasting heaven in his wonderful creations.  
—Big Mouth Bulletin: From Readers, 1927, Issue 1224, Epilogue

### 1021301 · 小径① 隐秘的经济运作

> Trail ① The Secret Business

一些老故事，更多旧传闻。随处打听，都能得知。

**Excerpt from the New York Times**

Around midnight two days ago, a large fire broke out in the oak forest on the western outskirts. The delay of the warning and the complex terrains full of inflammables in the forest resulted in a fire that lasted a whole night till dawn and 13 deaths.  
The victims entered the forest at dusk. Identities of 6 of them have been confirmed: the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers Tomson Joyce, Shelley Gerald, Kelly Keith, and the project manager Russell James. The others' are still being verified.

The Weyerhaeuser used to be a leader in the wood industry, yet it went bankrupt not long ago due to the wood supply crisis these years. After donating the formula of the Resurrection Potion (basic version) to the Maria Sanatorium in New York, the Sacred Heart Sanatorium in Ohio, and the Presbyterian Church Health Center on certain conditions, the Weyerhaeusers sold all of their forestry lands.  
On the night of the fire, the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers, and the workers went into the forest to start the disafforestation project before they died. The police has found traces of arcanist's activities and a small amount of gasoline at the scene, which shows the case is an arson attack. More investigations are needed to identify the suspect.

Reporter: James Welles/Dorey Johnson

### 1030101 · 小径① 开放式三明治

> Trail ① Open Sandwich

一张摸起来有些滑腻的纸，似乎曾包裹过某种油脂丰富的食物。

???：No, you are not awake yet. You are still drowned in this mire of meditation—sticky, swampy, and dark.

???：Welcome to nihility, Ms. Too Many Questions. You might be in an ocean, a well, the base of a ring finger ... whatever.

???：At that "warm invitation," you are rewarded with some mold-like bruises on your legs. "The paramecia" think this gift will force you to give in.

???：But for you, the invisible wounds are much more severe.

???：Don't worry. They are not gentle at all, but neither are you benign.

???：Memory? Good try! The logic you pride yourself on continues to serve you properly. It allows you to make some good deductions here, like a biologist analyzing the cause of a humpback appearing in a swamp.

???：But the answer will disappoint you, unfortunately. This is just a dream, an illusion they shoved into your head. These people are very familiar with using coercive power.

???：A group of people in gowns. Their gowns are made of white polyester and are over knee-length. There are seven, namely No. 1 to No. 7.

???：Probably only those odd-number guys are here. Or maybe No. 4 or 6 is also with them ... Damn it, I don't care.

???：What matters is that your classmate is among them. No, not the gentle and loyal one who always stands by your side.

???：It is the other one—the one with indifferent outlines that makes her look like a refined machine.

???：Let's skip her first name and only pay attention to her glorious family name, like what most people did.

???：Like sherry cask whiskies to the alcoholic, Upmann Cedar Aged Robusto Cameroon to smokers ... and the Mesmers to the arcanists.

???：Ha Ha Ha, you smart-ass. Love your humor. Yeah, you are right, boujee!

???：Long ago, long long ago, you might have had the chance to experience such a period.

???：At that time, humans just made their decision to live without prayers, and that's also the beginning of the long-term cooperation between Laplace and those ... heh, oh, "scholars," as they called themselves.

???：Anything mystical. When they sit there and point at you with an iron stick, you'll pass out. When they put an iron ring around your neck, you'll feel burnt ...

???：You must be familiar with all these freaky tricks, because you are surrounded by freaky tricksters, like that little girl who protects others with her glass dipping pen or that floating apple dancing in the air.

???：The Mesmers know how to suffocate the flames of consciousness; they help you free fall internally to the bottom of the abyss ... as you are now.

???：It's really hard finding an arcanist who can freely master such skill. As you know, scarcity causes tragedy.

???：That was the start of her nightmare: when the Child Labor Law was turned into a piece of wrinkled paper to wrap the sandwich.

???：The young daughter of the Mesmer family was led to a room when she was 12. Before she stepped in, she had sensed the messy magnetic field on the other side of the door, thanks to her acute perception.

???：Compared to humans, arcanists are much more emotionally vulnerable. It won't take much to overwhelm them and force them to regress to animals.

???：The Mesmers are merciful and professional. They will never turn away any patient who comes to them for treatment.

???：... By then, you should realize the wrapping paper was never protecting the sandwich, but the hand.

???：The mustard from an unknown bottle, the squeezed meatloaf, and rusty lettuce leaf, all were crushed and fell out from between the bread.

???：Just like the chaotic noises and the raspy screaming that fill her ears ... This filth contaminated the little girl's hands and corrupted the white polyester.

???：Hmm, it is indeed a good time for silence.

???：Now you've noticed those exposed wounds on the machine, those marks from repeated washing and adjustments.

???：Well, the traumata from childhood is usually hard to forget.

???：Those memories are engraved in our heart and run through our veins ... Eventually, they contaminate every single neuron with mucous snot.

???：Since then, every inch of growth is an extension of pain. New bones will be eroded and the condition can't be cured by cellular renewal.

???：Both you and her are familiar with this.

???：Okay, take your suitcase. Now it's time ... Get lost!

### 1030502 · The Nursery Rhyme that Doesn't Rhyme

**The Nursery Rhyme that Doesn't Rhyme**

George the Oak, George the Oak.  
An acorn between the branches falls from above.  
The birdlings fly into the marble cage.  
Little acorn, please help them.

George the Oak, George the Oak.  
The acorn in the bag is picked up by the child.  
The huge white umbrella gives it a shelter.  
Tall canopy, please help them.

George the Oak, George the Oak.  
The acorn buried in the soil falls asleep.  
The glimmering fireflies slowly approach.  
Deep tree hole, please help them.

George the Oak, George the Oak.  
The acorn has rooted and is waiting to sprout.  
The casket has become a lonely boat.  
Free breeze, please help them.

铅灰、羊毛与谷物，总有一个会对它们起点作用。

**There Is Not Only Darkness**

Hunger, silence, the dim light, and a little trouble.  
Aside from these elements, a standard guardhouse has to have two other things:  
Critters which are not too difficult for kids to handle and tools for the kids to solve the problems.  
The interior of the guardhouse will be examined and evaluated regularly, and every week people will come to maintain the facilities and clean it up.  
However, some words in childish handwriting were left in the room. They were written on crumpled little notes or on the corner of the wall, look like hints, or were left there for reasons which are unidentifiable now. **Arcanum Military Equipment List: Individual Vehicles**

An air combat broom of the Su-01ве (Witch) series for individual combat. With high mobility, high attack and wide range, this arcane vehicle can bring an aerial advantage to its pilot.  
The series applies titanium alloy and a lancet-like design. As a result, its streamlines allow it to fly faster than any other models (maximum level speed: 1553 mph). Beneath the main body is a wedge-shaped air inlet which guarantees the aerodynamic performance and there is an arcanum detector in the middle of the body. The broom is also equipped with an ST-30-1 30mm Instant Arcane Skill Casting Device at the bottom which works with a high-precision laser ranging aiming system.

The Su-01ве (Witch) series was cooperatively developed by the Sukhoi Design Bureau and ■■■■. Experience of piloting arcanum vehicle is required for its operation. Besides, the pilot must possess outstanding G-tolerance as the overload brought by the excessive mobility is inevitable.  
For now all the models of the Su-01ве (Witch) series belong to the Zeno Arms Academy and work as its main tactical vehicles. Only pilots who have been trained rigorously and systematically are qualified for piloting.

教师桌的抽屉里留有一册笔记，灰白的朋友们也对它充满兴趣。

**Appendix to Foundation Potion Management Ⅱ**

- Bitter Mute -

Warning: Appendix - Ⅱ Bitter Mute is an arcanum drug of Schedule Ⅱ. Arcanum Drug Possession License of the St. Pavlov Foundation is required for its possession. Organizations without the license must not produce or store the drug.  
The drug is a concentrated solution for external use. When used, the solution should be diluted with pure water until the concentration reduces to the recommended range (45% - 65%). The concentration is adjustable depending on the condition (maximum safe concentration: 77%).

[Active ingredient] Screaming mandrake root, flying squirrel bile, toad liver (collected on full moon), two-headed snake tail scale powder, three-year-old belladonna (whole plant), leech.  
[Caution] Use a copper cauldron for the production.  
[Appearance & Property] A dark blue or dark brown concentrated solution with a pungent smell and an extremely bitter taste.  
[Use] The drug can cause mental stability, and the bitter taste can cause temporary vision disorder and speech disorder. High concentrations can cause mild paralysis and even hallucination. Nowadays the drug's use in criminal penalty has been abolished and it is mainly equipped by the patrols and branches of the St. Pavlov Foundation. During daily patrols or under extreme circumstances mentioned by the Emergency Defense Code of St. Pavlov Foundation, the drug can be used as an effective suppression method against individuals or groups.

These clear, glistening, rainbow-colored accessories are the favorable tokens of friendship among the teenagers which allow them to hear the secret whispers.  
They all came from a student of decent arcane family. Unlike other students who were taken here, she asked to study in this school. Given her keenness of study and prestige background, her belongings weren't put through any close assessment when she enrolled.  
She hid them in her pocket and waited patiently.  
She was so preoccupied, paying so much attention on what would happen next, that she didn't notice one of the crystals was left lying on the floor.

### 1031101 · The Puppy You know

**The Puppy You know**

The warm fur, the little feet,  
The orange color under the light spots, and the pure white yarn.  
The unbreakable neck ring that symbolizes loyalty,  
And a sincere heart that deserves no suspicion.

Softly, mildly, without resistance,  
It licks the white marble.  
The frogs, the pebbles, and the stinky socks in the attic forgotten,  
It climbs up the cliff like a pious believer,  
Poking into the untrackable breeze  
At the closest place.

Between the gaps of the stone gate  
Are the moist nose and eyes  
That you couldn't bear to face.

### 1031401 · 小径① 一张合影

> Trail ① A Group Photo

一张不知从何处而来的老旧合影。

???：A discolored group photo with burned and brittle edges. It has been carefully preserved, but that was not enough to keep it from getting blurry as time passed.

???：The faces of the people in the photo are hardly recognizable, but it's clear that they all have dark hair. At the top, there is a sentence ... perhaps written in an Eastern language.

???：You have no idea who they are or what they did. You have never met them ... You concluded firmly.

???：Except for the third one from the right in the second row, you know her.

???：She looks a bit younger in the photo than you remember. The two pigtail braids fall to the sides of her cheeks. The eyes behind that pair of black-frame glasses are as deep and composed as a deer's.

???："Commemoration for International Students at Imperial College London, 19**–19**" ... You can tell it was once bronzed.

???：All the critical information is here ... I mean, this is good news! Given what has been happening, who cares about what year the photo was taken?

???：The East, a vast land to the west coast of the Pacific. The ferry she took once crossed the Strait of Gibraltar and the Suez Canal.

???：Do you have to be this nosy? It doesn't matter where it came from. What matters is where it has gone. The photo was really a warning for us all—don't end up being separated from the homeland.

???：Hey, be careful the way you speak, little prick! This is not a bedtime story, and I am not your sweet grandfather.

???：You know her, but you're not a close acquaintance of hers, neither am I ... There is someone else who knows her well.

???：All I know is that she has cut her hair short and became a drop of dew on the lily petal, shaking underneath the calyx.

???：It's pure white, with smooth petals and healthy, light-colored anthers. It feels ... cold and hard, like a piece of sharp marble, ready to pierce your hand.

???：The dew was brought here and cultivated by the lily. Undoubtedly, the lily is an excellent educator, while the dew is also a one-in-a-thousand candidate.

???：Here? Technically speaking, her initial destination was not "here," not this gray-and-white building, nor under the umbrella, nor the calyx. She is an expert at theoretical physics and studies string theory.

???：She voyaged across the ocean in order to return to the land with vitality.

???：Her plan, however, was interrupted by a sudden change. That lily always has her ways to navigate things to develop as she wanted.

???：Good question. She has no thirst, nor desire. Homeland is awaiting ... How could a scientist become a politician?

???：I hope I can answer you like this without hesitation … But you've also noticed it, right? You knew.

???：Now, the cat has been locked back in the chamber. The entrance has been tightly sealed. None of us can do anything about its fate. We can only wait outside, anxiously scratching our heads and placing our bets.

???：Tomorrow, or the day after tomorrow ... Hah! We'll lose it all!

### 1040101 · 小径① 湿漉漉的信

> Trail ① Wet Letter

一封贴有漂亮邮票的信件，被水珠沾湿。

**Wet Letter**

Dear Z, the last person from who I'd expect a letter,

I am glad to hear from you.  
About what you wrote to ask, I didn't find anything. No one in the France branch knows about the "Storm," including the leader and some of the senior officers. I think you have to give up on this one.  
But we need to know more about the "Storm," so do those big shots. That's why the Foundation is desperate for a source of information and values the "Timekeeper" so much. That being the case, we are back to the beginning ... How ridiculous. I don't think there is anything else I can do for you or her.  
The good news is, my business trip to the France branch has been smooth, and I'm estimated to be back in the headquarters tomorrow afternoon (I'm writing this before my way back so I can use the beautiful special stamp I got here).  
Maybe we can have another game by then. The people here are not good at ping-pong, so things became boring after I had beaten everyone I saw.

By the way, congratulations on your promotion.  
I'm glad to know you have already adapted to the style of communication and the work in the Foundation. Clever as you are, we all know you were still thinking and acting the "scientist" way. Anyway, the time no longer goes forward, but it didn't stop. It has brought us to this mess and we have to do something.

One more thing—  
keep the black umbrella you mentioned. You don't always get to find a rain gear used by someone that witnesses and braves the "Storm."

Yours,  
Katz  
From the France branch

### 1040301 · The Sun Will Continue to Rise

???：You fall back into the crowd. I understand. I can feel the desire and depression burning deep inside your brain: you are longing to be a part of an absurd, broken, bizarre arcanum story which is beyond your imagination and past experience.

???：But sorry, I can't help laughing: you got into some trouble and have to run around blindly among those in white robes like a panicked ant.

???：Come on. Close your eyes. Imagine inside a TV with static noise, a red-nosed bad guy opens his arms to you and says, "Welcome, to, the, human, world."

???：I know, dude! As puzzling as our usual big shots. They stand on the podium, with a mic in their hands, and everyone can see their uvula trembling from their wide-open mouths as they speak.

???：Hello, hello, testing, testing. Three, two, one, three, two, one. Comma, comma, comma. Everyone has to wait for what will happen with a full bladder and an empty stomach.

???：Nice pun! But you don't understand what I’m saying, for now. When the day comes, you will find yourself, you will find me, you will find them ... Oh, thinking of that day, my damned heart is beating crazily like a dying reindeer's!

???：But look at this moment, it's just fine. You know where you should be, and you don't have anything that you shouldn't have in your mind. You just crouch here with patience ...

???：Listen, this is not just a world of arcanists. Arcanists, arcanum creatures, and even arcanum itself are but insignificant existence hidden beneath all else.

???：You must learn about those who are in power at the present moment. They live on the other side of the earth, setting rules on the thrones in the scarcely populated wilderness.

???：Much has been done for the order, for the vision, for the ultimate goal which will satisfy the majority yet sacrifice the minority. They wage wars and strike deals, building a pyramid that symbolizes supreme power with skeletons of the commoners.

???：No, I'm not telling a lame villain story. On the contrary, it's a boring documentary about those in power standing between the sky and the earth, seeking the real meaning of fairness and ideals.

???：There's still plenty of time! Go ahead. Keep moving, but don't jump to any conclusion. Just make sure you're always on the way, so you will learn more about humans, more and more.

???：Take the microscope with you and dig into the rotten history of those poor little things!

### 1040401 · 小径① 静置一旁的笔记本

> Trail ① A Notebook Left Aside

一段工整的，详尽的，满怀憧憬的笔记。

**206th LSCC Seminar - Note**

At the meeting this morning, MP Callaghan and his great-grandson Dr. Callaghan Jr. submitted the research report On Zebra Crossing, Lemon, Snow Leopard Pattern, and Transportation and Teleportation Arcanum Rituals in joint names.  
In the report, they mentioned an innovational point of view on the relations between zebra crossing and the crisis in human subconsciousness, and that between horse herds and the feeling of levitation. Supported by a considerable amount of experiment data, they were granted series D funding of level 1-11 by the LSCC Funding Channel.  
In the same meeting, Tchangues 1-07 Stable Release was displayed,  
which were given rounds of applause in the room for 17.8 seconds. There is no doubt that the display has won was a great success.  
It was a pair of artificial lower limbs. Its skin and blood vessels crafted through cell reproduction and monkey brain transplantation, and the bones and muscles made with branches, white crystal powder, woolen threads and golden privets from the southeast of France. It is capable of all kinds of movements including walking, running, jumping, and gymnastics moves. It could even spider-walk on the wall for 120.7 seconds.

Just as its inventor Ms. Perotti said, "Centered on the rationality and efficiency, we learn from human civilization when we harness the unstable, everchanging arcanum technology, so we can provide real, stable help for more people." There is no doubt Tchangues is a good example of LSCC's core philosophy. Yes, this is the Laplace Scientific Computing Center.

I think I am going to enjoy working here.

11:21, 09/19, Mesmer Jr.

### 1040802 · A Ride on the Toboggan

???：Sounds of different colors go into your left ear and slip out from your nostrils, just like a toboggan sliding down a soft piste from a cabin up the mountain to the finishing line at the foot, leaving nothing but excitement that doesn't even last.

???：Errr ...

???：What did you say? I, I got bad signal here, oh, ba-bad signal indeed.

???：I mean, I may be different from all of you, but I have a conscience, OK? I never talk s**t, that's the bottom line. What I said were all felicitous hints.

???：Yes. You're always so clever, with an excellent sense of humor! Now take all the cold-proof equipment you've got. The view is good here, but it's still dangerous as you are climbing a snow mountain after all.

???：I'm sure you have noticed it. Between the glacier and the valley, between the snow mountain and the lake, stands a great building we're all familiar with.

???：Close your eyes and feel the patterns of interlaced gray and white. The flow of the building, so balanced and symmetrical, is like a sophisticated symphony with beautiful melody.

???：That may be your first impression of the Foundation. After that committee meeting, however, all I could see on your face was a terrible mess, like it was licked by a terrier which has just finished hunting.

???：What the f**k are they talking about? Who can explain their relations for me?

???：Good question! In this lonely, whirling, dark universe, I'm the only one who is willing to tell you all these stories on the cold bench in the park.

???：Countless! Oh man, you gotta understand, an international political institution with great ambition is bound to make troubles and make up for them.

???：The House of Integratus and the committee are in charge of lawmaking, not just in the Foundation, but in countries and even in the global society! Any affairs related to the human-arcanist relationship will be brought to their table, and they will handle each of the affairs with their properly manicured but muscle-torn hands.

???：Through the folds in their brains, people set this reasonable rule: delegates of the House of the Integratus are elected to take responsibility for their constituencies. As busy as bees, they submit countless proposals to the committee every year.

???：Then the committee will choose one lucky proposal and send it to the next step: all the committee members would gather to debate and vote for the proposal, like what happened in a Medieval Fair.

???：Many would show up in the fair. There would be political parties, a moderator, dozens of expert groups, each with a different standpoint and goal. It's like a mental boxing, modern version! We will have to wait while listening to the war of words. After dozens of days, or even months, we will finally get to see who wins the championship belt.

???：You haven't met him yet. Actually, only a few have, as the opportunities are always fleeting like the falling stars.

???：But it's okay. The Foundation still works well without him! Our responsible Constantine, working without any complaint like an unbreakable lily, is trying hard to change the Foundation's direction to what she believes to be correct.

???：Come on, let's look at the bigger picture. Humans have worked out with the brilliant folds in their brains that they need multiple parties to create a balance of power. So, bang! There come the committee, the House of the Integratus, and many other administration departments.

???：The House of Integratus and the committee are in charge of lawmaking, not just in the Foundation, but in countries and even in the global society! Any affairs related to the human-arcanist relationship will be brought to their table, and they will handle each of the affairs with their properly manicured but muscle-torn hands.

???：The committee has the right to approve or reject the proposals from delegates. Well, of course, some of the delegates are very stubborn. They will keep submitting their proposals no matter how many times they have been rejected, just like a red-back shrike with fledglings repeatedly submitting rejected motions until they are exhausted.

???：When it comes to the formal debate, you will get to watch a dogfight full of polite yet viciously aggressive language. Everyone, including the president, the delegates, the parties and the expert groups, will turn into maniacs in the arena of power.

???：After this stage, the final result depends on the white marble house. They seldom express their opinions, but they will sometimes.

???：Oh, yes, yes. Look how polite you are when you ask that question, like I'm some high school student working part-time in a mall selling local socks to you.

???：And I'd be very nice and tell you, "Of course!" We must not ignore the other administration departments of the Foundation, as well as the headquarters' poor branches located in different countries and the humanitarian School of Primary Defense of Mankind that works like a poorhouse.

???：Remember, the Foundation is managing the whole world! Of course, they need some rules that are hard to understand, so the wise will be wiser and the silly will be sillier, while those in the middle will show an innocent smile when they die!

???：Humans are experts on these things. My stupid arcanists, don't just complain; learn to see what they are after, okay? Catch the unspoken drifts between the lines! So one day you will understand ...

???：Life is like a ride on the toboggan.

### 1041101 · 小径① “无名垂钓者”记录

> Trail ① Record of A Nameless Angler

无处不在的传闻。有人将它们整理于此。

**Sightings of the "Nameless Angler"**

Sunday     07:21  
Angelina of the Field Operation Squad passed by the east side of the Foundation Square and saw someone in white by the fountain.  
This is the earliest sighting of the target.

Same Sunday  15:10  
Mary of the Medical Department took stock of the Picrasma Extract III in the warehouse, behind which she found a rubber fishing bucket with 3 suckermouth catfishes inside which were 3-4 inches long.

Same Sunday  15:22  
Mary left the warehouse and the fishing bucket was gone.  
There were water spots and a little granite rubble left on the ground.

Monday     06:30-08:30  
A cleaner found some abandoned fishhooks near the fountains as well as by multiple water sources (streams, estuaries). Most of the fishhooks were hung below treetops.

Same Monday  22:33  
A patrol squad found a set of fishing gears near reception area 13.  
The gear set includes a carbon fishing rod with woolen core, a foldable rubber fishing bucket, a comfortable folding chair, and three buckled boxes of baits made with Carbuncle's tooth pulp. It is now taken to the Lost and Found.

*  *  *  
All right, it seems to be a "Nameless Angler" in the Foundation.  
We have collected some basic information after the first inquiry, but a lot still remain unknown.

What does he want? Where does he come from?  
Is he a ghost? A spy sent by other political organizations? Or an arcane puppet made by someone that plans to wipe out all the suckermouth catfishes because they have a problem with the appearance of the race?  
Everything, everything remains unknown ...  
Hah! Let's move on to the next inquiry!

### 1041402 · Legwork

???：...

???：That was a good sleep, dude. You drooled everywhere. Almost glued my eyelids.

???：You didn’t expect there to be so many complicated, confusing factions in the Foundation—can’t they just make this institution simpler so that people can tell one group from another easily, like telling the difference between cake layers?

???：Really? Dude, are you 100% sure that you are trouble-free?

???：I was sleeping like a baby, having a sweet sweet dream which has been long absent in my life. There were three ladies, a bevy of goofy swans, and I, living a harmonious life together in my dream.

???：Suddenly, a certain jinx woke me up, saying, “I have no trouble. Don’t come over.” How wonderful! I was like a stray dog sleeping on a square. You’ll see, this old man is going to shovel all his complaints into your pure, innocent ear.

???：How do you feel about the structure of the Foundation? I can see your cockily raised neck, showing your attitude of rejection. Well, perfect! Let’s start with this intriguing topic, then!

???：The Foundation is a mess. Listen up, it drifts along the chaotic swirl between order and disorder, enticing countless idealists and realists to come over.

???：The “Storm” exacerbates the chaos. Those arcanists who were snorkeling are now surfacing—You see, Ms. Z is a great example. She is even sitting at the same conference table with the human elites.

???：The undercurrents, however, are still lurking. Natural Resource Committee, Mankind Caucus, Culture and Education Department ... Their members are hard to differentiate given the intricate backgrounds. They bustled for benefits and mired in countless trivial matters.

???：You see? You need to divide them into groups, and divide and divide ... until you break a huge entity into small groups, as small as possible. That’s when they are able to clean the vomit on the living room floor effectively and emotionlessly!

### 1041601 · 小径① 球类游戏

> Trail ① Ball Game

???：Ping, pong. Ping, pong. Ping pong ping pong ping, pong. Ping. Pong.

???：Yup. Agreed.

???：It's too noisy. You were looking at the little ball made of plastic. It hops from left to right, making an annoying sound.

???：But all we can do to it is look at it, just like all you can do to me is tolerate my existence, for both of us have nowhere to go.

???：Yes, an imitation show. A ball imitation show, little thing.

???：You're imitating the Messiah, while I'm imitating an orange ball made of plastic.

???：Being pushed back and forth by two rackets, I have nowhere else to go. Once they apply a force on me, I can't help bouncing to the sky. You can see how the air flows across my dry, wrinkled skin ...

???：Oh, what a coincidence! Aren't you in the same situation as mine now!

???：Ha! Look at your frowning face—

???：Your face wrinkles from the eyebrows to the nose tip, but people can barely see those light furrows. We know what that means. You have a poker face, and it's never your fault.

???：But for now, your face is not the thing that matters. Well, maybe for someone it is, but still, it's not.

???：You know what really matters?  
The game! Yes, it's the game, of course the game matters!

???：Everyone loves the game. They gather at the stadium, yelling or roaring vehemently. They choose one team to become its fan and spend good money on a team uniform or an autographed ball.

???：Yet, it's just a ball. You see where I'm coming from?

???：Just a ball. Its outline, a circle, could be found in any geometry textbook and anywhere in this world.

???：When the first hominid picked up a coconut and threw it to the sky—and caught it—and threw it—and caught it—and threw it again, when she felt joy and yelped, had she ever thought of the future?

???：The future where a simple ball has become so complicated and enchanting, now we call it the present.

???：Hahahahahaha! Yes, sure! Complicated and enchanting!

???：Use your silly and smart head to think about this ball game carefully.

???：The complex scoring rules, the harsh requirements for reactive agility, the countless possible foul points ... Your fingers, your wrists, and arms, a correct way of using them will lead to victory!

???：What matters more is, like every ball game, it focuses on how you serve the ball and hitting it back.

???：It's not an easy job as it sounds. When you are in the game, you need to concentrate. Where will the ball come from, and how will you return it to your opponent—you need to figure that out within half a second.

???：Use your power in a proper way, move your feet in a stable pace, inhale and exhale at the right timings.

???：Ping. Pong. Ping. Pong. Ping. Pong. Ping. Pong ...

???：The sound will last forever and ever until that bouncing little thing falls to either side of the court.

### 1041701 · 小径① 与火共舞

> Trail ① Dances with Fire

???：Look at where you are. Paulina also once stood here.

???：A human. A member of the St. Pavlov Foundation.

???：She started from here, took 33 steps and a half, turned left, opened the door, and then turned right, went on for another 5 meters. She stopped and opened the historical intelligence cabinet.

???：Human-like. A standard human. Grave-looking, rational-thinking, efficient-acting.

???：She was digging in the files. The only touch of bright color on her was across her neck—a blue polka dot scarf, tied in the French way.

???：The scarf was there to cover up a nevus on her skin. A lonely, dark spot that lacks a symmetric counterpart on the other side of the neck. It's a mark too random and out of place to exist. She did not like it.

???：For work, sweetheart. Most of the actions we took in most of the time were for our jobs, or for the sake of getting one.

???：She’s a good employee, the kind of person who sincerely wishes to contribute to society. Her dedication to work equally applied to events as big as maintaining peace and matters as trivial as looking through old files.

???：It was the 1931 file, or the 1935 one. She hasn’t gotten there yet. But there is another folder which has caught her attention.

???：On December 28, 1932, on the wonderful Day of the Holy Innocents in Mexico, 13 arcanist high school students attacked the crowd during the nighttime celebration.

???：They claimed the oracle had uttered to them, from the inside of a gigantic Cordia elaeagnoides, that they had to shoulder the responsibility to protect the people.

???：So they took their wands and knives, carried bottles of potion of concocted Picric acid and Nitrocellulose and walked into the festival market like any regular happy young people.

???：Their attack led to 17 deaths. Six were injured, among which four were disabled for life.

???：One young girl among the attackers couldn’t physiologically stop her hysterical laughter even when she was under arrest. She quivered convulsively, with her hands twitching tightly on 31-cm-long pruning shears.

???：She kept muttering the word “Bocote,” which is the common way to call the Cordia elaeagnoides among the lumber dealers. In the Aztec Civilization, it means “giant mouth.”

???：“Any mouth can talk”—in this case of mass hysteria, this was the one true rule.

???：Hysteria, madness, paranoia, hearing voices or seeing things ... these abnormalities are the sparks flying in the air, and arcanists are like dry cotton. The encounter of the two ... will cause a fire.

???：Paulina complained quietly. She accused the danger of fire, the risk of heat. One can hardly hold no grudges against the flame, when it is hidden next to you, patiently waiting.

???：She started to mutter something of a more extreme nature. She was carried away—forgot that each individual has their own right even in a collective body. She forgot what public opinion can do to subtly influence a community. Her eyes were fixed on the scarlet flame.

???：Racist thoughts crept silently into her blonde-haired, beautiful head, like a cunning centipede.

???：Fortunately, this is not yet the end of the story. Paulina still had her chances to change.

???：Change. Yes. An alteration. Everybody has to change.

???：You can improve your living standards. Pay for prettier white clothes, drive around in a limo painted in bright pink—anything to prove that you are as much of a man as those tough warriors in ancient Europe!

???：Or you can try the bitter side of life. A life so bad that every weekend, you have nowhere to go but lie on a couch hollowed out by your dog and pick your nose. You pick it hard, just to locate and take out the hardest, largest booger that irritates your nasal membranes to stop the endless sneezing.

???：The change of Paulina started from a question.

???：A senior staff of the Foundation walked to her, flipping through the pages of the same folder, and asked, "But Paulina, how are you going to deal with the fire?"

???：On the next page, the survivors started a new life under the supportive measures, and the crazy children have left their poor family, leaving the violence and harassment behind, and moved into a sanatorium with white walls.

???：The record of the follow-up visits showed that they have stayed mentally stable since. They learned to calm themselves and overcame drug addiction and alcohol abuse. Some of them were even married.

???：Between the last two pages, there lies a thank-you letter. The names signed at the end of the letter went on for two lines, and the last name ends with a cheerful strike going up, like a smiley face.

???：“The fire is always there, Paulina. We need to find a way to live with it, not to extinguish it.”

???：“Keep them somewhere safe, like a high shelf, and away from the fuel. Watch over the temperature. Maybe teach them to burn in a manner that no one, including themselves, would be hurt.”

???：“Division and confrontation have never taken this world to a better place, have they?”

???：They went on talking for a bit more—about the responsibilities of this job, about personal liabilities, about hatred, about the minorities, about the practices that ought to be taken to realize ideas.

???：Young Paulina had changed. Drops of tears seep out from the bottom of that pair of beautiful green eyes.

???：I shed tears with her, of course. Sadly, I was busy sucking the flavoring powder on chips off my fingers and missed the right moment to wipe those tears off my face.

???：Nevertheless, this is my favorite episode ... We should watch it again! Ahem ...

???：... Look at where you are. Paulina also once stood here.

### 1041801 · 小径① 似曾相识

> Trail ①

熟悉的色彩吸引着你的目光。

**Déjà Vu**

An undefiled scale,  
Sitting high up in the hall of judgement,  
with the listening ears, the sniffing nose, the watching eyes,  
the raising brows, and the speaking mouth.  
Being the well-polished, the bright, the spacious,  
it is the dazzling daylight itself.

It is the carrier of a noble virtue,  
as untouchable as the feathers on a bird's throat,  
as unsullied as the unexplored snowy ground,  
as undisturbed as the ripple-less milk in the glass.  
I trust myself, that I have encountered such whiteness before, sometime, somewhere.

### 1041901 · IMPORTANT NOTICE

**Interpretation of the Supreme Court concerning Some Issues on the Use of Filibusters**

Based on the current circumstances, this interpretation is hereby given in accordance with the relevant regulations and laws, in order to ensure the debates in the legislative process are carried out to serve its designated purpose, and the rights of the parties and interest groups involved are respected in the process.

Article 1 During the legislative debates, any member of the Joint Committee of the St. Pavlov Foundation who has doubt on the bill itself and requires explanation has the right to request an open debate from the chairperson. The chairperson in charge of the matter is obliged to accept this request.  
...  
...  
Article 12 Within the 60 days of the legislative process and on the premise that all the parties are in compliance with the universally ratified Code of Conduct in the legislative period, both parties are entitled to an unlimited number of debates before the official voting on the bill and have the right to give up and withdraw from the debates.  
Article 13 Termination of speech by either of the two parties will be considered as their withdrawal from the debate. The debate will hence end and the voting on the bill will be initiated. If the other party requests to continue with the speech, the Supreme Court will approve the proceeding of the debate. The debate will then last until that party requests voting or withdraw from the debate.  
Article 14 Had no conclusion been drawn within 60 days, the Supreme Court shall determine the bill to be repealed. Any repealed bill will not be allowed to re-enter the legislative process within the next 5 years.  
Article 15 In 10 days after the debate starts, both parties involved will be allowed to request voting to end the debate.  
...  
Article 20 The filibuster is a special legislative process established for controversial issues. Any bill being put through the filibuster process will require two thirds of the votes of the Committee members' to pass. The Supreme Court will determine the bills which has less than two thirds of the votes to be repealed.

### 1042001 · 小径① 聚光灯之外

> Trail ① Outside the Spotlight

???：The story has come to a new stage.

???：It is fascinating. It has plots of unwavering loyalty, survival from the jaws of death, and even a little bit of ... unconventional excitement!

???：Raise your head, and you will see this splendid spotlight grilling the top of our heads like a stove heating a frying pan.

???：We mumble, and mumble, and mumble ... something like arcanists, new changes, perseverance, and the "Storm." Right, that's it! The "Storm"!

???：The "Storm" is no longer something new here. Everyone in the story talks about the "Storm." People don't find it attractive anymore.

???：Once you turn the camera out of the spotlight, you'll see many other people on the stage. Many, many other f*****g people.

???：The other actors. Actors who are different from you.

???：Their name won't be listed in the cast at the end of the movie. Not even a poor "St. Pavlov Squad Leader A."

???：Neat question. People always need a place to come from.

???：Then I will give you a neat answer as well, you inquisitive little thing. They came from their mothers' tummies. Can you understand it? Is it too difficult for you?

???：They're a bunch of people, a bunch of people just like you, though there are still differences between you and them. Open their bellies, and you can find everything that should be there—the liver, the stomach, the spleen, the lungs, and the heart.

???：Their faces are clear to be seen. Look at them carefully, carefully. Oh, you might have met one or two of them.

???：They come from the same institution as yours, the St. Pavlov Foundation.

???：Eve from the France branch, La from the Egypt branch, and the Matryoshka from the Russia branch.

???：Oh right, your first friend made in a branch, that—that young man with a red nose—Ken! Ken from the Scotland branch. You looked here, you looked there, you couldn't find him anywhere, however hard you tried.

???：Good question. I'll help you find him.

???：Let's keep looking around, but this time, vertically instead of horizontally. That's right! Upside vertical. Don't be afraid of that radiant spotlight, look there! Ignore the risk of blinding your eyes!

???：And you see it. A white umbrella, bigger and wider than any umbrella you have seen. Those green flashes stinging your eyes is the price you have to pay for finding it.

???：The white umbrella is a shelter from the rain, saving that Orange-hair girl in white for you.

???：And you have witnessed how people standing in the pouring "Storm" without shelter fade away.

???：Like your little friends, and Ken from the Scotland branch.

???：Remember the differences I just mentioned?

???：A great time to keep silent.

???：Your ugly trauma, a mixture of your mental issues and miserable past, would burst out in the next second, making everyone's heart twitch.

???：But think otherwise.

???：If the truth were known to all—today's not real, and there's no tomorrow, I'm going to eat my shoes, and an asteroid is falling—then all of them would go crazy.

???：Those "pigeons" valuing sense above anything won't sit aside and let this happen.

???：So they made the most sensible choice. The huge white umbrella has only sheltered those qualified people. "Qualified" according to their standard.

???：Now, let's cross our arms and make an arrogant judgment like those cynics, "How cruel it is!" And then, you may start contemplating.

???：Should they all go aboard the ark? Can this ark carry these many people?

???：Try to raise the question as peacefully and slowly as you can. And their enthusiasm will be dampened and let out a sound like what you hear when you pour water into a heated pan—sssss ...

???：Their feelings. Oh, they! You mean Eve, La, and the Matryoshka! Of course, you care about your friends. That's a virtue of yours I won't forget.

???：In order to understand their feelings, you need to bend down and reach. That's right, reach for the dark corner under the stage.

???：Until your fingertip is wrapped by soft, warm, and considerate black velvet, as comfortable as a mother's singing to her baby in a crib.

???：That's what the people without a shelter above them are feeling now.

???：More silence. You are really good at finding the right timing for your silence, just like my friend and I are experts in farting soundlessly in the elevator cart.

???：My hypocrite and dumb friend, think about it in a different way.

???：Who knows how much garbage there is under the stage? There may be liquid seeping out of rotten pastry and dairy products, intrusions of American cockroach with sleek abdomen crawling everywhere ...

???：Who can say it's not a blessing to know nothing about the truth?

???：Especially when you can't leave this shitty place.

### 1042101 · 小径① 被裁剪过的旧报纸

> Trail ① A Clipping of Old Newspaper

一则有关弃票的讨论。

**A Clipping of Old Newspaper**

What Does Abstained Vote Mean And The Necessity of Abstention Ballot Box

Abstained Vote is one of many forms of protest votes.  
It usually turns up next to an empty seat, inside a dustbin, between the washing machine and the wall, in the middle of a book, or in one of the inner pockets of an old jacket. It is the invalid vote cast because the voter has abstained from voting, and thus would not be counted into the election result.

Different from other reasons which caused failure to vote, voters cast abstained votes because they neither approve nor disapprove of the available options of the voting. They may have swung between different answers, or even were once slightly more in favor of one motion/candidate over the rest of them, or hold a pessimistic opinion to all the options provided; whatever the reason might be, in the end, they decide to not support any motions/candidates in the election.  
Many have also argued that abstention from voting is, in fact, an action guided by a clear awareness of their given rights. As the subjects of rights, voters are entitled to the freedom not to vote.  
It is also argued by the same group of scholars that, when the voters are requested to choose between unfavorable options, it takes courage and determination to stay true to their own ideas and become the dissidents in this case. Such action is worthy of praise. They are individual thinkers who know what they need and are strong enough not to compromise.  
The Abstention Ballot Box is specially set up to count the votes of this group of people. It stands for a new possibility, a new way to the yet unknown solution to the problem, a relatively bumpy path compared to the easy way. Those who know its value and seek for the difficult path will eventually find their way in, and along which they shall find places they truly belong to.

The New York Daily  Jan 19th, 1961  
Written by: John Greymirror Brown

### 1042201 · 小径① 小狗儿之歌

> Trail ① The Song of A Puppy

节奏轻快，歌颂着某只小狗的勇气。

**The Song of A Puppy**

I once looked down at a brave young dog,  
It was gallant, the most courageous of all.  
It wears a hat in piebald, and has eyes of emerald.  
The first-magnitude stars sprinkle on its spine. Its tail was as green as the moss.

It was once asleep, once hungry, once afraid, once in tears,  
once an umbrella, once a tree,  
once a jumping coat rack and a ship  
Oh, but then, but then --  
It's again the brave young dog. Loyal and gutsy, it walks among the hearts of gold.
