### 1050301 · Personal Notebook

**Ms. Moissan's Personal Notebook**

October 9, 19██  
Ms. Sotheby seems to have lost some weight. The Foundation's diet is perhaps a bit monotonous ...

Nuts & Cheese Crisp / Raspberry Protein Crisp Pudding  
260g light cream, 45g sugar, 100g milk, 150g raspberry jam,  
3 eggs, 1 vanilla pod, 15g gelatin sheets

October 16, 19██  
Mission: Germany Branch  
▪  Arcane Materials Catalog  
▪  Inventory of Remaining Arcane Goods  
▪  Inventory of Remaining Arcane Equipment  
▪  List of Senior Technical Personnels

Contact: Ms. Mary Smith  
New Identity: Nola Ward

November 5, 19██  
Arrived at the branch. Is the number wrong?

There were some problems getting on board. Ms. Smith is missing, and my name is not in their HQ contact book.  
The letter of introduction allayed some of their suspicions, but I still need to wait for the contact to confirm my identity with HQ.

[A letter clipped to the log book]  
Ms. Moissan,  
The list and reports have been received; thank you for your efforts. The number of missing persons is fifteen percent higher than we expected. I will report this and might need your assistance in investigating this matter with HQ.  
I noticed Martin Moore is not on the list sent back this time. He is a top talent in the Germany Branch's drug research project, and I'm afraid we will need you to travel to Bavaria to find out the situation.  
Z  
From Foundation HQ

November 14, 19██  
The German Branch can no longer provide aerial vehicles.

November 19, 19██  
Ceramic workshop. I was probably only a few years old when I moved away ... but I still remember this spruce tree.

They didn't recognize me.

### 1050401 · In the "Storm"

**Analysis Report on the "Storm" Sample**

Sample Name: "Storm" Raindrop  
Analyzed by: Laplace Scientific Computing Center  
Sampling Date: [Obscured by liquid stain]/27 They asked a 12-year-old to collect samples?  
Sample Quantity: 5 ml  
Objective: Composition of "Storm" Raindrop  
Analysis Results:  
The sample contains water, sulfur dioxide, nitrogen dioxide, suspended solids (sediment particles, human hair, skin flakes, and dust), and unknown matter.  
Further observation of the unknown matter through arcane imaging shows that the substance presents a bifurcated structure with radial energy fluctuations, identified as an unstable unknown nuclide (temporarily named "Asymmetrical Nuclide R"). The substance disappeared 5 seconds after observation. In the image captured in the 4th second, the dispersion of surrounding energy was observed. No stable state of Asymmetrical Nuclide R was observed.  
A controlled experiment was conducted to explore the nature of Asymmetrical Nuclide R. Based on the results, it is believed that Asymmetrical Nuclide R is an important constituent in determining the characteristics of the "Storm." Further follow-up studies are recommended.

Appendix 1: Image of Asymmetrical Nuclide R  
[A black and white photograph taken during the experiment, in the center of which a white luminous "γ"-shaped substance can be distinguished.]

Appendix 2: Experiment Recording #1  
"For this direct contact experiment, 0.5 ml of raindrop was placed in contact with a laboratory mouse. Hair and skin tissue damage and deformation started from the contact site, with swelling observed around the wound. The wound spread outward ..."  
"Final depth of the wound ... about 3 mm, diameter ... about 11.5 mm."

Appendix 3: Experiment Recording #2  
"For this direct contact experiment, 0.5 ml of remaining raindrop was placed in contact with a laboratory mouse after the disappearance of Asymmetrical Nuclide R."  
"No visible skin tissue damage, no wounds."

No one is spared from the scourge of time, yet some still dare to try. How many years has Vertin been walking in the "Storm"?

4|Four years|four years|4 year|4 years|Four Years|four-year|４

### 1050601 · 小径① 何时与何处

> Trail① When and Where

???：You still look confused … I know, darling, you haven't even vomited up the mouthfuls of sea water you ingested deep into your stomach. Just relax and lie where you are! You don't have to get back on your feet right away.

???：Usually, yes, usually, when those drunkards and patients wake up from a coma, just like you do right now, they only have two questions.

???：The first one will help you find out the name of the land you are lying on.

???：And the other will help you calibrate that bizarre watch on your wrist.

???：Alright, now it's finally the right time to make things clear. Open your mouth and throw up whatever you have, sea water, questions ... Just get them all out, right here.

???：Oh, darling, it's an honor to be your first question and the one haunting your mind.

???：But I'm going to let you down, I'm afraid. I don't have any breathtaking stories to tell, just one simple fact after another. You know, the most popular topic among women my age is the healthy oatmeal our teeth can barely handle.

???：Luckily, my ears are more reliable than my teeth. I can still nag like I always do, answer questions to my knowledge, and have a little chit chat with you sometimes. So, to summarize it, just consider me a kindhearted chatterbox.

???：Oh darling, why not let it be? You're all on your own now. Don't ask for more when you already have someone to talk to, even if I'm only nagging most of the time.

???：We gotta let the past be the past, like how we deal with old newspapers, because all I speak is one simple fact after another.

???：Anyways, we will keep each other company for quite a while.

???：Finally, a question about yourself. In front of you is a boundless sea. All you can see, besides the beach you are lying on, is water, water, and water ...

???：A cry for help will be drowned out by the waves, and even the strongest legs are tied to the ground here and cannot break free.

???：See? There's no way to leave, but it's a good opportunity to have an island tour. The locals have good manners, so I think you can ask them to show you around. That's at least better than just goofing off.

???：Not even you can avoid asking this question. But a kindhearted soul already told you that time is moving forward as it used to be, step by step. So, the time is ... the year 2007.

???：Yes, time is back on the right track, going where it's supposed to be. Raindrops literally drop again, and no one goes back to the past anymore ... Well, some miss the good old days, though.

???：Oh ... You still doubt it, don't you? I can see it in your eyes. Don't get me wrong, I'm on your side. After all the hard times I have experienced, if anyone told me that my life was just a boring dream, one neither good nor bad, I would throw a shoe at them!

???：I can tell you are being burned by anxiety. But to find the "time" you want, we need to comb through the situation, like how you do with your hair.

???：Time is like a Sachertorte, and now it has been divided into many pieces. On the largest piece stands the white chocolate card—yes, the last good days of the 20th century.

???：The gentlemen are still growing handlebar mustaches, and the ladies have not yet flattened the curves on their bodies. New money buys one art piece after another to show off their good taste. They don't even mind turning their homes into museums.

???：A cafe named San Marco officially opens, gathering the most outstanding intellectuals and writers of the time. They talk and laugh there, as if nothing could stand in their way.

???：Then the cafe receives a neurologist whose briefcase carries the interpretation of dreams, and celluloid film and projectors become a new means of expression for artists.

???：At this point, helmets remain intact in the warehouse, gauze only works in hospitals, bullets only fly in the firing range, and the flame that swallows lives is not even lit ...

???：But soon, soon enough ... the young people who swore to retrieve their homeland will take up daggers and guns to fulfill their oath.

???：Buildings will shake like a leaf, and rocks will fall off and hit the walls. Screams will try to break free from people's throats. At that moment, a clip will be loaded secretly, in the name of supreme honor.

???：A golden age is about to end. A vortex of blood is already swirling in the river of time, waiting to devour everything that passes by.

???：When that actually happens, all the beautiful curves derived from nature, the bee-shaped brooches, the punch, the eiderdown duvets, everything will be destroyed, leaving only an insurance contract behind, and it doesn't even work.

???：Even this cafe will be smashed into concrete pieces.

???：See? We have combed the hair now. Everything I just talked about is the "time" you want.

???：Heh heh, you wouldn't buy the nonsense about the fairness of time when you reach my age.  
It actually feels different between those who keep their eyes shut and those who keep theirs open; likewise, those who kill never share the same feeling with those who are killed; this is also true between those who have enough food to eat and those who are starving ...

???：Alright, that's it. I've talked enough. Now get up, darling, and brush the sand off yourself. Trust me, you will find your own answer to this era.

### 1050701 · A Notice of Use

**Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version) Instructions for Use**

I. Preparation  
The Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version) is issued to the Foundation Special Operations Teams (FSOT) and will be distributed along with other equipment during your initial gear issuance. Please carefully check the equipment receipt list to confirm that you have received the correct model and number of items.  
The Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version) is developed by the Laplace Scientific Computing Center (LSCC). Designed for the daily mission needs of human team members, it does not require Arcane skills to operate. Before official use, proficiency in the correct use of this equipment is required, and the user must pass related practical training assessments.

II. Awakening and Linking  
When first equipping the Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version), please manually awaken and link the weapon by performing the following operations:  
▪Sit with eyes closed and perform deep breathing in 10-second intervals until the body reaches a meditative state;  
▪After opening your eyes, silently count to 5 seconds, then grasp the handle of the equipment with both hands. Release your hands after feeling a slight vibration, at which point the Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version) will be awakened and successfully linked to you.

III. Parameter Setting  
When first equipping the Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version), please follow these steps and wait for the weapon to adjust its parameters according to your body data:  
▪Grasp the handle of the equipment with both hands, quickly clench, and relax, repeating 3 times;  
▪Place the equipment upright, with the tail end against the tip of your right foot, raise your chin, and wait for the razor to buzz once;  
▪Place the equipment horizontally, lift the razor to shoulder height, turn your head left and right once each, and wait for the razor to buzz once;  
▪Read clearly in a moderate voice: "Entities should not be multiplied unnecessarily." Wait for the razor to buzz once.

IV. Equipment Maintenance  
The maintenance cycle for the Villiers W5 Strategic Razor (Enhanced Version) is 3 months.  
If you believe the weapon is exhibiting abnormalities, please contact the customer service personnel at the LSCC. Follow the instructions of the customer service personnel to send the weapon to the Computing Center, so that the center's technicians can check and debug your weapon's performance.

### 1051001 · 小径① 美妙巧合

> Trail① Wonderful Coincidence

???：As you can see, this school on the island lives in a box made by the truth. Even if they have a donut in their hands when they are hungry, they still see a topological space—like the cute ring attached to an initiation toy—it's not food anyways.

???：But if you give them a puzzle, they will enjoy it like the best Kaiserschmarrn ... Oh, my apologies. I was not talking about the one you are holding.

???：No. With all due respect, that thing in your hand is pretty old: the mold on it seems to come from the 18th century, judging from the smell. I'm sure you know it will make you sick if eaten.

???：But we have to admit, it is not just ordinary mold. It reminds us of the story in which, once, a merchant's son looked up at the night sky and found the answer to the stars for us.

???：The young man firmly believed that the movement of the stars and the sun follow certain laws, just like how the delicate gears in a watch work together.

???：And his point was even proven correct by Zeus's grandfather! When another gentleman observed the sky through the reflecting telescope he took great pains to make, a celestial body was exactly there.

???：What was it called ... Emm, 19.6, the eighth number in the sequence, the seventh planet in the solar system, and the legendary God of the Sky.

???：If the world was not created by God, are there any theories we can use to explain such a beautiful law?

???：Yes, the one that says it never actually exists. Anyone can see the breach in this number sequence. That is the planet, which is supposed to be the fifth number in the sequence. I mean, how come we have never seen it before, if it does exist?

???：Either God is wrong or we've been blind all this time. What do you think?

???：Oh, darling, I forgot that you are one of the boring people from the modern times.

???：You need to understand that we are talking about a forgotten masterpiece from the 18th century. Back then, everyone still believed that the imaginary creatures living on the stars got to witness the birth of God's creations and the everlasting temperature changes up there were set to punish the exiles. How romantic.

???：Yes, such a planet definitely exists, and as expected, it was discovered in the end. Yes, that's the Ceres. It only took that great mathematician an hour to find its orbit through calculation.

???：Oh, what great astronomy, what great mathematics, what great creation.

???：It was a beautiful dream, but it was still ended, eventually, by another discovery: the eighth planet.

???：Not the Ceres, nor the Uranus, but a new planet. People found it through more precise astronomical data and calculation, proving that the number sequence found by the merchant's son was only a myth.

???：Now we are clear of what we've been talking about: it's an unsolvable puzzle. It makes no sense from the very beginning.

???：We found the Ceres because it happened to be there. It is real, and no one can deny its existence.

???：In other words, we can perfectly prove the rationality of mathematics because we INVENTED it.

???：It's been a good dream, I suppose.

???：Now imagine that you're walking alone in the darkest valley ever, trying to drag your fragile body upward as much as possible before it is worn out. Hoarse cries squeeze their way out of your mouth to ask for response from the gods. Suddenly, an afflatus dawns on you. The next moment, a miracle happens: you are already at the top of the mountain, witnessing the rise of a huge celestial body from the skyline.

???：Even if it's only in your imagination, you have just witnessed the dawn of the Creation and the shock is real.

### 1051201 · 小径① 恰好的停顿

> Trail① Just the Right Pause

???：Come closer. That's right. Take a few more steps and immerse yourself in the shadow, including that hat. You don't want those good ears to hear this private conversation.

???：That pad of paper from the miss, yes, the one wielding the ruler, is quite valuable. I can tell it is of a certain age and records quite a lot of anecdotes.

???：One visitor to the island took out a baguette from his luggage. To share it with others, he made an attempt to parcel it off. Another visitor fell to the ground and happened to sit on a quart measure and break it ...

???：You can learn the punishments for them from the paper: the former was thrown into the sea, while the latter, into the further sea.

???：Only God knows how this wine-dark sea holds so many visitors.

???：Oh, the debate! That's really a pain in the neck for that logical, pragmatic assistant of yours, isn't it? People always tend to mark what they can't figure out as "arcanists' doings," as if things would make sense once they do so.

???：But it won't change the fact that the debate is only a game involving sophistry, improvisation, and straw man fallacy. So what makes it superior to beans, if the Form is what they are really after?

???：Anyways, whoever fools the others first will win the game, no arcanum at all.

???：And that argument of hers is the best attraction for the audience ... A special number, a "0."

???：You are neither positive nor negative, which means ... you don't belong to either side. Now imagine one side as an orderly line and the other as those wandering around. The wanderers can smell the abnormality in you, and of course, no one in that line sees you as one of them.

???：In a restaurant, fruits are served without the seeds, but in a farm, the seeds are retained for the next harvest.

???：Is there any value in the seeds? It depends on what you see when you look at them.

???：The moment you realize that, you have come to the crossroad of fate.

???：That's ambitious, darling, and I love it! But you need to know that infinity is the most unfamiliar thing for the people, and that's why it triggers the most curiosity.

???：Whoever tries to eat up this elephant with one bite will get all bloated and pinned by their own weight, waiting to die like a turtle upside down.

???：By then, I guess you would be eager to throw up the elephant and turn back into the adorable, easygoing "0" we all like.

???：See, just like you, people are always on the way to finding something that can satisfy them undoubtedly, as if what they already have was disappointing. They stare at the flame of the matches, read the tea leaves in the cup, and bustle around the instruments with star patterns ...

???：Now, back to the number we just talked about ... Oh my, it's the prediction of your whole life. Can you believe that?

???：You owe that spirited little genius a thank-you ... By the way, what do you see in her number?

???：Oh yes, of course, 37 is larger than 0.

???：But it's not so stable as 0. These prime numbers, they are just hard to divide ... because they have very few things in common with others.

???：That's why she's always so energetic, highly and solely focused on what she takes an interest in. She has nothing like the characteristics of the ones you know in the gray and white house. Those people are proud of themselves for having kindhearted factors around them.

???：As their teaching goes, it's always dangerous to be unique.

???：But some look up at her again and again exactly because they can sense the danger in her.

???：You are also a good example. You've been looking up, haven't you?

???：I always know you're a quick learner. You're very good at noticing new things.

???：That's right. 37 is only 37, a nice, unique prime number which equals the word "genius." At least everyone prefers to think this way.

???：Why not deify the one that is able to reach the truth when you have the chance? It's much easier to pin your hopes on others than to blame yourself.

???：I guess you also did that, right, darling?

???：It's okay. She never says no. She just lets the hopes pile up on her shoulder ... because she simply doesn't care at all.

???：What a good, good child.

### 1051301 · Light-hearted Poem

**Bacchus's Poem**

This wording is hasty and reckless,  
Yet no offense is meant to your extraordinary spirit (yes, you naturally know),  
You know, along with the many, many  
Countless blind admirers,  
I am deeply attracted to  
Your imposing breadth of mind,  
That makes you eloquent even facing a stone wall.

Oh, how astute!  
Only by avoiding labor can one prevent mental fatigue,  
Eloquence triumphs over facts,  
And wit refutes truth,  
Truly, a genius in your own right.

In the team, everyone is equal,  
The wise argue with the mediocre, the mediocre chatter confidently,  
Sincere praise (truly sincere!)  
For your joy in sharing with everyone,  
So considerate and comprehensive, yet so broad and not specialized.

And you lead us in the dance! And to  
Draw a circle, then smear it randomly,  
For logic is all a play,  
And wakefulness is all sleep.

### 1051401 · 小径① 第三条岔路

> Trail① The Third Fork in the Road

???：Oh, look at you, climbing out of the cave in the allegory, all dusty and dirty like a little savage.

???：They must have tried to fill your little brain with long preachment, but you should feel lucky because at least they didn't give you a quiz right after that.

???：Oh, that's our expert on absent-mindedness. Yes, we should bear in mind those glory days of yours back in school.

???：Oh, how modest you are. Looks like you didn't spend all your student days catching tree frogs.

???：Try to reach the corners of your mind, if you would, and there you will find another bunch of people standing in front of a stone table.

???：Yes, those people in gowns, white gowns with gray diamond patterns. One of them was standing on the stairs, gathering all the little vacant eyes to his side. What was he called? Give me a second ...

???：Teacher, instructor, tutor, educator ... Just titles like that, no names at all. No one gets to write down their names on that banner, after all.

???：But they have nothing to complain about. You know what they say, "What is reasonable is real; that which is real is reasonable."

???：They showed you a broad avenue in their teachings, where you could enjoy a sense of security with all the solid ground, convenient automobiles, and warm sunshine. And at the end of the avenue even lies an inspiring mission, to which you are expected to devote your whole life.

???：There is a distinction between good and bad here, and it's as clear as our innate sense of right and wrong, like a floor of black and white tiles. Any stains on it will be noticed immediately.

???：But in fact, there exists another path in the woods. It's much less traveled, leading to the unknown darkness which cannot be seen with eyes or proved false. You may even experience indescribable chaos and madness there. This path, however, has an excellent view, and it's one of a kind: the plants are thriving with bursting energy, and the flowers are blooming in a delightful atmosphere.

???：... So, which will you take?

???：Oh, of course, as you've been on it all the time. But that's exactly why you know how ... inflexible it is. You know that even better than any of those old fellas who have spent decades on it, don't you?

???：Just because you've been on it doesn't mean you can expect a comfortable journey, darling.

???：But luckily, there's still one more way you can take. It would be a good choice if you want to enjoy the scenery without getting lost in the darkness.

???：Oh, that's not an easy choice to make. This grandma must give you a round of applause. I would have even pinned a boutonniere to your suit if I had one. But I have to remind you, those things in there are not easy to deal with. They can drive you insane, and you have to be very careful ...

???：You've seen the living proof: many were freaked out after they came back, and people had to tie and lock them up; others even dug their own graves.

???：Oh my, that was a bit over the top. I hope I didn't scare you, darling.

???：But luckily, there's still one more way you can take. It would be a good choice if you want to enjoy the scenery without getting lost in the darkness.

???：I'm sure you know that myth.

???："You shall take the soul of your love out of the underworld, yet you shall not look back." That's right. You shall pass by the sleeping sirens, yet you shall not make a sound.

???：The ones with a belief set off. They entered the dark woods barefooted, yet moved so smoothly as if they were walking on the avenue.

???：They kept their mouths shut and eyes forward. They never turned back, never picked up what was not theirs, never set foot on the forbidden lands, and never looked straight at the sacred greatness.

???：They showed frugality, patience, and wisdom in the face of hunger and danger.

???：So the beasts continued to lurk in the shadow, and the ones with a belief passed through the woods safely until the path disappeared along with the direction sign. Then the first one to look back gave an exclamation before being exiled.

???：Think about what I just said, "That which is real is reasonable."

???：Never look back in the woods, darling! If you don't want to be turned into a pillar of salt which can do nothing but wail, then ... NEVER, EVER look back.

???：Yes, we'd better stay awake, stay calm, stay cautious all the time.

???：But then there comes the problem: how can anyone ... how can you ignore your love for those thriving plants and blooming flowers? How are you supposed to deal with the unstoppable feeling that overflows like a melody when it is stirred up by the beauty of the scenery?

???：Of course, you will cry the loudest cry, dance the wildest dance, drink the strongest drink, and vomit all the filth inside you.

???：After all, there is no need to enter the woods if the soul-stirring scenery is not what you are after, right?

???：No, so you'd better let it slip your mind, darling.

???：Just let it go, and hurry forward. Remember to keep your eyes shut and hold your breath.

???：When you do so, the scenery will no longer mean anything to you, nor will the fragrance of the flowers. You will get out of the woods and reach where the truth lies.

???：Is that what you want? Don't worry. We have plenty of time. You may come back at any time and pick another way.

### 1051402 · Strange Cave

**Mediator's Fate**

In the fable of the sun,  
Sunlight is always rhythmic,  
Never to be dampened,  
Not to be an umbrella, a ship, or shelter in a downpour,  
Not to be a shore, a reef,  
To be battered, to be worn down against the waves,  
Not to be a cause of capsizing, grounding, lingering, surviving,  
Nor to stop moss,  
Nor nurture the buds,  
To harmonize all colors, but not to pour into each one.  
Before the rain veils the sunlight,  
Before the clouds swallow the faint shadows,  
There remains one last question,  
One last answer,  
One last way to dissolve conflicts,  
One last neutral party,  
One last bystander.

November 13 No equipment abnormalities.

November 14 No equipment abnormalities.

November 15 Shutdown for 1 hour 15 minutes to replace the meter. Also obtained a new type of connector board from off the island, scheduled to be replaced the next day.

November 16 Replaced punched paper cards. No equipment abnormalities.

November 17 Replaced the new connector board. Shutdown for 3 hours, replacement completed.

November 18 No equipment abnormalities. Disposal of the original connector board: I gave it to my daughter, Sophia, who is quite fond of studying these. The disposal has been approved.

November 19 No equipment abnormalities.

November 20 Replaced the punched paper cards. No equipment abnormalities.

### 1051801 · 小径① 格格不入者

> Trail① The Misfit

???：Come here. Place your hand on it. Feel the texture. The craft is one of a kind, not some cheapie you can get from the flea market.

???：I mean it. This is a kilim, and even the most traditional, delicate, and expensive kind. It has applied fiery red as the dominant color, and the stitches are perfectly neat.

???：From the sea, just like you, darling.

???：It arrived with a meerschaum pipe, a glass jar, a pack of sugar cubes, and a child in panic.

???：Both she and the kilim were born on the land where the craft originated. Their appearances apply the same dominant color, and the patterns in them share similar complexity.

???：Oh, your curiosity is aroused? But we are not done with the carpets yet. You've always gotta get a souvenir or two every time you reach a new place, so you will have something to show when you tell the stories one day, right?

???：Look at these handmade fabrics. How splendid ... Those incredible patterns were the only thing on the craftswomen's minds when they made the carpets. It's safe to say they have spent their whole life improving their skills at using the vertical loom, and that's how we get these carpet knots arranged in beautiful lines.

???：But they are just people like us. What if they get distracted? What if the carpet knots go off the right track and get tied to the wrong threads?

???：Then you will be in trouble, big trouble! All the problems in the universe will come at you, and the situation will be even worse than the bureaucratic system of a millennial empire.

???：Calm down. Many swear like you do when there is nothing they can do.

???：They always step into the same river named Mistake again and again. That's why we need THEM, those crossing the river with their rulers, spanners, tool boxes, and dusty faces.

???：I like the pragmatic quality in you. I'm sure it will make your life even more wonderful. The people will be impressed and line up to tackle the problems for you.

???：Oh, don't be mad. Some make troubles, and others deal with the troubles. That happens.

???：You know, every question has a questioner when they are raised. Likewise, every error has a corrector when they are corrected. That's how we get to know THEM.

???：The Correctors, an ancient profession.

???：Their duty is to remove the loose threads and iron out the wrinkles on the carpets. In the language of this island, that is to check the calculations on the papyri and even the holes on the punched tapes.

???：No one is capable of doing this job except the altruists, because it is similar to unraveling a ball of wool. Besides finding the end of the threads, you must always pay attention to the cats. Yes, the cats. They may jump on the ball and make things even worse.

???：And she, the red-haired little girl with the carpet in her arms, was overwhelmed at the beginning.

???：Not yet familiar with the ruler, she tried her best to find the end of the threads. So the cats took the opportunity to jump on her together and left countless scratches on that little forehead. Poor girl.

???：See, that's the problem every Corrector has to deal with: their job is to correct errors, but what if their own existence is also part of the errors?

???：Oh, it's no big deal. There is more than one loom in this world.

???：She left the complicated patterns behind and came to this simple kingdom featuring numbers only.

???：There she made the first contact with the spinning loom named geometry. She had never seen anything like that before, and it was almost impossible for her to comprehend how it worked. She could hardly convert the numbers on the books into an actual diagram in her brain.

???：Without a whole picture of what she was working on, she resorted to her hard work, and more hard work. Then a long time passed, leaving one wound after another on her fingers. Later, the wounds even became calluses.

???：In the end, her hands were no longer tender, but tough enough to resist the damage from the loom. Yes, she has won a place on this island for herself.

???：Yes, she's a quick learner. She learned even faster than some of the locals, but that was not enough, because she has been outmatched by her playmate, the leading one in the field of mathematics on the island.

???：That girl is like a perfect piece of satin born by nature. It is decorated by the beauty of mathematics and geometry, no manual work involved.

???：In order to reach the showcase where that perfect piece lies, and ... to stay on the right track and fit into the school ...

???：She has done everything she could.

???：But, BUT! She is still the most distinctive carpet there.

???：Anyone can tell that as long as they spend some time on the island.

???：Of course not. She's only a bit "different" from others.

???：So she will continue to weave on the loom until one day the spindle pierces her callus and the fire burns everything to the ground.

???：Shh, I should stop here. You know that mathematician who was thrown into the sea? He died because he said something he shouldn't have.

???：Cut not wood on a public road; open not an unwanted bottle.

???：I don't know what to say next. I guess the best line for this circumstance is, "See you later."

## 章节 106

### 100601 · A Doctor Interpreting Dreams

???：*breath in* *breath out* O-oh, you're here? Not a good time. As you can see, my eyes are barely open.

???：*sighs* In the blink of an eye, my dream slips away like a deer into the deep of the forest.

???：Heh, what kind of question is that? You make it sound like me dreaming is the most ridiculous thing in the world!

???：I have to remind you, except for some out-dated wisdom, there is nothing different between your head and mine, dearie.

???：I'm as curious about that as you are.

???：You could always allow me to return to my soft bed to try to summon that dream again. But even then, it likely won't be the same dream.

???：See, we seldom remember our dreams, and even what we don't forget can be hard to understand. And that's why we need a dream interpreter.

???：A doctor that values Shakespeare above his patients, and cigars above his own career—a doctor like Sigmund Freud.

???：He lures out forgotten memories from your past, through a technique called "free association." Through this, he can reveal what makes you who you are.

???：All you need to do is to lie on the sofa, and spill out your thoughts like old milk.

???：Once you do that, your mental health will surely improve. The sleepwalkers stop falling out of their beds, the hysterical go back to their sewing, with enough time maybe Nietzsche could write a comedy.

???："But what about the medical journals?" You may ask. He must have released them. Otherwise, how could we prove the effectiveness of his treatments?

???：And he has. You can read them in journals, in the text books, and hear them in the chattering of medical students—

???：Your name will be blacked out on the pages by then, but your dreams will become the latest gossip on everyone's lips.

???：Oh my dear, all medicine has side effects. The best medicine is bitter to the taste.

???：It must be tolerated for the sake of the treatment. How else will these patients free themselves from their mental cages of unconscious desire and delusion?

???：It really is the smallest of prices—a mere coffee stain on your shirt, just telling a few people about an unlucky moment or two.

???：Besides, the patient does not give a name. The true protagonist remains a secret between you and the doctor.

???：Heh. Perhaps you're right. It's not the only way ...

???：Come, listen ... Do you hear that? That shrill, piercing sound of metal on the skull, the blood-cuddling screams ... Even the strongest soul would quake in fear.

???：But this is the other way. However, funny enough, some people prefer the pain. It ends faster than a hard talk with the doctor.

### 100622 · 小径① 剧场印象

> Trail ① Theater Impressions

???：Hey, you! Yes, you, my hurried child, you rush through the street like a cyclone ... Where are you going?

???：A show, a show! So you came to Vienna for the love of stories. That's for sure.

???：Then this is just the right place for you. A place where the floors are decorated with gold, where music sings along with the stories ... Even a newcomer could be entranced here.

???：Oh, listen to me! You must not be a newcomer, but a true connoisseur! Well, before you leave, may I ask a simple question?

???：Which act in a show do you prefer?

???：Oh! Well begun is half done! You're always right to the point. We should know how a story starts and when we have stepped onto the path of fate.

???：Haha! The rising action! The most thrilling part, like biting into the filling of an apple strudel—you're about to have the sweetest part!

???：Yes, of course! The resolution, coming to its fated ending. We stand up and cheer, or stay in our seats and weep ... Our reward for not falling asleep during the intermission, worth every krone you've paid.

???：Haha, so greedy. It's a tough choice, isn't it? But you can't escape from making decisions.

???：Do you know which act I love the most? Not Act One, nor Three or Five ...

???：But Six. Oh! Act Six, my dear, that's the best part.

???：The part in which the king picks up his fallen head; the knight pulls out the sword in his chest; the bodies stand up, dust their clothes and come to the front of the stage, bow to the audience and receive their applause ...

???：Yes, it is! My child, that is precisely when the real show begins.

???：I have a good script here. Let me put on my glasses ... Hmm ... Let's start from ... from Act Six.

???：The princess, a nightingale decorated with diamonds, an opera singer taking center stage, steps into a building of gray-and-white diamond patterns.

???："And the curtain falls forever. The smile on her face seems too serene. She is still dreaming."

???："Her madness keeps them all alert. But in the court, she seems so meek and mentally steady, like a docked boat on calm waters."

???："Nodding elegantly to every gentleman she meets, she makes the shackle on her neck look like a piece of dazzling jewelry."

???：Next page ... oh.

???："In a transparent room, under an unyielding limelight, her dignity is stomped on, like a silk handkerchief thrown on the ground. They smash her crown, forcing her to pay for her madness."

???：No ... not anymore.

???："'Sleep, she said to me, sleep. If that's what she said, then I don't need to wake up.'"

???："That's when the star falls into an ever-lasting dream. The blinding light becoming a spotlight on the stage; the empty room carrying the best reverberations. She has never been so calm. Everything beautiful in her is there forever, frozen in time."

???：Or found eternal peace.

???："Her world has become the performance. A show that never ends."

???：I'll say she's a great actress, isn't she?

???：Hahaha, my dear. You are indeed a great connoisseur.

???：Only one act of a play can't tell a good story by itself.

???：Oh, forgive my ramblings. The golden curtain has risen.

???：Fix your collar, darling, and keep moving forward.

### 100603 · Employee Notes

**St. Pavlov Foundation Vienna Branch Employee Notes**

Procedure for Requesting the Dispelling of a Mute Incantation (Simplified Version)

1. Initiating the Application

The applicant must go to the administrative desk of the St. Pavlov Foundation, Vienna Branch - Austro-Hungarian Empire Internal Security Department - Austro-Hungarian Empire Public Security Bureau with a ticket to receive the Joint Processing Application Form.

2. Filling Out the Form

The applicant must fill out the application form truthfully and submit it to the administrative desk of the Austro-Hungarian Empire Public Security Bureau, where the administrative staff will conduct a preliminary review.

3. Commencement of the Dispelling Procedure

After the administrative staff has conducted a preliminary review, the form will be submitted to the Social Responsibility Monitoring Group of the St. Pavlov Foundation, Vienna Branch - Austro-Hungarian Empire Internal Security Department - Austro-Hungarian Empire Public Security Bureau for risk assessment.

4. Commencement of the Assessment

Members of the Social Responsibility Monitoring Group will conduct a detailed investigation and assessment of the applicant, fill out their review comments, and submit them to the team leader.

5. Secondary Approval

The team leader will then summarize the review comments and submit them to the director of the Public Affairs Bureau for a secondary approval, who will then submit the application to the Internal Affairs Department.

6. Completion of the Process

Upon approval, the director of the Public Affairs Bureau will initiate an interdepartmental collaboration process and send the comments of the secondary approval to the Harm Prevention Office, which will then issue a notification letter to the administrative desk.

7. Execution of the Dispel

Upon receipt of the notification letter, the administrative desk will archive it and issue another notification letter informing the applicant to participate in the mute incantation dispel ritual.

Don't forget any of the steps or you'll regret it! This is my third @#&* time starting over! D**n it all. I don't ever want to fill out these d**n forms again!

### 100604 · The Mitosis

???：Look at this, dearie. This kind of hand mirror has become the latest fashion. Oh, it's a trifling thing, but people like me have to stay in the loop to make a living.

???：Come here. Gaze into the mirror, and fix your hat. What do you see? Do you like what you see?

???：A clever answer! I can tell you will fit right in here.

???：But there is still much left to see beyond the mirror. Oh. The young folk step to an ever-changing waltz. If you want to follow their trends, you'll need perfect timing to join the swirling dance.

???：Some have fallen behind. They lounge by the fountains of the park, bemoaning their lives and the price of rent. Come the next banquet and exhibition, they arrive wearing a new fine coat, but underneath their linens are ragged and old.

???：The mirror's reflection held a silver sheen once. Now it's tarnished at the edges.

???：Oh, look at our little artist in the mirror.

???：Something of a narcissist, then? No, I'm not laughing at you. In this place, valuing one's uniqueness, one's own voice is much to be desired.

???：You have to be loud enough, firm enough to show no fear, nor hesitation. So that at the end of the waltz, you will have the strength to break their instruments.

???：Is that so? Oh, has something gone wrong? Did an old lady like me waste her money?

???：Or, perhaps what it reflects is the "truth."

???：The mirror shows reality, nothing more, nothing less. So, it may be that there is nothing to see, or it may be something that can't be seen.

???：But things change, darling, especially in this city. Like an avalanche taking everything in its path, changing them irrecoverably and inevitably.

???：So next time when you look into a mirror like this, you may just see something else. Be patient.

???：So, in the warmth of spring, the ice cracks deeper. One splinters into two, and each begins to splinter again.

???：They set up the mirror and lift the cloth exposing to light—the good, the bad, and what lies beyond them both.

???：Then the factions split and then split again. "Secessionists," by their nature, are not in the business of keeping things together. They won't stop the fractures—they can't.

???：In lifting the cloth and seeing their reflection, they scatter themselves like dandelion seeds in the wind.

???：Oh, I don't know where, but I do know what they're doing. Some of them still paint with gold leaf, others believe they must depict what is real, some depict the ravings of their dreams, some seek to change the streets into works of art, and others drive out all meaning from their work.

???：The only time they ever reach an agreement is just before they lift the cloth from the mirror.

???：Before the Potemkin village with all its empty facades moves somewhere else, some of them might just achieve their ends.

???：To be like the golden cabbage sitting atop its dome over the Ring Road—to be recognized by everyone.

### 100605 · Trail ① The Weight of a Name

???：Let's start our conversation with a simple exercise. First, please repeat after me: Dittarsdorf.

???：After all the days you've spent in Vienna, you should be familiar with the name. Tell me, how do you feel about it?

???：Nothing indeed. Well, you've become numb to it after hearing it so many times, on the street corners, in the cafes, every place you go.

???：You can't blame them for that. Most at least speak kindly of them, so long as you don't pick up on the venom hiding in their words.

???：These days, to be an opera singer is more glorious than any noble title; it supersedes them all! The Dittarsdorfs are the true nobles, the ones who can create art through "channeling."

???：Haha, you are making light of it again. But fair enough, you're new here, not as familiar with this name as those who live here, murmuring it in their dreams.

???：Most of the time, it swirls invisibly through the conversations on the street; other times, it is tangible in the signatures of the exhibits, seen in the performances and the metal badges clinging to collars ... and always in the boys and girls who inherit the Dittarsdorf talents.

???：And just like that, they are thrust into the role of the outstanding Dittarsdorfs, the lady songbird and the artistic painter.

???：Oh, of course. Well, then, high time to tell you about them.

???：Vienna, at present, is a splendid, crumbling house. Whenever the waltz starts playing, the roof trembles along with the music. While the dancing crowd—and funny that it is a crowd—none of them dare look up at it!

???：The people here live in identical, pretty, little houses, the social classes smoothed out like butter on a cake. Now, works of art become their icons, and in their madness, they scramble to make these beautiful, dazzling things.

???：The Dittarsdorfs, nobles who can perform channeling, are made to perform like dancing animals in a circus. Businessmen and nobles clamoring to sponsor their performances in order to glorify themselves.

???：The noble ladies prefer to curate the finest acts, highlighting their good taste and fashionability, while the lordlings seem to prefer new and hidden talents as some example of their virtue.

???：How, then, could a good singer ever miss a performance? But she must, to take her chance, seizing the lovely bird in her hand—that poor, noble little bird, because people said that she was born with madness.

???：And it is in this act of "fighting against her madness" that she proves herself proper among the noble ladies.

???：She knows better than anyone why a piece of art is valuable, and how to make it worth even more.

### 100607 · Children of the Empire

???：The Danube, a river born from dewdrops grown into a mighty course. She is a mother to many children, and all are eager to be fed.

???：Here is the empire founded by two of her strongest children—a rich and admirable duo.

???：There is an alluring charm to the music of Musikverein, captivating everyone who visits this place. I can talk about its art all day, and that's just the beginning!

???：There are 11 children in this empire. When bond together, they are invincible.

???：Their enemies will face catastrophe.

???：See, the flourishing cities, the cheering citizens, even the chirping birds are singing a song of a land of freedom.

???：Heh heh ho. You're familiar with the devils in the detail.

???：Ah. You're just buying everything I'm selling. Be smart, my dear.

???：You'll see, if we dive deeper, you'll see something different.

???：You'll see charity dances raising funds for the poor. The icing on the cake for the philanthropist, they said, though no one knows where the money goes.

???：You'll see mice eating the bodies of the beggars, the arcanists being ousted from their arts, and poverty spreading its misery like an illness.

???：Except in the great duo, Austria and Hungary, in the vast and intimidating centers of power they've built together.

???：The other children, however, were shut out at the door. They may have their talents, but that alone cannot buy them a place at the table.

???：Bohemia, Moravia ... The empire has only the slightest sympathy to these forgotten children, nothing more than articles of fine words written in the newspapers.

???：How funny it is. To see these poor humans now sharing in the same fate as the arcanists.

???：Do you think they will choose to hold together, even if left outside of the negotiation table?

???：That's no small task. A chain is only as strong as its weakest link. An age-old story.

???：Oh. So you're a pessimist. Then, I guess, you will enjoy the drama of this broken family.

???：The truth is, they will fight each other like rats in the barrel, until every one of them falls.

???：Crack, crack. Did you hear it? It's the fracturing of a great empire, like the breaking of spring ice.

???：In an army that speaks twenty different languages, who can a general trust to obey his commands?

???：Only those who speak the language he does.

???：You can already imagine it—a ridiculous beast with 11 limbs, each pulling in their own direction, limping across the battlefield until it gets shot ...

???：Luckily, that time hasn't come yet.

???：The crying of the children is still being drowned out by the music playing in the theater, and the beating hearts within the dancers' chests.

???：The wails of suffering are silent in the newspapers, and the world sees only the silvery mirage of freedom and prosperity.

???：Believing all these children can live in harmony.

???：But my darling, we know that the ice must break. It's as inevitable as the thaw of spring.

### 100611 · "Trash"

**Faltering Footsteps**

May 26, 1913

We can no longer pretend that nothing is happening here! The world is about to descend into chaos!

The upper echelons of the empire were indeed harboring a Russian spy. Before this, everyone regarded him as a hero of the empire, but he turned out to be a secret operative, sending his own compatriots to the enemy's guillotine. Who knows how much intelligence he has sent back to Russia? Is our country just a naked fool in the eyes of the Russians?

The whole of Europe is secretly in turmoil, wars are breaking out everywhere—everywhere! Even in Vienna, one can always hear whisperings of war, and the people no longer have the heart or the mind to appreciate art. The bullets of war have shattered the glass domes of Vienna, the sound of shattering echoes through the nights of beautiful dreams, scratching the phonograph records, and clogging the tubes of the horn.

Is tonight the last time I will sit in the opera house? Oh, beautiful place, like a mother's womb, only here can my grief and panic about the war be alleviated. Perhaps I should transfer my family fortune, but where to? Or should I exchange it for gold bars and jewels, and hide them in a secret compartment under my bed? It was only two short years ago! How wonderful it was then—there was no constant call to war between nations, and mankind was as innocent as Adam and Eve before they left the Garden of Eden ... No ... No ... I shouldn't make such comparisons. I do not wish to face a fate of eternal damnation.

Perhaps all this is just baseless speculation on my part. Perhaps I have been infected by those madmen who fantasize about the end of days. Now, there are few countries that even come close to the Austro-Hungarian Empire, and I cannot imagine scenes of war ravaging Vienna any time soon. Such a scene would indeed herald the end of the entire world ...

*A fine leather notebook! An expensive gold-plated pen! What's that embossed on its velvet box ...? "Fischer"?

*The good parts have already been ripped off! D**n it! Just leave this broken book in the trash!

**Shoeshine Boys**

"Come, sir, put your feet up, please. I'll wipe the dust off your shoes, and we can even talk about the opera, if you'd like. The opera, yes! Isn't it just all over the posters, in the streets and alleys! Isn't it just, sir! Please, just a few more coins, sir, so that I can afford a ticket for tonight ... Of course, I understand, sir, I know all the lines off by heart! "Pourquoi faut-il que le destin l'ait mise là sur mon chemin?" I learned that off a kind gentleman outside the theater ..."

...

...

"Look, sir! I bet you don't even recognize your shoes anymore! I can even see my face in them. I've polished them so hard ..."

"You must be here to see the sights of Vienna, right? The streets here are spotless, and after this polish, you won't have to clean your shoes for at least ten days ... Yes, if things carry on like this, we'll have to go to school. I hear they teach French there ... Next time you come to Vienna, maybe I'll be that little apprentice up on the stage."

### 100613 · 小径① 维也纳咖啡

> Trail ① Viennese Coffee

???：Ah ... A lovely day to rest at an outdoor cafe, isn't it, darling? Everyone working here is a capable server and knows how to address you properly with "my lady."

???：I'm here for one simple thing: information. With a careful ear to the goings-on, a shrewd businesswoman can gather all the latest news.

???：Oh, there she comes, that girl in green with the bouncing feathers in her hat. Don't worry. She can't hear us from here.

???：Of course, you do. Isn't she our lively little cockatoo? She says she's a psychiatrist, but with all the work she does for the people, she's more like Vienna's own Robin Hood.

???：Look at those people around her, tut-tut. With their gold cufflinks and patched sleeves ... Very likely they're from Leopoldstadt.

???：You can hear them saying, "Our golden era is finally here! Forget all these melodramatic rumors!"

???：Maybe you know it by a different name? Perhaps you caught a glimpse of it under the list of exhibit sponsors?

???：Europe, at that time, welcomed people from all over the world; its culture and art held as their highest ideals; its institutions believed they held the keys to paradise.

???：They once believed that they were the lucky few. Blessed with the wisdom of a golden age, that's why they still linger here even after the smoke of war faded the glimmers of gold away.

???：It's not surprising to see our lady with them. See, she loves to seek these well-hidden things, and chirp along with the most overlooked voices.

???：But I feel sorry for her, because she herself overlooks some of these teeny-tiny things said at her own expense.

???：Words taken as jokes. Ignored because her wishes and anticipation outweigh their sting.

???：But while songs are floating in the wind, the clock keeps ticking, people in the cafe talk about the secessionists and the newest plays in the same breath ... No one cares about the rumors trafficked in the newspapers. They're only concerned with what is in front of their eyes ...

???：Still, the good doctor comes to share the pain of that melancholy lady, and then, next to the cafe, to encourage the lower class to persevere.

???：She says that every man is entitled to the same rights. She'll draw a circle around them all, like the Ring Road or the round mirrors in her clinic.

???："The good times will come again," so say the coffee sippers. But you know, in the end, they'll be like the undissolved sugar at the bottom of the cup.

???：My dear, your coffee is getting cold.

### 100614 · 小径① 枪响前夜

> Trail ① The Night before the Gunshot

???：Darling, did you hear that? The sound of a match scraping against the box, igniting in a dark, silent alley ... and then, "BOOM!"

???：War, like an unstoppable train, hurtles down the track with its passengers, vanishing in the thick, black smoke.

???：In the train, the elderly tremble in the corner, and the infants babble dreaming ...

???：Ah, but the only young man aboard is filling coal into the furnace. Meanwhile, another train is hurtling down the same track, puffing the same black smoke ...

???：Alas. The two trains are destined to collide, in "a war to end all wars."

???：Pay close attention. You'll see the banner reaching out above their train, and the bloodstains on it.

???：Next, let's turn to where the train departed, to find the source of everything.

???：Rewind the clock to before the whistle blew, before the furnace ignited ...

???：And then, child, look somewhere far from here—in the Schönbrunn Palace, where the fine gentlemen of Austria-Hungary eagerly plan the extension of their national borders.

???：But the Habsburgs value their "honor" so much that first they must find themselves some excuse to cross that border.

???：Yet in another land, a humiliated people were gathering in secret. Their blood as hot as fire, ready to burn anyone and anywhere it was splattered.

???：Fate drove an open-topped car there, carrying an ill-fated couple. Their presence a crushing indignity that could not be endured.

???：An avenging bullet was sent to them as a gift from a luck-struck patriot.

???：In a moment, each party was granted their long-sought excuse. Boots had their excuse to kick down their doors, and guns their excuse to fire.

???：A yearning furnace at last had its reason to burn, to steam toward its final destination.

???：My dear, it's not a story of hatred or anger. The footprints were laid down before you ever stepped forward into them.

???：Fresh-faced boys traded books and toys for rifles, and poets were buried in trenches, musicians deafened by explosions and the howls of death.

???：But those war-starters? Those who doodled their ambitions on maps while supping their dessert, would lose nothing in their gamble.

???：The dew-fed river would still flow, and the facades of their great palaces would never be chipped or rocked by the guns they set off.

???：Nothing stains their hands, save the cream from their cakes.

???：Perhaps ... We wait. Wait for a pouring rain to douse the fires, a flood that might sweep the armies, wagered like betting chips, from their maps.

???：Then perhaps that car will turn, and the fated dead will come back to life—But you know that will not be how the story ends.

### 100616 · 小径① 雨天的老朋友

> Trail ① A Rainy Day Friend

扶起伯爵的鼻子，捏出贵妇的耳朵。“大可丢掉雨衣和雨靴！”戴面具的朋友在门廊处热情地招呼你：“快来淋淋这场酣畅淋漓的雨吧！”

**Oh, Venus Most Divine**

Venus, Venus, when your golden blood cascades upon us,  
Passing through the shimmering azure, over ankles and veins, and supple thighs so pure,  
A hammer falls at the temple's core,  
You forget.  
Touching night with your foot's tip once more.

Oh, Venus, have you already left me?  
Oh, Venus, could you be no more?  
Applaud, applaud, applaud, and cheer! Fulfill that raucous roar!  
Dances not unpaced in the coffin's stiff embrace, for you, majestic and sublime, do offer meager praise.  
I follow you, Venus, follow your fingertips in a haze,  
Stepping onto the path back to better-sweet days.

Attempts have been made to control the variables in order to determine a body fluid environment that might render the reagent relatively stable.

Sample No. 023

Sucrose is indeed useful, but its product has not yet reached the expected stability. Attempts have been made to remove alcohol-like compounds from the reagent.

After dropping 25% Picrasma tree solution on the inner wrists of volunteers (pure humans), 3 volunteers developed itchy, swollen rashes, and 1 volunteer experienced skin ulceration. All recovered without further effects after treatment.

Sample No. 159-C

*What is safe for the human body has no effect on arcanists!

Don't even think about putting these things in human mouths! If you try to use this to pressure us for next year's funding, I'll slap this experiment notebook in your face!

### 100618 · Heavy Door

**In Distant Romania**

Five days have passed since the Mărțișor.

Grandma Jina received several amulets and gave them to Elena and me. Grandma Jina said that this amulet was for saying goodbye to winter and welcoming spring, but I saw some yellow powder mixed into the white hemp thread, I thought it might be daffodil powder, but Elena told me not to think too much—it couldn't have come from the cemetery.

Elena tied a knot on my amulet just like hers, so everyone would know we're sisters. She was so excited for Grandma Jina to see her handiwork that she didn't even take the time to read to me today—she just kept dragging me in front of Grandma Jina, but even now, as I write in my diary, Grandma Jina still hasn't said anything, and Elena has fallen asleep. Maybe she'll forget it tomorrow, but I'll remember it for a long time, because I want to tell her that we can't be sisters. The children in the orphanage don't have relatives. But I never said that.

There are exceptions, though, like if Elena and I are taken away by the same person, or the same couple, then we'll become sisters by law ...

The door of the orphanage is a very heavy door. I don't know how my parents managed to open it and send me inside, but maybe there are a lot of adults who can't open that door like me, so they couldn't come back to get their children. If someone knocks on the door and wants to come in, I'll run over to help them, and then I'll ask them to take me and Elena with them, and if possible, Andre as well ...

But it's okay if no one comes to knock on the door, because the three of us are together now.

### 100620 · A Warrior's Song

**I Never**

I never step forward.  
In solitude, I never step forward.  
I merely stay in place, swaying,  
A wooden oar.  
I never step forward.  
In joy, I never step forward.  
I wait until the waves wither to caress,  
The spine of a beast.  
I never step forward,  
In death, I never step forward,  
Until my sleeves, my shoes, myself,  
Sink to the bottom of the water, until the night comes.  
Until even the helmsman covers his mouth and nose.

—Then, I start to run.  
Picking up the oar, the spine, the helmsman's soul.  
I never, ever stand still.

▪ Unregistered arcanist, Isolde von Dittarsdorf

We will temporarily mobilize the "Razor" squad, a special operations squad stationed in Prague, to arrive in Vienna and assist the investigative team in any way possible to ensure their safety throughout the containment process.

St. Pavlov Foundation Headquarters

### 1070101 · 象征动物

> Animal Symbolism

???：Now, we've learned some manners in Apeiron, haven't we? Namely, to abstain from beans, never parcel off a loaf, and stay away from the white roosters on the road.

???：It's been so long since we last talked about these things. How time does fly! This rusty brain of mine is struggling to keep up ...

???：We should have waited until dawn to light the candles. The sun would have mistaken the day for night and delayed its appearance in the morning. That way, we would have had more time to talk.

???：Indeed. You possess a far greater understanding of the laws of nature than your ancestors. It's only natural that you wouldn't relate candlelight to sunrise. But, for the people of earlier times, there was a commonly understood connection between the two.

???：Did the trees not bear fruit after the Horn of Plenty was filled at the harvest festival? Did the field not wave with grain?

???：Does the lighting of a match not illuminate the moon?

???：It's human nature to try to understand the world. Of course, at that time mistakes were made, and some phenomena were attributed to the wrong causes; some theories were developed from false facts.

???：It was a time of symbols, you see.

???：The flower represented blessings and harvest, the ouroboros the eternal cycle, the hawk wit and astuteness, and the soil safety and protection.

???：Aside from nature, the circle symbolized protection, the triangle stability, and the triple helix ascendance, change, and the unity of mind, body, and soul.

???：As time went on, things became associated with one another, and more and more symbols were created.

???：You can see the cause and effect with your own eyes, dear. It's clear when something is related and when it isn't. If the outcome isn't what we expect, these symbols must be incorrect.

???：But it's not their physical form that's wrong. Physical objects never lie. It's the meaning and concept given to them that are wrong, you see.

???：Objects are just objects. A match is just a match, not the illuminator of the moon. An animal is just an animal; the circle and triangle are only shapes ...

???：... and "you" are just "you".

### 1070201 · "Storm" Shelter Notice

**"Storm" Shelter Notice**

Emergency notice—for your personal safety, please stop what you are doing and read this report in its entirety, unless your life is in immediate danger:

The Timekeeper has issued a 24-hour "Storm" warning. All investigators are to abandon their current tasks upon receiving this notice and return to headquarters to seek shelter.

"Storm" Shelter Precautions:

• We will prioritize space for the preservation of personal items that comply with regulations, but note that space at headquarters is limited—please do not bring unregistered personnel or personal items over 20 kilograms back to headquarters. Note that family members of investigators who are not members of the Foundation are also considered unaffiliated personnel.

• In special circumstances, such as having to bring unregistered personnel or extra items back to headquarters to protect the Foundation's core principles, please first contact the headquarters through the nearest device with sound functionality and a dial (must have a round movable part and sound, such as a car radio), and we will respond as soon as possible. For the Foundation's core principles, please refer to the Foundation Investigator's Code of Conduct, Chapters 1 through 3.

• Since the Storm Syndrome is currently irreversible, for the safety of the Foundation, returning personnel must undergo an inspection. High levels of infection will restrict your entry, and we will place you in a special isolation area until the "Storm" ends.

• If you are in a critical situation and require assistance, the various rescue methods outlined in the Field Mission Evacuation Instructions will remain in effect. Reasonable rescue requests that comply with regulations will be responded to immediately."

### 1070301 · Storm Syndrome Investigation Record

**Storm Syndrome Investigation Record**

The symptoms of the current Storm Syndrome can be roughly summarized as oil painting transformation and heightened delirium. Depending on the region, the severity of the Storm Syndrome also varies:

Vienna

Expressionistic oil painting transformation pervades the body uncontrollably. At the same time, emotional stability is severely impaired—irritability and abnormal behavior occur, including but not limited to:

• Using deadly force to correct pronunciation.¹

• Declaring oneself as an independent nation and founding a country by family unit.

• Identifying parties by the color of their oil paintings and persecuting people of different colors.

...

Athens

Oil painting transformation symptoms only appear on the face, with a few cases of recovery reported, specific reasons unknown. Aggressiveness increases, especially a strong destructive impulse toward olive trees.²

Democratic votes on whether to go to war have occurred several times in different squares, often splitting into two factions and starting a war during the vote.

Cairo

Oil painting transformation symptoms are limited to parts of the facial organs, with minor actual effects on the body, which can largely be ignored. The main mental effect is reflected in an increase in swearing.

The local military, severely affected, has begun attempting to convert the pyramids into war fortifications.

...

...

...

Based on feedback from investigators around the world, it has been tentatively concluded that the epicenter of this "Storm" is Vienna, and the closer the physical distance to the epicenter, the more severe the symptoms of the Storm Syndrome.

¹Example: German speakers with non-Austrian accents have been attacked by Austrians.

²To be more precise, this is an act of rebellion against peace.

### 1070501 · Decryption Team Personnel List

**Decryption Team Personnel List**

※ Ulrich—Approved

Reasons for selection: The most skilled cryptologist in Laplace, also the most brilliant expert in arcane cryptography. In addition, his energy consumption ratio is excellent, capable of high quality work for 72 hours with only two supplies needed. Recommended for selection as the leader of the decryption team.

Team leader's comments: This assessment of mine is incorrect; this record has since been updated to 360 hours. Nevertheless, I agreed.

※ Dawkins—Approved

Reasons for selection: Dawkins is an expert in linguistics. We need more of such people when the origin of incantations is unknown.

Team leader's comments: Dawkins is talented but lacks a sense of seriousness, as evidenced by his inappropriate jokes. Do not let him work alone—assign a serious staff member to supervise him, and also use a cold humor punishment device, setting the penalty at 3 pounds a joke.

※ Richard—Approved

Reasons for selection: Richard is an excellent theoretical mathematician and is able to provide us with advice from a mathematical perspective.

Team leader's comments: Richard is only cautious when solving math problems, but averages three collisions with table corners per day. He also needs a supervisor to prevent such accidents during experiments.

※ Matthew—Approved

Reasons for selection: Matthew has excellent decision-making skills. His fighting spirit will surely bring about important breakthroughs in our decryption work.

Team leader's comments: He's just too impulsive. I do not recommend letting him make any decisions, otherwise an explosion in the lab will be the least of our worries.

※ Williams—Approved

Reasons for selection: Williams has a meticulous thought process. He excels at finding loopholes that others cannot—perhaps he can assist Matthew and prevent him from making any egregious mistakes.

Team leader's comments: Williams is what we call an introvert. His penchant for discovery doesn't mean he'll be good at communicating his findings. He should be left alone to deal with verification.

※ Victor—Approved

Reasons for selection: Victor has a leapfrogging thinking pattern and can keep research from getting stuck.

Team leader's comments: Victor is good, sure, it's just that his ideas are sometimes incomprehensible. Do NOT let him team up with Dawkins—he will definitely end up paying 3 pounds for Dawkins to tell a joke. I can assure you, putting a weirdo and a dark humor fanatic together will cause us problems.

※ Adler—Pending

Reasons for selection: Adler's recommendation is not only because he is an excellent cryptologist, but also because this decryption is not just a task for arcanists—we need opinions from humans as well.

Team leader's comments: I have my reservations. It's not that the team doesn't need humans, but it shouldn't be a silent enigma who hasn't been seen in public for eight years.

### 1070601 · 用火安全

> Fire Safety

???：Be ever so careful, dear. The fiery thirst for knowledge is blazing bright within you; be careful not to put it out.

???：Once, many fire thieves sat here, sipping coffee, as they tried to decipher the best angle to hold up their reeds to jam the burning wheel of Helios and steal his flame.

???：The gods are selfish. They're stingy with their might.

???：That's easy to say when the power's not yours to give. Knowing how to share is a rare virtue. Knowing how to share knowledge is a rarer virtue still.

???："How can we transfer our wisdom into mortal minds?" The gods pondered.

???：Can inspiration be duplicated from one brain to another? It comes and goes like a gust of wind, so what can be done?

???：True. I'm sure the early customers of the bakery share the same philosophy.

???：The line must be respected, however hungry the latecomers may be.

???：There are many fire thieves in the field of science—many more than the bread buyers in the bakery. Take a look at these geeks in uniforms made of aerospace materials.

???：Among them are arcanists in love with science who spend most of their time studying diodes and machines, and humans who are passionate about the future, always finding new inspirations ...

???：Elements, lightning, atoms ... Perhaps you've forgotten their counterparts in arcanum, but you must be familiar with them as scientific discoveries.

???：These discoveries are the milestones of progress, sweetheart. The fire thieves firmly believed that they would be able to bring about many more technical revolutions: the fourth, the fifth, the 65535th ...

???：Not yet, darling. We've only finished the prologue. However, there is a "but" waiting down the road.

???：A sudden rain dampened people's enthusiasm for the future. The floppy disks and many clever minds perished in it.

???：Some of the fire thieves grieved the loss. They screamed and swore to retrieve what was lost.

???：What about the rest of them?

???：They returned to primitive chaos, like coelacanths crawling back into the ocean after thousands of years of evolution. Once again, they became the proteges of arcanum.

???：Perhaps they made the right choice by leaving that useless science behind. Who knows?

???：The story of the fire thieves has only gone this far, darling. At this point, no one knows what will happen to Prometheus or what's inside Pandora's box.

Conway Automaton V, also known as LifeGame V. ### 1071101 · 问与答

> Q&A

???：Here is a well known question you must have heard: "What goes on four legs in the morning, two in the afternoon, and three in the evening?" it's so well "known" that no one finds it novel anymore.

???：But please do me a favor and answer me yourself.

???：Correct! This is the same answer Oedipus gave to defeat Sphinx. Thus becoming a hero, but also starting him on his ill-fated destiny.

???：Raise a question, give an answer. This is the most fundamental ritual. Through this, we get nearer to God.

???：The Sphinx, an embodiment of mercy and nobility in Egypt, was seen as a mystery to the Greeks: a dangerous being that kept secrets, and delivered the punishments of the gods.

???：When the question is answered, the ritual is finished, and the answerer is granted the right to reach the treasure it guards.

???：Yes, a ritual. Whether it is by pouring out wine; slaughtering a calf; or circling round a fire with a baby in your arms... It is an agreement; a question that demands an answer.

???：Every desire has a price. Whether it is paid in wine, blood, or fire, but sometimes that sacrifice is taken from you without it ever being noticed.

???：And the treasure you seek might be the undoing of your own desires.

???：Let us take a look at another question: "Stranger, what's your name?" Cried Polyphemus.

???："Nobody!" Odysseus answered, and it allowed him to flee the cyclops' cave, safe and sound.

???：A human being and nobody, two answers that could not be more alike, yet both were bound to fate by their answer.

???：Alright, dear, time to call it a day. As for you? You'll get the answers you seek, you've already paid the price.
