### 1071201 · 酿酒时节

> Wine Brewing Season

一段不知来处的诗句，记录于某只卷轴之上。

**Wine Brewing Season**

—Bang!  
In the distance, something falls.  
The cup drains the wine,  
Tired eyes dry out the sun.  
Everyone clings to the remaining days,  
The body speaks to its guest.

Who builds, also uses the same hand  
To destroy, to peel open the rock walls,  
The scar is a perfect circle.  
A death tied with a knot, thus  
—The end.

Only it remains:  
The wilderness.  
Everything extraneous, swarming in,  
Just like that, they talk about a bottomed sky.

### 1071301 · 七年的霉运

> Seven Years of Bad Luck

???：Look at this mirror, dear. There's a sizable stain on it.

???：Yes, it is. Maybe its owner meant to break it so that the stain would be thoroughly removed.

???：Now the stain is gone, and the owner has a collection of little mirrors. She must have accidentally left this one behind.

???：Look closer, dear. You can no longer see the part that has broken off, but you can see where it once was. Right there, a sizable stain.

???：The owner thought the stain would be removed if she smashed the mirror. Now she has a collection of little mirrors, and the stain has gone into hiding.

???：A lady with great ambitions and an unconscious charm. A lady who wishes to make the rest of the world accept who she is, and has suggested that many others join her way of thinking.

???：Her brother, on the contrary, hopes to be taken in. In his attempt to act like everyone else, he lost an arm in a duel.

???：If you ask me, dear, I'd say they're very alike. Equally lonely.

???：The brother and sister are a mirror image of each other. She's often repulsed by the sight of him, unable to reconcile herself with all that he reminds her of.

???：Now, the brother has found his missing arm, along with his long-lost dignity. The surgery that the sister had been hoping would be done on this world has finally started. But all of a sudden, she's terrified of the incisions.

???：As I said, despite their differences in approach, the brother and sister are mirror images of one another.

???：But now the mirror is broken. I hope she can make it through the seven years of bad luck. Poor thing. There's no way around it.

### 1071501 · Diagnosis Record - Awakened

**Diagnosis Record - Awakened**

Diagnostic Subject: Lucy

Examination Items: X-ray scanning, visualization, fluoroscopy, magnetic induction check, intelligence checks for age groups 6 to 60, including vision and hearing checks ...

Clinical Manifestation: The subject's core briefly lost consciousness after channeling an incantation, then returned to normal. Considering the replaceability of the external shell, the damage is negligible. Only changes to the core are described below:

Basically unchanged.

The subject's core remains stable, with no apparent external injuries, nor any internal damage found. All examination indices are normal. It is speculated that the subject's external shell bore the brunt of the curse, and that the subject's spirit is greater than the material of the vessel—according to one of the current mainstream views, the consciousness of the awakened comes from some still unknown macro-arcanum phenomenon. Perhaps it is such arcane phenomena that override the side effects of the incantation, helping to overcome the curse directly affecting the core, so that the target does not die from the curse while the consciousness remains.

Final Diagnosis: The subject's suggestion to further verify the side effects of the curse by using herself was agreed upon. However, according to past records, the awakened are not forever indestructible, so observation must be maintained, and the experiment must be terminated immediately if symptoms such as core degradation or mental abnormalities occur.

### 1071701 · Encrypted Record #832

**Encrypted Record #832**

"Regarding Zeno's request for assistance in establishing a dedicated communication line with the Timekeeper, we hope to understand the details."

"The exact content of the communication is a military secret. I cannot divulge the details."

"The Scientific Computing Center needs to understand the purpose of Zeno's establishment of communication to judge whether it is worth diverting manpower to build a dedicated line."

"Zeno's purpose is a military secret. I cannot divulge the details."

" ... Next, I will ask some simple questions. If you continue to answer with 'I cannot divulge the details,' I will take it as a sign of admission. Do you understand?"

"I am not authorized to answer that question."

" ... Very well, then is Zeno preparing for a military operation against the Manus Vindictae and therefore needs to establish communication with the Timekeeper for this purpose?"

"I cannot divulge the details."

"Good, this operation will ensure the Timekeeper's safety then, won't it?"

"I cannot divulge the details."

"This is an unconventional operation, isn't it?"

"I cannot divulge the details."

"This operation was most likely inspired by the Pax Security Council, correct?"

"We are soldiers. Soldiers act on orders, miss."

*Zeno then hung up the call.

### 1072001 · 林中的行迹

> A Trail in the Forest

命运的预兆隐藏在字里行间，读吧，一个声音说，选择的时刻已至。

**A Trail in the Forest**

A fate has been forged for this purpose,  
A maxim, a preconception,  
A deliberate contemplation, a step taken.  
Believed to be a choice.

The woodland is older than all within it.  
Through passion, or reason,  
Travelers grope and stumble through the branches,  
Or silently tread the narrow path,  
Or choose to turn away,  
Stepping into the subtle unknown.

Read, the voice urges.  
If now, without pursuit, close to truth,  
Yet let go.  
Then never before had it been.

Step forward. So they say.  
Before the tale is told, all is decided,  
Merchants, competitors, mortals, and heroes.  
And as the feast begins,  
How shall they fade away.

And those outside,  
For whom the time they've made has not yet come,  
Those who deem themselves transcendent,  
Are merely not in this procession.

### 1072101 · 子弹与微积分

> Bullets and Calculus

???：A bullet is among us, right under this roof. It serves no army, no country, only peace.

???：The heavy rain at the end of the century dissolved and washed away many of its comrades, but it was lucky enough to survive. Its pointy end still shines silver, ready to drill a hole into its enemy.

???：Sadly, its bulky colleague stole its thunder in this war. One push of the button, one big explosion. There was no need for the rest of the ammunition.

???：It seems someone has become more budget-conscious after working with the mathematicians.

???：Saving ten bullets is better than saving one. Saving a hundred bullets is better than saving ten. It's simple math.

???：Certainly, who doesn't want a life without the need to work? Few would hesitate to choose a life of freedom over the achievement and satisfaction of a job.

???：Given the short lifespan of a bullet, why would it choose anything else?

???：But its boss, the general, has more to think about. What are they going to do next time? Will this bullet be put to use when the rain comes again?

???：A big shot, dear. A wise man of the most insightful kind. You should talk to him if you have the chance.

???：But he's been in a gloomy mood lately. Be careful not to mention anything related to the conservative battle tactics used in 1999, the missile that would have been launched, and especially anything about the bullets lost in that battle.

???：He has paid a great price for the future, but you see, the future is sneaky.

???：Every time you think you can grasp it, it takes a step away from you, nullifying every effort you've made to get near it. Even a bullet isn't fast enough to catch up.

???：Pursuing the future. How romantic, dear. As long as we continue to close the gap between us and the future, it will eventually be reduced to zero.

???：But know that, as time goes by, we are only moving slower and slower in the race. Technology is regressing, and we have switched from AKs to Mosin-Nagants. The bullets won't be as fast as they used to be. The future is drifting away from us, and the past is catching up.

???：Shhhh ... Don't say such things out loud. Especially not near the general, sweetheart.

???：The bullets have paid a great price in order to find their way back to the future. If they hear you say they won't be needed anymore, you'll end up with quite the ammunition bill.

???：Alright, let's leave this bullet to itself. Solitude may help alleviate its anxiety about the future.

### 1072401 · Applying Chladni Plate Sound Propagation

**Applying Chladni Plate Sound Propagation**

Required Materials:

A square sheet of metal

Other shapes and materials can also be used, observing the differences they produce is equally entertaining.

Sand

Flour, salt, or sugar can also be used, but be careful, they're not easy to clean once they get into those body crevices.

An element of sound

Yes, that's me, glad to help. You can also find any friend who knows how to sing and make vibrations, or say, dance.

Working Principles:

It is known that sound is produced by vibration and propagates in the form of waves, and different sounds have different frequencies of vibration, so certain sounds can produce fixed waves under certain conditions, thereby representing certain patterns.

Operating Steps:

First, place the square metal sheet, hereinafter referred to as the plate, on a stable and well-fixed smooth solid surface.

This is to prevent phenomena such as slipping, an important step.

Second, the sound element is made to contact the plate and emit sound at a specific frequency.

The Scientific Computing Center has compiled the wavebands corresponding to the 26 letter shapes—you can ask Mr. Ulrich for a copy, he is a very generous gentleman.

Third, observe the pattern and draw conclusions.

Wasn't that simple? You can do it alone at home—try it and see what pattern your favorite music program turns into.

### 1072601 · Surveillance Report on Ms. Weyerhaeuser

**Surveillance Report on Ms. Weyerhaeuser**

Following the forest incident, Ms. Weyerhaeuser, suspected of using her arcane skills to destroy and invade on public places, and thus causing severe damage, was required to eliminate the influence of arcanum and restore the original function of the site, and was forced to participate in one hundred and twenty hours of Foundation service activities, in addition to a fine of two hundred thousand Sharpodonties.

Ms. Weyerhaeuser showed a high degree of cooperation in accepting her punishment. She enrolled in the Temporary Tree Processing class and, within three days, dealt with the excessive tree overgrowth she had caused by trimming all the greenery within the Foundation—at her impassioned request, the materials from the processed trees will be fully utilized. In the following period, the Tree Processing Class responded to this request—we processed the trees into lumber and sent them to the engineering department. The by-products were processed into organic fertilizer, and the remaining fruits were distributed by the canteen at lunchtime to offset Ms. Weyerhaeuser's service activity time—the acorn bread and berry pies received quite good reviews.

In summary, given Ms. Weyerhaeuser's excellent performance and good attitude in admitting her mistakes during this period, we believe she has fulfilled her compensatory obligations to our satisfaction, and it is recommended that surveillance and movement restrictions are lifted from Ms. Weyerhaeuser in advance. That is all.

### 1072602 · The Last Big Newsflash!

**The Last Big Newsflash!**

I don't know if I'm crazy, maybe today is the end of the world, but I have to write this down—this is my duty as a journalist!

The whole of Vienna has become an oil painting splashed with turpentine, and I'm not using that as a metaphor—literally!

Everything is dripping with paint, and I'm no exception! Fortunately, my right hand is fine for now and I can still write.

I thought three men fighting to the death over the pronunciation of a word would be enough to make the front page of the newspapers, but I was wrong—▊▊▊, a great war is unfolding! A great war that will involve the whole world! Those feet marching on the streets can't wait to throw the world into the roasting flames of hell! Hungarians, Austrians, Transleithanians, Germans, Cisleithanians, the streets and cafés are full of lunatics talking nonsense, except for me and the little girl who just came to make a phone call carrying a case of books. The girl was crying so sadly when she made the call, she ran so fast when she came that she even slipped and fell, and she didn't realize her books had fallen all over the place until after she finished the call. I don't understand what there is to cry about when everything has become like this everywhere, and any semblance of order will soon disappear. If there is anything that still makes you sad, then you should do everything in your power to destroy it, just like the editor who keeps rejecting my drafts—if I don't take him down, I'll never have my chance to shine.

This article will appear in tomorrow's paper, along with the destruction of my editor, if the world hasn't ended by then.

### 1072701 · 绳结的艺术

> The Art of Knots

???：My dear child, look at you, you're exhausted. I couldn't be of help then, but it's ok, the "party" is over now. At least let me brush your hair, darling.

???：Or your hair will get all knotted, and it will hurt you when you try to untangle them.

???：I'm not surprised. An old friend of ours, "knot." Often we learn our knots before we even know how to read and write.

???：A knot could be an obstacle that breaks the consistency of a flow, and also a reminder of forgotten things.

???：You can use just about anything on hand to make a knot: leaves of grass, the fur of an animal, even fallen hair ... But the sturdiest knots are tied with rope.

???：Knots are to be found in many stories. Signify promises, counting and measuring, an instrument of salvation and one of execution.

???：Oh, I could go on all day! The influence and power of knots can't be overstated...

???：It is said that it was in the cutting of the Gordian Knot that Alexander became "the Great"; just touching the meters-long rope provides blessings to the faithful in Belem; and tiny knots on strings can carry the history of an entire people.

???：The ancient Inca called it "Quipu." The intricate variations of hundreds of fabrics, colors, and lengths of strings were used to record their stories through the ages. And to think, Shakespeare only had 26 letters to create his masterpieces.

???：Just look around you: don't sailors use knots to mark depths and mend sails; or coachmen tying knots to rein their horses; and any respectable lady or gentleman knows the knots they must tie about their necks to stay in fashion.

???：Or consider the knot-like helix structure of DNA, and the changes in states of matter ... Invisible to the naked eye, these knots carry some hint at a universal law.

???：That there might be a string, like the Quipu, an endless rope that records in its knots everything in the world. One that can't be seen, yet there it is, everywhere, all at once.

???：Maybe you can tie this knot yourself. Use your hair, then form a loop, pass the end through it, and tighten it up. There you go.

???：I hope you keep this knot as a record of the words we shared here.

### 1080101 · Instructions in Advance

**I Am Venturing Out!**

Greetings!

By the time you read this letter, I will already be on an auto island far out at sea. Oh! Please don't misunderstand, I adore your company, especially the hallway walking parties at 3 a.m. Though I do love all parties, walking parties are a particular treat, but unfortunately, The Lady's Code of Conduct dictate that I should be sound asleep by 9 p.m. What a pity.

Also, please tell Ms. Moissan not to worry! I'm not adventuring alone; miss Vertin is also going on a trip to the legendary El Dorado. The City of Gold! Magic, gold, and even bouncing coffee beans! I can't wait to talk to the locals about potential invigorating potion recipes!

Speaking of invigorating potions, now everyone can become a brewing master! All it takes is a cup of coffee, and before drinking, add three grams of gold, and voilà, you've got an invigorating potion! I've already prepared the ingredients—the gold is on the table! And thanks to the automatic coffee drip-beast in the lounge, we're saved from the hardest part—making coffee!

And do look forward to the special gifts I'll soon bring back!

Sincerely,

Sotheby

> The Red-Bellied Piranhas

???：So, things went sideways. That's what happens when you put guns in the hands of amateurs.

???：But you handled the water better this time. You controlled your breathing, kept your airways clear, and this time you remembered to lie back and float.

???：Does it matter? I'm all you've got. We're at war—bullets flying, wounds festering, typhoid, cholera, and all the worst of it. It's a living hell, and only fools think there's any honor in it.

???：You never expect to see a familiar face again, not in times like these.

???：Now I need you to listen. There's something you should know.

???：The raid on the river was a ploy, and its mastermind is an old acquaintance. As for the ones who carried it out—you already heard about them this morning.

???：They promised their followers they'd push south, and they did. With their supplies secured by a mysterious third party, they paraded through every town along the way, playing as "liberators" and taking out anyone they decided were their enemies.

???：They swelled in numbers, larger than any local gang or cult had ever gotten before.

???：Now they swarm across the land like a school of red-bellied piranhas. Always hungry, ready to tear apart anything that crosses their path.

???：Chances are they're doing fine, just like you.

???：Would've been nice if you caught what that little executioner and that "gold-hearted" gambler were talking about during their dice game. Guess you'll have to ask her later.

???：Listen, I'm not here to run your errands or play your messenger boy, so ask nicely.

???：There's nothing more I need to tell you. For now.

### 1080701 · 自救与拯救

> Who is the Savior?

???：This place was once known for its inexhaustible mines, the riches of its rainforests, and its brave and passionate people. But now, the first two are gone.

???：Chaos and power struggles loom over the younger generation, while the rich and well-connected sit atop their piles of corpses and rubble and feast. Dining off golden plates with silver spoons, turning their noses up from the dogs at their feet.

???：But that gold has lost its shine and their silver spoons have tarnished, everyone can see them for what they really are. So, what now? Either the people save themselves or turn their fate over to the hands of the so-called "savior." What would you do, if it were you?

???：That's a smart choice. Now that the carnival's over, people have to deal with more than just their hangovers—they must figure out how to save their own skins.

???：Most though will just run around like chickens with their heads cut off, begging for their pain to be taken away. But all they'll get is another morphine shot—nothing close to a real solution.

???：Fear spreads like a disease. Until an entire generation is out on the streets, hoping to find anything that will lift them out of their misery.

???：Who's asking the questions here, me or you? But fine, I'll clue you in a little about this "savior".

???：I once met a kid from Buenos Aires who told me about the "hand of god" that gave them the World Cup. He saw it as proof there was a higher power, something men should follow and seek protection from.

???：Maybe he had a point, I don't know. Maybe there's truly something greater out there, or maybe it's just what we mortals need to believe.

???：These men from the Apostles Brotherhood used to be regular church-goers just like anybody else. But faced with an opportunity to rise above their own cruel realities, they all too readily turned away.

???：They left their old altars and took communion with a new god. Will this new path save them? Is it worth all their efforts? I can't say.

???：But you wanted to know if this 'savior' can save its own. Well, I guess you've got your answer now.

???：No need to start panicking just yet. Things are still under control. Your lady friend has stepped up to solve this mess, and the veteran knows what he's doing.

???：This city is unlike any you've seen before. You must learn its secrets to survive—remember which street kids are from which alley and what intel they can be relied on for, learn the dates when the dealers bring their "medicine" into port, and figure out which water tanks hide the key to the local gang's safehouse.

???：Don't look at me for answers. It's none of my business.

### 1080801 · Interview Guidelines

**Encrypted Record#5225**

"Ms. Barbara, thank you for agreeing to this conversation."

"Please relax, this is the Foundation's way of reciprocating UTTU's interview. We can proceed at your leisure."

"Yes, it's rare to be the one asking the questions and hosting the editor of UTTU. Let's make the most of this opportunity. Regarding your method of avoiding the 'Storm' ... "

"I'm sorry, but as you probably know, you cannot hear the wind, so you can't hear the secrets of the 'Storm'. There's nothing I can do about that."

"UTTU shows no interest in cooperating on the 'Storm' issue. I'll note it down like this, for the record."

"That doesn't sound like an amicable record, but maybe that's how it's perceived by you."

"Let's move to the next question. From what we understand, UTTU has a unique communication method. Can it help you locate each other? Does it work for ordinary contributors?"

"Only UTTU members can hear the wind, but I can ask about the source location of the contributor's package for you ..."

...

"Ms. Barbara?"

"Sorry ... the wind just told me something unrelated. I need to head to Texas now."

"But our conversation isn't over ... "

"I apologize, but I must go to Highway 77 immediately. Please allow me to end the call."

*The other party then hung up.

**Matilda has been appointed as a special investigator to continue our interview on Highway 77.

### 1080901 · Innovative Advertising

**The Untold Stories of the Invisible College—Boyle's Impossible Flask**

Perhaps you are familiar with the Royal Society, maybe you're even able to name every president. But do you know its predecessor—the mysterious scholar's assembly, known as the Invisible College? Today, we will introduce the central figure of the Invisible College, Robert Boyle, the famous alchemist, natural philosopher, and founder of modern chemistry, along with his mystical stories.

When discussing Boyle, one must mention Boyle's Law, which states that at constant temperature, the pressure of a gas is inversely proportional to its volume. Boyle demonstrated this with a U-shaped tube filled with mercury. But before that, Boyle tried various devices and countless designs in his quest for a frictionless flask with a piston. He even employed arcane skills, casting an incantation of proportional scaling into a flask. As a result, the flask could indeed compress and expand objects perfectly. However, arcane skills being what they are, the object's mass would shrink with its volume, and the process would cause noticeable temperature changes. A very successful arcane skill, but a failed experimental apparatus.

These flasks were eventually donated to the Royal Society, but it's said some ended up in private hands. So, keep an eye on the ordinary flasks around you—one of them might just be a highly convenient "Impossible Flask."

Below is a recording of the interrogation of a member of the Order of Enlightenment that occurred in San Francisco:

"Next, you will need to answer a few simple questions. This 'Order of Enlightenment' is just another front for Manus Vindictae, correct?"

"Ask whatever you like, I ain't gonna tell you anything, Foundation lapdog!"

"Please calm down. This doesn't have to be a hostile interrogation, but your answers here will influence our assessment of you. You know Arcana is dead, right?"

"Ignorant fool! The Guiding One's fate is always in her own hands! She chose a temporary death to suffer for us. One day, she will return, like an angel, and we will be reunited. We will once again become one, returning to that glorious age!"

"I have no intention of arguing with you. You're sweating profusely—do you need some water?"

"I won't drink anything you give me!!!"

"Alright, let's finish this quickly so you can return to your room and rest. Can you tell me who your current leader is? Is it 'Forget Me Not'? Or the former School of Apeiron arcanist Sophia? Or someone else?"

### 1081401 · 牌桌礼仪

> Gambling Etiquette

???：A gambling game's been set up in a place unfamiliar to you. Soon, you'll be invited to join the table as well.

???：Hmph ... you seem to have grown quite assertive. But remember this: as a newcomer to the table, you have no privilege to make demands or probe for information unless you're looking to be targeted and isolated.

???：You have to lose a few rounds—pay your "entry fee." Only then can you start to learn the secrets of those at the table. The players' backgrounds, the dealer's habits, and the truth behind the highest-stakes gambler.

???：It's baffling to me. Why would anyone travel halfway across the world just to throw away their money?

???：That was a rhetorical question. But thanks for answering.

???：You're right; the allure of winning is irresistible, even in a game of chance. People blow on the dice, clutch a rabbit's foot, or murmur a prayer, foolishly believing they can sway the outcome.

???：How deluded. It's pathetic.

???：Tsk, disappointing indeed. To think you're entangled in such empty words.

???：Some are so captivated by these grandiose phrases that they feel empowered when using them. Despite their vapidity, it makes them feel important.

???：Ha, hypocritical. Delusional, even.

???：Oh, quite the opposite. The odds are rarely equal. Your opponents could have any number of advantages stuffed up their sleeves—a larger stack of chips, easier winning conditions, or even the power to change the rules. Want a fair game? You've got to fight for it, by any means necessary.

???：The white suits always play it safe, carefully hedging their bets, while the black robes plot to overturn the table.

???：Clever? More like cretinous and atrocious.

???：Once the table's flipped, there'll be nothing but chaos. Who would bother to set it right again? No one.

### 1081501 · 三声敲响

> Good News and Bad News

???：All it takes is one lie to set off a chain reaction, and I'm afraid the butterfly effect has reached you. I have bad news. You've been betrayed.

???：But there's good news, too: a certain flight lieutenant appears to be more loyal to you than to the organization she swore to serve.

???：You know him—the admiral who was pitted against an enemy he couldn't possibly defeat. This conflict has left an incurable ulcer in his once lofty heart.

???：Yet, as a leader, he must remain unyielding in both body and mind.

???：Despite his continued victories on the front lines, he made a decision so counterintuitive that even his loyal adjutant found it questionable.

???：The top brass always sees things differently, I suppose.

???：You must have heard the story of the Trojan Horse, right?

???：Greek soldiers sneaked past the walls of Troy inside a wooden horse. One Trojan priest tried to warn his people, but was executed by the gods themselves for doing so. Soon after, all the leaders of Troy were killed by the hidden invaders.

???：Being killed by your enemy is bad; being killed by your ally is worse. It was an honorless way to go.

???：Don't get your hopes up. He's more like a desperate patient than anything. Weary from the pain of his ulcer, and frantically searching for a way out.

???：In his desperation, he has clutched at an opportunity presented by none other than the Preacher herself.

???：Deep down, he knows he's backed himself into a corner.

???：Great. I'm done reading reports anyway.

???：I look forward to your wise decisions. We need some good news for a change.

### 1082101 · This is Not a Drill

**Special Emergency Notice**

The headquarters has informed all branches that the Brazilian branch has experienced another mutiny, though specific details remain unclear. All branches are ordered, effective immediately, to recall all units and temporarily detain any personnel who have served in Brazil or were under the command of any officers from the Brazilian branch. These individuals are to remain in custody until a special agent from the headquarters arrives for inspection. Noncompliance will be treated as support for the traitors.

The mastermind behind this mutiny remains unidentified, and discussion of the matter is strictly prohibited.

These successive mutinies in the Brazilian branch in such a short time are a great dishonor for us and represent the most severe loss Zeno has suffered in recent years. However, we stand resilient in the face of this loss, all is still under control. No sacrifice will stop us from moving forward, and all traitors will pay the price for their actions.

*Only brigadier generals and above are permitted to view the following message:

We all know what Igor has done, but now may not be the most opportune time to confront our former war comrade with anger and vengeance.

### 1082201 · 远见与筹划

> A Minute to Spare

???：I have some news you'll be happy to hear.

???：Trust me, you'll wish you had more than a minute for this. It involves a mutual friend of ours—a long-lost acquaintance you've been searching for.

???：Doctor, writer ... she goes by many titles, just like you and I. Sadly, in her current state, her brain capacity is limited.

???：It's just like after that fateful battle ended. Few survived to remember her awe-inspiring feats.

???：Now, this kind-hearted doctor has become a crucial part of another wicked scheme of which our dear admiral is well aware.

???：An orchestra without a conductor can barely play a symphony. Recognizing that missing, vital piece, the admiral seized the opportunity without hesitation.

???：Experience gives a veteran his scars, yes, but also a scrap of wisdom. He knew he should gain the upper hand in this one-sided gamble.

???：I don't know yet; there's no mention of it in the reports. I do have some speculations, however.

???：Those troublemakers have been focused on nothing but cultivating chaos and fanning the flames of conflict, paying little attention to the scale and extent of their intended retribution.

???：Now, it seems they've realized the uncertainty of this approach—drastic, volatile retribution won't get them anywhere. To reach their destination, they must forge a new path, even if it's more arduous and perilous than the last.

???：Your minute's up. Time certainly does fly, doesn't it?

???：I'll be back when there's a new development. Stay alive if you can. I'd like to keep reading reports for you.

"151134122, 167148135, 186166150, 203181163, 180161145, 163145132!"

"What are you talking about?"

"166148134, 208185168, 221197177, 197176158, 155138126, 141125114!"

"203180163, 185165149, 175156141, 164146132, 136122111, 138124113!"

"Has John Titor infected you? Speak in human language or we're leaving."

"The gibberish on these papers might be a treasure map! You have to help me crack it. Where's Ulrich?"

"Probably standing in line to buy a ship ticket to Australia. He won't be back for a while. Where did you get this?"

"There was a box of punch cards outside the computer room, I tossed them all into the decoder."

"You should stop wasting your time, those might be Ulrich's discarded scribbles for all we know."

### 1090101 · 回环的幽灵

> The Circling Ghost

???：The moment armies start to march, the world stands still.

???：You fight or fall back, charge forward, or run. Those are the only rules on the battlefield.

???：I don't see a start time on this report, nor does it state the reason for or the demands of this war.

???：That's what you think. The scars left on the land may be the most obvious form of destruction, but the true devastation of war is one that develops gradually in the souls of people.

???：Ah, a literary war. Indeed, the violence enacted upon words is often just as devastating as an order on a battlefield.

???：Words must fulfill their duties to their creators, just as soldiers must answer to their commanders.

???：You don't? Hmm. The young writers have been exiled—sent down a path with no signs to follow and no destination.

???：In dark days of bloody conflict, soldiers often find comfort in scriptures and verses—or in letters from their loved ones.

???：They hold those words close—a fragile piece of home—a brief solace from the roaring of the battlefield.

???：But, as they fall one by one, they learn the bitter truth. No destiny awaits them but death, that bitter homesickness still weighing heavy in their hearts.

???：In dreams? Maybe. Walking the streets of your hometown, spending a lazy afternoon at the writing desk—dreams can take you anywhere.

???：But live there too long, and the lines between fiction and reality will blur, and soon you'll never wake up again.

???：Soldiers crawl through trenches, roll in the mud, and charge to their deaths again and again ...

???：... while words are forever trapped within the confines of poetry, made to live, die, and live again under the golden prophecy ...

???：Ugh. I can't stand the way poets talk.

### 1090103 · The Ushuaia Port Explosion

**The Ushuaia Port Explosion**

The tremors from the explosion at Ushuaia Port shook the surroundings for nearly 10 seconds, after which strong winds carried the flames across the dock area. Thick smoke spread for several kilometers. The explosion injured 12 people, over 200 residents were evacuated, and it took the navy and fire department nearly six hours to contain the fire. Unfortunately, key logistics facilities were reduced to ruins.

Investigations confirm that the explosions originated from three sources: an oil storage tank and two cargo warehouses along the third dock. However, the disproportionate scale of the explosions raises suspicions of arcane skill involvement. It is still unclear whether this incident is related to the Zeno mutiny, and simultaneous investigations are ongoing into other arcanist organizations that may be responsible.

What the hell is this?! You call this a report? This is all you're handing in? Panic is already spreading—do I need to remind you that Ushuaia is our nearest deep-water port? Flames higher than the lighthouse, seawater dyed blood-red—this catastrophe is bringing up painful memories of the war from over a decade ago! Our wounds are being torn open in the worst way possible, and after all this time I gave you to investigate, you're just giving me a list of possibilities?

A few days ago, people suspected those lunatics locked up in prison—but what the hell can they even do besides bash their heads against barbed wire and spout their pathetic rhetoric? Maybe they scribbled out a doppelgänger on a pile of backwards poetry, conjured up a few imaginary hot-air balloons to float them to the port, and then molded a ton of bombs from mud and ice? Fat chance! The only thing you got right was not including them in your list of suspects—otherwise, I'd have you thrown in that prison alongside them!

### 1090201 · Ushuaia, the End of the World

???：A gambling game's been set up in a place unfamiliar to you. Soon, you'll be invited to join the table as well.

???：Hmph ... you seem to have grown quite assertive. But remember this: as a newcomer to the table, you have no privilege to make demands or probe for information unless you're looking to be targeted and isolated.

???：You have to lose a few rounds—pay your "entry fee." Only then can you start to learn the secrets of those at the table. The players' backgrounds, the dealer's habits, and the truth behind the highest-stakes gambler.

???：It's baffling to me. Why would anyone travel halfway across the world just to throw away their money?

???：That was a rhetorical question. But thanks for answering.

???：You're right; the allure of winning is irresistible, even in a game of chance. People blow on the dice, clutch a rabbit's foot, or murmur a prayer, foolishly believing they can sway the outcome.

???：How deluded. It's pathetic.

???：Tsk, disappointing indeed. To think you're entangled in such empty words.

???：Some are so captivated by these grandiose phrases that they feel empowered when using them. Despite their vapidity, it makes them feel important.

???：Ha, hypocritical. Delusional, even.

???：Oh, quite the opposite. The odds are rarely equal. Your opponents could have any number of advantages stuffed up their sleeves—a larger stack of chips, easier winning conditions, or even the power to change the rules. Want a fair game? You've got to fight for it, by any means necessary.

???：The white suits always play it safe, carefully hedging their bets, while the black robes plot to overturn the table.

???：Clever? More like cretinous and atrocious.

???：Once the table's flipped, there'll be nothing but chaos. Who would bother to set it right again? No one.

### 1090402 · To the Pen Pal I Never Met

**To the Pen Pal I Never Met**

Aleph, my dear pen pal, enclosed is the latest revision of my novel. In your last letter, you suggested the role of a bank clerk, marking Amalfitano with the stamp of the capitalist production model—an excellent idea! In this revision, I have incorporated some of my own thoughts as well.

...

That is everything about my novel that I was impatient to share with you. Perhaps you won't mind me discussing something else? Throughout my creative journey, your encouragement and validation have been my greatest comfort when facing the countless closed doors of publishers. The long, futile efforts feel insignificant in the face of your understanding. Oh, my dear pen pal, I say this sincerely—though I suppose such explanations are unnecessary. Perhaps I do not need to waste more ink describing just how much your letters mean to me. So, I ask, with both sincerity and trepidation: may we speak in person—about the dead, the desert, fate, the ending that troubles me so much, and the tides of imagination and insight that carry us both?

Recoleta,

At the poetry gathering in Santafé de Bogotá's open-air theater, Colombia

Miss Recoleta, as I have mentioned several times in my letters—and as you may have already surmised—I reside in a prison. This is why I have never met my pen pals in person. Due to its remote location and cold climate, Ushuaia is not a place people readily visit. People would rather sip a hot drink in one of Buenos Aires's century-old cafés than travel such a distance.

...

Finally, I still wish you the best on your creative path.

Aleph,

At the Comala Prison in Ushuaia

### 1090501 · 南方也存在

> El Sur También Existe

???：Let's take a step back—pull you out of that whirlwind of passion and dreams.

???：A sudden explosion that shook Latin American literary circles about 30 years ago. It was destructive—but also gave rise to new life.

???：Lucky for us, we don't have to worry about shell shock in the literary world.

???：And here are our new "Columbuses," heading to the docks to embark on their journey.

???：Carmen and Carlos, the driving force behind this literary feast between Spain and Latin America.

???：Carmen, with an eye for opportunity and a mind for business, sought out the continent's literary gems and secured generous publishing deals for them.

???：Carlos, with his deep understanding of market dynamics, brought these works to the eyes of the European public.

???：Then? A golden era began. It all kicked off with a scandal at a military academy, and soon the entire world was captivated by the story of a baby with a pig's tail from a faraway land.

???：But we mustn't forget the writers themselves. Fuentes, a towering figure of the Spanish-speaking world, and Cortázar, the writer ever seeing through the eyes of a child.

???：And of course, Mario and Gabo—key players in this literary revolution—will forever be etched in the annals of history.

???：They corresponded regularly, always expressing deep admiration for each other's work. In fact, one even wrote a doctoral thesis on the other.

???：Ah, friendship.

???：It was.

???：But no matter how explosive the boom was, it couldn't escape its fleeting nature.

???：With the second imprisonment and self-criticism of a Cuban poet, in came an inexplicable right hook, and bang!—silence.

???：The military junta had gripped the poets' throats. The verse of the pioneers was cut off, their voices no longer heard.

???：The bond between two generations was broken, and for the younger of the two, the silence was intolerable.

???：They looked for a new theme, a new idea, a new voice—a new method to speak on their disappointment, their horror, and their anger.

???：And they became the new guerrillas.

### 1090602 · Typewriter Record #P5

**Typewriter Record #P5**

...

Even though they refuse to discuss the outside world, spending even a few days inside the prison makes clear that the reason they are here is precisely what they avoid speaking about.

Ushuaia is a cold town on the tip of Argentina's southernmost territory. The polar night, the frozen sea, the proximity to Antarctica, the endless winter—it all makes the place seem buried by time and forgotten. It possesses all the necessary qualities of a prison, making it an ideal place to send arcanist suspects diagnosed with mental illness, a place to be forgotten.

Setting aside the true definition of madness and their status as prisoners, what I see in this prison are former poets, writers, and social activists. In their era, some spoke of poetry—then disappeared from society for owning a book by Jean-Paul Sartre. Some incited grassroots rebellion against hierarchical structures—only to be exiled. They were repeatedly questioned, "Why do you read these books? Why do you write these poems?" They were branded "thought organizers" and eventually sent here to partake in an experiment conducted under the names of Foucault and psychoanalysis. It is nearly impossible to determine whether their "madness" was defined by society because they resisted order, or if it was induced through relentless electric shocks and torture. Or perhaps all of these reasons are one and the same. This reason is often avoided, sanitized, or ignored—because deep down, we all know how deeply it is woven into the fabric of all our lives.

...

At first, they said nothing, but this very act of deliberate avoidance was what truly conveyed their grief and fear.

### 1090801 · Typewriter Record #P12

**Typewriter Record #P12**

When they speak of "Manus Vindictae," their tone is always light, often laced with jokes—colorful descriptions like "a greasy-headed musician smearing tiles like a drunk Edward Goya" frequently crop up in their poetry. Even though I have never "seen" such a scene myself, I can still grasp their attitude toward Manus Vindictae—it was an oppression surpassing even their past sufferings. Yet, subconsciously, they "ignored" this similarity—perhaps out of denial, or perhaps because this prison had already taught them to obey, also subconsciously.

But as far as I know, Manus Vindictae did bring their terror here. Those masks drove arcanists to insanity and caused unbearable suffering, as if they were caught in an inescapable snare. After prolonged torment, they lost the ability to utter even a single phrase of their own language, reduced to slaves of this underground faction—left with only a singular thought, their souls having left the empty husk of their former selves.

And as for humans—it seems they were driven to death en masse. At night, they were not allowed prolonged sleep, woken by their companions every hour to sit beneath the watchtower and chant prayers. And by dawn, high-intensity training and interrogations that did not require or expect any answers would begin again ... The purpose of Manus Vindictae is buried beneath these cruel methods, as clear as the deepest crevasse in the ice field—it is nothing but destruction. They seek to "completely destroy" humanity.

1. Two days, eleven hours, eight minutes, and four seconds later, an eight-millimeter crack appeared on a bar of his cell, which is on the second floor, near the third staircase, and is the eighth cell from the left. He scraped off a four-by-three-millimeter fragment from the thirty-second tile.

2. Mentions of "the other me" increased from three times in 48 hours to twenty-five times in 48 hours.

3. Four days, three hours, seven minutes, and eight seconds later, he transitioned from merely collecting manuscript paper to collecting manuscript paper and mirror fragments. Within the next twenty-four hours, he collected eleven mirrors, which totaled one hundred and three shards.

...

> Gamble of Fate

### 1091201 · A Clipping

**A Clipping**

In 1935, Professor António from Portugal became intrigued by a medical study documenting how gorillas became docile after the removal of their frontal lobes. Inspired by this, he successfully performed the world's first human frontal lobotomy, known as leukotomy. The surgery was remarkably successful, paving the way for significant breakthroughs in research. In 1949, Professor António was awarded the Nobel Prize in Medicine for his work.

Through continuous practice and refinement, he and others eventually improved and perfected the procedure, even developing a specialized leukotome. However, the surgical technique required a high level of expertise, and as a result, it was never widely adopted.

In 1945, American doctor Walter Freeman modified the procedure, which became infamously known as the "ice pick lobotomy." The technique involved rendering patients unconscious with electroshock or anesthesia, and then hammering a steel pick—roughly the thickness of a chopstick—through the upper part of the eye socket into the brain. The pick was then manually rotated to destroy the frontal lobe.

In 1950, following strong recommendations from psychiatric pathologist Vasily Gayanovsky, the Soviet government became the first to announce a complete ban on frontal lobotomies. Over the next two decades, most countries, including the United States, followed suit and passed laws prohibiting the procedure.

### 1091402 · Draft of a Strange Incantation

**Draft of a Strange Incantation**

Regarding the ... (blurred by water stains) ritual:

1. A large number of Arcanists—this should be an easily achievable goal for Manus Vindictae. Young people capable of lifting bricks and setting sail are plentiful. Devotion remains the best catalyst.

2. Roar Lion's Tail, Sacred Scarab Wings, Nukutaeao Shell—when combined with the Fountain of Immortality, it seems likely that Manus Vindictae aims to achieve something extraordinary. Given the shell's properties, it can withstand an immense amount of energy, ensuring the ritual's success. This is indeed an incantation requiring considerable meticulous preparation.

3. The location ... (blurred by water stains)—remains an excellent choice, but for the ritual itself, timing is of paramount importance. The "time" referred to here is not our traditional understanding, but time "itself."

### 1091701 · 文学兵役

> Armed with Literature

???：Ahem ... Take a deep breath, soldier; the smoke's cleared now. This story—this "battle"—will soon come to an end.

???：A writer has departed from her domain, leaving her creations scattered behind her, like blood-soaked bandages and fragmented skulls in the trenches.

???：What a garbled mess.

???：Glad we're on the same page. Thank heavens my commander wasn't some kind of littérateur; else I'd have been blown to bits before I could decipher their poetic orders.

???：*whistle* How wonderful for you. You know what? You should be the leader of the Literature Encryption Communication Team. Just arm yourself with a dictionary, and even enemies with .45 Maxim machine guns won't stand a chance against you.

???：Because the poetry you recite will surely blow their minds before their bullets pierce yours.

???：Well, it's all thanks to a certain writer, the embodiment of the wandering poet and traveler from Santiago to Buenos Aires. She impressed me with her precious experience as a cafe dishwasher and campground caretaker.

???：What really rekindled my sense of humor was her seriousness about her literary pursuits. You'd understand what I mean if you'd ever heard a cheesy joke at a comrade's graveside.

???：Drawing strength from her wanderings and rebellious exploits, she attempted to change society, wielding the sword of literature as a revolutionary intellectual and a leader of thought.

???：Some would hail her as a battle angel of Latin America, with words as piercing as bullets and poems as powerful as tactical bombers.

???：Many share your doubt, myself included.

???：You stand firm in your beliefs, huh? Well, maybe that's a good thing. A commander has to trust their soldiers, or they'll be forced to surrender before long.

???：Here, come closer. Let me tell you a secret ...

???：Many—if not all—people foolishly think it's the executioner who drops the guillotine blade, the powder that fires the bullet.

???：While they're half right, they're substantially inaccurate. The craters on the battlefield aren't the result of bombs, but of clashing ideologies—a trigger pulled long before the powder ever ignited in the barrel.

???：As for literature ...

???：Well, it's probably best not to buy too much into the Latin American Boom. Forget about the status report from earlier.

???：That said, at least there's one thing that's certain:

???：Books can bite back—and they'll leave scars.

### 1091801 · 愚人船

> Ship of Fools

???：Look, it's sinking. Corpses float on the planks, and the fleeing prisoners are swimming toward the shore. Time to abandon ship.

???：One that was exiled to a desolate corner of the ocean by the resplendent civilized world.

???：It's both a prison and a sanitarium. Are any of your friends aboard? If so, you might find the following report a little offensive.

???："Dregs of society" ... "Human scum" ... These are the terms used to describe its eccentric passengers.

???：To be fair, they do lack discipline and independence.

???：But that may well be a response to the heavy pain they've endured.

???：Some theorists suggest that they're innocent victims, trapped in the confines between rationality and irrationality, defined and redefined by historians as they please.

???：But let's ditch the theories and simplify things: the moment the shackle called "madness" was fastened around their necks—whether they were truly mentally ill or not—their lives were forever scarred.

???：And now, the mad passengers are storming the shore.

???：I suppose it is. You can see the joy in their faces, even in the footprints they leave behind as they hop across the beach.

???：Yes, freedom is favorable, indeed. The freedom to squander our lives, to trample on the dignity of others, the freedom to embrace absurdity and meaninglessness.

???：But one can gain immunity through controlled infection—the disease can become its own vaccine.

???：Just beware of the ignorant commander who deifies a wandering lunatic in pursuit of a little knowledge.

???：Let's dig deeper—scavenge through the messy battlefield for dog tags—were these people born mad?

???：After watching the inhabitants of this earth tear each other apart in one war after another, I've concluded this: only knowledge drives people to madness; ignorance does not.

???：Madness is a side effect of the sedative that is "knowledge." As long as those on land continue to consume it, this ship will never be short on recruits.

???：This is a war between madness and civilization. Both sides have leveled accusations, spilled blood, and pummeled each other ...

???：... and soon, everyone will have leapt into the trenches and beaten and been beaten to a bloody pulp. Only a few can escape this fate.

???：Check your six.

???：The one leaning against the sunken ship, showing no signs of struggle or sorrow—García.

???：The seawater has flooded his windpipe and reddened his eyes, yet there he is, staring blankly at the shore ...

???：He's in his home base. He holds no hope for forgiveness from his enemies—not in the slightest. He's a one-man army, fighting only for himself. Alone.

???：Now, do a 360 and look again—he's been utterly defeated.

### 1091901 · A Journey to and Fro

**A Journey to and Fro**

I return once more to Comala Prison. To me, this massive cage is no longer an inescapable labyrinth. When I left, the lady from Manus Vindictae did not stop me, yet I could still feel her gaze—so sorrowful, so blurred, so cold—never once shifting away from me.

On the streets of Ushuaia, a young girl pointed me back toward the prison. That severed thread tugged at my heart, and I knew with certainty that my mission lay here. If I were placed before "fate," my life and death would seemingly follow a predetermined path to its destined end. And as I walk this path, I must carry not even a shred of hesitation.

It is time to speak with "Aleph," the one who answers all questions.

### 1092001 · A Secret Meeting

???：The moment armies start to march, the world stands still.

???：You fight or fall back, charge forward, or run. Those are the only rules on the battlefield.

???：I don't see a start time on this report, nor does it state the reason for or the demands of this war.

???：That's what you think. The scars left on the land may be the most obvious form of destruction, but the true devastation of war is one that develops gradually in the souls of people.

???：Ah, a literary war. Indeed, the violence enacted upon words is often just as devastating as an order on a battlefield.

???：Words must fulfill their duties to their creators, just as soldiers must answer to their commanders.

???：You don't? Hmm. The young writers have been exiled—sent down a path with no signs to follow and no destination.

???：In dark days of bloody conflict, soldiers often find comfort in scriptures and verses—or in letters from their loved ones.

???：They hold those words close—a fragile piece of home—a brief solace from the roaring of the battlefield.

???：But, as they fall one by one, they learn the bitter truth. No destiny awaits them but death, that bitter homesickness still weighing heavy in their hearts.

???：In dreams? Maybe. Walking the streets of your hometown, spending a lazy afternoon at the writing desk—dreams can take you anywhere.

???：But live there too long, and the lines between fiction and reality will blur, and soon you'll never wake up again.

???：Soldiers crawl through trenches, roll in the mud, and charge to their deaths again and again ...

???：... while words are forever trapped within the confines of poetry, made to live, die, and live again under the golden prophecy ...

???：Ugh. I can't stand the way poets talk.

### 101001 · 南极登陆战

> Antarctic Landing

???：You made it. From the US to Brazil to Australia ...

???：From a pleasure cruise across the South Pacific, through Argentina, and finally here, your feet firmly planted on the southernmost continent on Earth.

???：The enemy's been beaten back, retreated to this polar desert, and here you are too—all because of a single command:

???："Find that writer."

???：That's all you've got. No plan, no place or time, not even a full squad.

???：I can't help but wonder what pushes you forward. What gives you the eagerness of a newly enlisted rookie?

???：I'd say it's your instinct to follow the path laid out for you—to satisfy the desires of those who watch from afar.

???：That IS your "arcane skill," isn't it?

???：But you're not a soldier. You're not even a Girl Scout.

???：You have no obligation to follow orders.

???：Personally, I suggest you think twice before obeying a command.

???：After all, you're headed straight into the stronghold of the enemy.

???：In situations like these, those who blindly follow orders often end up as cannon fodder.

???：So, keep this question in mind: Why are you looking for that writer?

### 101002 · Ghost Story

**Ghost Radio**

"So last night I picked up a really weird shortwave station."  
"Was it a buzzer? Or a numbers station? Could just be interference."  
"No, there was a woman's voice spelling letters, but it made no sense. MYDA, SEVA ... She also said something quite creepy."  
"Did you record it?"  
"Only got a few clips. Here, listen."

"Mike, Yankee, Delta, Alpha ... *static noise*"  
"Sierra, Echo ... Victor, Abandon ... I'm the only one left ... Am I really the only one? No, I-I can't give up, no ... *static noise* Run away!"  
"Mike ... Yankee, Delta, ugh, what? *static noise* Buzzing ... the bug is in my ears ... buzz ... Buzz, Bless ... Blasphemy! We brought on the devil's wrath! This is blasphemy!"​  
"Starve,  I'm so hungry ... Is this thing edible?"  
"I'm close to the *static noise* end now."  
"A frozen corpse, a broken skull ... I must ... *static noise* ... find Mother Spirit ... guide ... *static noise*"  
"..."  
"Hotel, Lima, Evil ... Providence. The radio's not working, the radio ... I need to throw it away. Mother Spirit, bless me."

### 1010051 · Workplace Survival Guide​

???：Turn away. Cover your nose.

???：Better not to look at this scene unless you're mentally prepared.

???：What a mess. Surely the work of amateurs.

???：Metaphors aren't really my thing, but if you insist.

???：There's cream cake splattered on the wall and bread ripped and shredded all over the place.

???：Then there's strawberry and chili jam—lots and lots of it. Damn! Is that a rotten grape I just stepped on?

???：Well, as you can see, there was more than one person here.

???：How many is anybody's guess.

???：Anyone with a sense of justice wouldn't let this happen.

???：I'm just a soldier—a brainless follower of orders. I have no grounds to make judgments on morality. But loyalty, that I do know about.

???：Anyone could pledge their allegiance to an undefeated commander. It's a no-brainer for most soldiers.

???：But give that commander a few heavy losses—a couple of misjudgments—and that loyalty becomes a whole lot harder to give.

???：So, if you really want to prove how loyal you are, you should follow a stupid commander—the stupider the better. Just be sure you know what you're getting yourself into.

???：That lieutenant, though—she's a sure way off from being a loyal subordinate.

???：She must've been sick to the stomach when all this went down.

???：Heh. Just a kid.

### 1010111 · 《创世纪》隐喻

> Metaphor in the Fresco

???：Congratulations, you cleared the stage. Though I hardly thought it'd take you so long.

???：The answer was right in front of you the whole time; just cover your eyes. Funny—you had to do the very thing the enemy wanted you to.

???：That was your third lesson: Hands serve to control.

???：Back to the topic. You almost died in there thanks to the false intel from your eye scouts.

???：Luckily, brain command reacted quickly enough to prevent the worst from happening.

???：You'd better set a second-in-command to avoid this kind of thing happening again.

???：Hmm. The ever-beating strategic center, the hub that every drop of blood must pass through, the violently beating when agitated.

???：The most reliable organ, besides the brain. Because it has to be.

???：If only it didn't have a mind of its own. That'll surely lead to destruction, whether it beats fast or slow.

???：The pass—the gate to the body, allowing thoughts to spill out and nutrition to enter.

???：And the minister of foreign affairs.

???：But don't let it take too much charge. Otherwise, disputes and disease will fester in it.

???：It wasn't my intention to talk like a Renaissance artist.

???：But when you reflect on Michelangelo's painting on that grand ceiling, there's no choice but to do so.

???：The key to creating life could be either the hall of wisdom or the land of birth ...

???：So, you should understand art a little better now.

### 101015 · Dark Fairy Tale

**Tales of the Mother Spirit**

The First Tale  
When he arrived in this world, the child received the Mother Spirit's first blessing.  
He stopped crying as a sunbeam fell on the pillow. Through his tears, he saw Her gifts: golden rays warming his hands, white cloths softly swaddling his body, and fresh air breezing into his lungs.  
A single life is all we are given; thus, the Mother Spirit taught the child to cherish.

The Third Tale  
The child gripped a bow, leaving deep footprints in the snow. It was his day to hunt.  
But this land hadn't seen any trace of birds or squirrels for ages. The long-absent spring was growing fainter with every passing year like a distant myth.  
Finally, the child took down a doe just before sunset. Closing those watery eyes glowing with innocence, his weary hands shakily splitting the flesh, he recognized that this was the Mother Spirit's second blessing.  
Staring at the frozen, fragile branches, the child knew it was time to go south.

The Seventh Tale  
The sun fell into the moss-covered plains, its once eternal fire frozen by the ice. Fish drowned in the waters, sinking to the riverbeds where they transmuted into swords.  
Coffins creaked open. Shriveled fingers clawed at passersby, seeking redemption.  
The child trudged across the land, his blood and cotton wool forming scabs over the surface of his skin. His fingertips, first ravaged by chilblains, were then abraded by hoes and pickaxes. Only his vocal cords remained alive to pray to the Mother Spirit.  
Please rewrite the laws of this world. Please grant us a new spring.

The Ninth Tale  
The lost child received the Mother Spirit's third blessing.  
In the farthest south, the child passed through the Mother Spirit's body and knocked upon the gate to Her soul. A sense of warmth he hadn't felt for a long time enveloped him, making him Her child once again.  
Cradled in the Mother Spirit's arms, he heard the last message.  
Many, many years later, when the earth was again dotted with trees and flowers, the child's children sat around the campfire, begging for tales of the Mother Spirit. "I embraced Her and She enlightened me. That was a great gift," he said, "Her great gift."

> A Debate on Religion

???：Join her now, while the gate is still open.

???：Or she'll leave you behind for good.

### 101024 · 再度呼吸

> Breathe Again

???：Another near-drowning. How nostalgic.

???：Take it easy. You can't move yet. Just stay right there and listen to the report.

???：Urgh …

???：Okay. You win.

???：You did it, noble savior. You turned the tide and saved the world at the last moment.

???：And I didn't see it coming. There. Are you satisfied? Can we get to the report now?

???：This is a universal truth. Not just for spinning wheels, but for animals, fathers and sons—mothers and daughters.

???：Just wait a moment longer. The greyhound's looking for your hat.

???：This is the only thing she can think to do besides first aid measures.

???：It's cute, isn't it? Seeing her so naive and superstitious. We should play along for a little longer.

???：Sure, you deserve a moment of quiet, savior.

### 101025 · Distortion

**Discussion over the Radio**

"Team 'Difference Engine,' this is HQ. Southeast district sweep complete, and Arcana's presence has not been detected. Abort mission, link up with Team 'Tungsten Wire,' and proceed to ... Hello? Team 'Difference Engine,' come in! Damn these primitive comms! If I'd been born in this era, I'd have chosen any career without radios."  
"Got anything on your end?"  
"The Field Squad's got cut off, but my device kept picking up crosstalk. There's this Royal Mail Ship reporting a sudden submarine attack, requesting Entente warship assistance. So I guess it's 1917."  
"Can't be 1917. Multiple bands are broadcasting about Hollweg. More likely 1915, if you ask me."  
"No way it's 1915! My liaison team mentioned the Battle of Verdun has been over for ages. Optimistically, we might be at war's end with everyone analyzing the aftermath!"  
"Can't be optimistic. From what I'm hearing, Austria-Hungary is still holding strong, and the Ottomans are no slouches. Probably Manus Vindictae has had a hand in this."  
"Yeah, maybe the arcanists in this war—Wait, Team 'Difference Engine'! This is HQ. Go ahead!"  
The Foundation's communication officer grabs a pen, nearly splashing ink on the other one's face.  
The scratch of pen on paper lasts a full minute before stopping.  
"What did he say?"  
"Two years ago, the Compiègne negotiations failed. It's 1920."

### 111001 · Yet Another War

???：Ah, a visitor. How unexpected. There is nothing here now—only the ashes of war. What is it you want from a place like this?

???：Run along now. Back to your schoolbooks ... or a swing in a playground, perhaps. This is no place for a girl like you.

???：Naturally. I am no celebrity.

???：I'd prefer to keep it that way.

???：Fame is of no interest to me. Just an opportunity for greater harassment.

???：Then you and I are in agreement.

???：The stench of rust and burnt flesh is quite unbearable.

???：Even the maggots squirming over the soldiers' corpses have summoned all their strength just to crawl away.

???：But the entire world is in ruins now. I have nowhere else to go.

???：And it seems you're stuck here with me. You poor sap.

???：You trekked to the end of the world, fought a pointless war, and for what? A broken spinning wheel?

???：Even that, they took away from you—along with the girl clinging to life by a thread—before sending you off to the next battleground.

???：I hope that rusty old thing holds SOME kind of secret—to saving the world, or ending this war.

???：Maybe then, you can take on another career before you die. A watchmaker, perhaps.

???：I've nothing more to say. Would you kindly leave me alone for a while?

???：I have no tea or snacks to offer. I can't even recall what they taste like.

???：Go on. Savor the war. There'll be another after this. No need to worry about things going exactly as you'd like.

???：I've nothing more to say. Would you kindly leave me alone for a while?

???：I have no tea or snacks to offer. I can't even recall what they taste like.

### 111002 · Best Wishes

**The Soldier "Back from the Dead"**

████,

Being stuck in recovery is boring, but at least it keeps you from getting shot on the battlefield. By the time your knee gets better, this damned war might finally be over! Assuming I make it—which is a strange thing to say—my first stop would be to come see you. Maybe, if we're lucky, we could even go home together.

Let me share the recent news. There's a soldier that everyone claims "came back from the dead"—whether that's true or people invented the story doesn't really matter to the officers—he has been reassigned to the front as a sergeant under Major Braun.

Why would he accept such a posting? Why even go back to the front at all? I know your mind would be swimming with these questions, but nobody can figure out his motives. He turned down a safe position with the press bureau but has refused to ever pick up a rifle again. He's only interested in retrieving the bodies of the fallen from the field. Can you believe that? Personally, I'd rather be a dressed-up press puppet over ducking artillery shells any day of the week, even if it meant encouraging young men to march to their deaths ...

... Mein Gott, ████, as I write this, it finally dawns on me: I've always known why he refused them after all. I just never thought anyone would have the courage to make that choice.

### 111005 · Cloak and Statue

**Field Hospital Conversations​​**

"Have you heard about the 'Sentinel'? I saw her that night. Before anyone could react, old Noah was already down. The shot was unbelievable!"  
"Did you get a clear look at her?"  
"It was too dark to see much, just a figure wearing a cloak. But I'm almost certain she was carrying a Lebel rifle; she's probably on our side!"  
"I wouldn't be so sure. They say she's the one that took out General Mathis two weeks ago. Might be someone playing both sides."  
"Either way, having that warmonger gone is good for us all—ow! That hurts!"  
"Bear with it, I'm almost finished."  
Marsha soaks a towel in cold water and wrings it out. The wounded soldier's fever still hasn't broken. Her eyes move from one amputee to the next, checking each stump for signs of infection.  
"Listen," another soldier cuts in, his face wrapped in bandages with only one eye visible. "The 'Sentinel' doesn't take sides because she isn't human. That's no rifle; it's a javelin or a spear. And she's got wings, silent as a bat. During the day, she turns into a stone statue. You could walk right past without noticing. But at night, she comes alive and hunts. Ever seen those church Gargoyles? That's exactly what she looks like."  
"Maybe those wings were just her cloak," the first soldier insists.  
"It's more than that. My comrade saw bullets hit her, but she didn't even flinch, like they were hitting a solid rock. Hey, Johnny, isn't that right? Johnny?"  
Marsha pauses before looking up.  
"Johnny died just after dawn this morning."

### 111007 · A Mother's Rage

???：Look at these boys. They left home with puffed chests and confident smiles on their faces—

???：Thinking they'd go to the front, pull a few triggers, kill a few enemies, and come back as heroes.

???：But when their feet hit the soil, they turned "pacifist," it seems, wounding their own hands rather than their enemies'.

???：Their mothers should have begged them to stay, not waved their handkerchiefs to see them off.

???：Ha, "misjudgment"—quite the convenient excuse.

???：Soldiers follow orders, officers give them, higher-ups make them, parents send their children off to die ...

???：And not one of them will bear the weight of their "misjudgments." Only the dead must answer.

???：Really? You think death is the only option?

???：What could possibly be the point in that?

???：Don't tell me you believe there's GLORY in dying on the battlefield? The thought makes me sick.

???：Look there—at the lifeless body lying flat in the mud.

???：He couldn't even muster the courage to flee, so he bribed a fellow soldier—a man he barely knew—with three cigarettes to surrender with him.

???：He died—killed in a moment of pure, senseless hatred. And the other lived—saved by a flicker of mercy.

???：Where should his mother aim her grief? At the officer who changed his mind just a moment too late? Hah!

???：The officer will be pardoned; they'll say he, too, was a victim of this endless, miserable cycle of hatred.

???：So tell me—please, tell me—to whom this rage should go, and I will take this kitchen knife and plunge it into their heart!

### 111009 · The Last Turnip Stew

???：I'm afraid I only have turnip stew to serve you. I will add a few sausage scraps to your bowl, however.

???：Here. Now go. It's best you leave before another battle breaks out.

???：Is there something else you want from me?

???：You know what? Since you're so fond of visiting ... take the chair you're sitting on.

???：Even through the coldest winters, that chair was spared the fire. Perhaps it was meant for you.

???：Ah, yes—a tragedy to serve such a dish to such an honorable guest, but I'm afraid I have no tears left to shed to season the thing.

???：If this farce had ended sooner, perhaps I'd have treated you to a feast—

???：Bacon, beef stew, pretzels, mashed potatoes, sauerkraut, and a tankard of beer, of course.

???：But the big men in the army disagreed. They valued shiny medals on their chests over food on their people's tables.

???：Right. Since you insist on staying, I will go to the market and see what I can find.

???：Sit here for a while longer, if you like. Prepare for a lifelong stay, even.

???：With any luck, this place will become your grave by the time the next farce ends.

### 111011 · Discipline and Regulations

**Arcane Support Company Special Regulations**

1. Respect all comrades equally, regardless of classification.

2. Execute orders exactly; no changes based on divination.

3. Do not neglect conventional science; bullets often outkill arcane powers.

4. You are fully accountable for entities you summon.

5. No mental surveillance, influence, or manipulation of personnel without authorization.

6. No arcane modification of issued equipment without authorization.

7. Conduct spirit contact only with another member present; limit to three minutes.

8. Know the alternative forms of your comrades.

9. Report and secure approval before using large rituals or high-level incantations.

10. Expect enemy arcanists and other supernatural threats during operations.

### 111012 · Researchers

**Gargoyle Research Notebook**

It was during the Merovingian dynasty that the Bishop of Rouen successfully subdued the first known Gargoyle. This Gargoyle and his descendants then formed a unique lineage devoted to protecting churches across generations. However, in recent times, many younger Gargoyles have chosen to abandon this ancient tradition, leaving their dark church perches for the light and glamour of the modern world.

Based on my recent observations, this generational conflict continues to cause significant distress among elder Gargoyles.  
* It's worth noting that the younger generation's departure also relates to modern churches being unable to provide adequate sustenance, reflecting broader societal changes. This aspect warrants further historical analysis.

Gargoyles have some unique arcane skills. First, their arcane ability allows them to petrify their own body parts—a formidable defensive mechanism during the age of close-quarters combat with bayonets and spears, though considerably less effective against modern artillery bombardments.  
Additionally, Gargoyles possess exceptional visual capabilities, with particularly remarkable night vision. This trait complements their natural stealth abilities, enabling them to both detect hidden threats with ease and execute perfectly timed ambushes.  
* I must admit, it's surprising such impressive combat abilities were historically limited to the protection of churches—though my lack of religious knowledge may explain this confusion.  
· Your theological ignorance is indeed astounding. Mandatory religious studies await you.  
* While I object to you reading my research notes without permission, I'll concede that compared to your piety, I'm clearly the irreligious one—just a pure biologist.  
· Who said I'm not a "pure biologist"?

Gargoyles carry a curse. While the origins of their curse remain shrouded in secrecy, people are open about its manifestations: excessive bloodshed appears to corrupt them. This corruption takes the form of mental instability, progressing from rejecting to completely abandoning their faith.  
· Well, my "pure biologist" friend, your Gargoyle research now necessitates the very religious study that you say you have no interest in.  
* This notebook clearly requires a sturdier lock.

### 111016 · Entrusted Visions

**A Letter from Home​**

My Dearest Cousin Ladislas,

How oft thou art in my prayers. I trust with all my heart that Italy hath already begun to feel as home unto thee. I am told the folk there are warm of spirit and generous of hand; I can well believe thou hast found many a kindly friend upon thy path.

Marianne and I do prepare for a journey into Austria, and our hearts are full of longing. For there lieth, as thou hast spake so oft, the very heart of the new art. I yearn to lift mine eyes unto the golden tracery crowning the Secession Building, whilst Marianne doth dream of wandering through the galleries and little shops of the Wiener Werkstätte. We have set our minds also upon the Karlskirche—Fischer von Erlach's great Baroque church. They say its nineteen stained-glass windows shine as jewels and its nave holdeth a majesty not soon forgotten. Should any sketches from our travels serve thy studies, it would delight me to send them forth.

Our past words dwell yet within me. Marianne telleth me ever of the new age dawning—of electric light supplanting the gentle glow of candles, and of the darkness of our nights fading away. Yet, dear Ladislas, I still feel the nearness of the old ways. This age of machines hath left many unsettled and without refuge; soon they shall crave a quiet place wherein to kneel and seek God's comfort and counsel.

Then do I call to mind thy words, and my heart is moved—that our prayers must rise above the clamour of industry. And so do I begin to dream, with thee in my thoughts, of a church for this new century, yet still a haven for the soul. Though our cousin smileth at such fancies, her words have sown new imaginings in me, and I have begun to set them down in drawings. Mayhap to raise such a place would take ten, even fifteen years; yet it comforteth me to picture thee by my side when the time cometh, helping to bring our vision to life.

Thou art ever in my thoughts and ever in my prayers.

With all my love,  
Agnès  
April 2, 1914

### 111018 · Dog Whistle

???：The lapdogs are barking again.

???：Their masters remain quiet ... for the most part. And you know why.

???：But silence gives no orders.

???：So the masters whistle. Just a pitch. No words.

???：And so, no words are heard—only the baying of dogs.

???：Perhaps you've forgotten the words echoed in that cave ... darling. Never mind.

???：Forgetfulness can be a mercy. Especially in this dog fight.
