### 111019 · Amid the Chaos

**A Recent Report on Manus Vindictae​​**

Reports continue to arrive from across the globe concerning arcanists operating under Manus Vindictae (a complete list of names and locations can be found in the attached appendix). These individuals have been gathering in coordinated cells, their training evident. Given the efficiency and precision of their attacks against human military forces, it is no longer accurate to call them merely an "organization"; they are, in effect, an army.

Each morning I open what feels like ten thousand urgent messages clamoring for my attention, with still more pleas for reinforcements. Our office has been working without pause. Fatigue has dulled even my anger; nothing surprises me anymore—not even reports of skirmishes between warlords in the eastern territories. At times, I am tempted to reply that they needn't fret; east or west, it makes little difference. The entire world is sliding into chaos.

...

The contingency plan drafted by Creius has now been submitted to the committee for review. It addresses the escalating threat posed by Manus Vindictae. Should they succeed in building a large-scale regular force—or if their attacks continue to spread disorder—the proposal urges the Foundation to form its own standing military rather than rely on Zeno. Creius sets forth what he claims is the most efficient approach: recruiting and reorganizing both human and arcanist soldiers already at the front.

This is madness. Does he intend to make the Foundation a rival to every government on earth—an entity viewed with still deeper suspicion? Creius has always been radical, but war elevates such voices. He shows no concern for how the world might react, arguing instead that "we live in an age the Storm could erase at any moment, so we are not obliged to heed their protests." Only recently would such words have been unthinkable.

His position is plain: if Manus Vindictae declares open war, governments will have no choice but to align with the Foundation. Yet should we follow his plan, we begin the most perilous of games.

### 111022 · Harsh Condemnation

**Formula Excerpts**

Living bone buds, withered mandrake leaves, owl feathers, turpentine. Burned in an airtight container.  
Oily volatile liquid carrying a moss-like scent. Weakly soluble in water but readily absorbed by animal fat.  
Causes immediate nervous system damage, including tremors, aphasia, mania, delirium, and muscle paralysis. In rare instances, it may temporarily increase arcane abilities.

Jack jumper ants, Inchman ants, sulfur. Processed in acid-treated belladonna juice, using mithril as a catalyst .  
Colorless gas with a faint garlic smell that often gets hidden by other odors. Slightly acidic, easily soluble in water, and potentially explosive at high concentrations.  
Causes acute liver and kidney injuries and mucous injuries, with particularly strong effects on humans.

Suriname toads, bloodroots, brown algae, shed snake skin. Heat to 180℉ while adding chlorine gas.  
Deep purple gas with a sharp, irritating smell. Slightly soluble in water, slightly denser than air, and easily decomposes when exposed to light.  
Causes lacrimation and coughing. Pulmonary edema occurs within 2–3 minutes of inhalation. Both humans and arcanists may experience hallucinations after exposure.

### 1110221 · Your Excellency, There's Nothing We Can Do

**An Undelivered Letter​​**

████,

It has taken me a long time to write this letter, though my mind has never ceased its vigilance.

You know, as well as I, that I have spent my life mastering the concealment of intent, approaching even the unknown with a scholar's patience. You know, too, why I have worked to prevent conflict—not from idealism, but because I have seen what war truly leaves behind, long before the Foundation, long before the "Storm" rose.

My principles have often led me to aid or forge alliances with adversaries. I have long sought to create a balance between humans and arcanists. I firmly believe that Force is a tool, nothing more, and its misuse brings only chaos. While we may bear a fraction of the consequences, ordinary lives carry the rest. I have acted with care, and for that I have been called a Dove—a label I dismiss. My concern has never been for titles or comfort, only for what must endure.

Zeno's defection is largely credited with the rise of the Hawks. Committee members, wary and distrustful, sought to establish a military force directly under Foundation control. Meanwhile, the "Storm" has transported us back to a time dominated by warfare. Creius's philosophy runs contrary to our values and the traditions that preserve a fragile equilibrium. His contingency plan would not only threaten security but also collapse it. Should we provoke governments, forsake our mediating role, or coerce allegiance—how could the Foundation survive the "Storm", Manus Vindictae, or the world itself?

And yet ████, I cannot deny that Creius has succeeded—though his triumph chills me more than it reassures.

Hill 299 is secured. Eight divisions have been withdrawn from the frontline trenches. Soldiers untrained against arcane assault survived where they would otherwise have perished. Nearly one hundred thousand lives were spared. All achieved under the shadow of his contingency plan. None can dispute the effectiveness of his actions—but effectiveness is not wisdom. The currents he has unleashed will embolden the reckless. Those who were once cautious may now lean toward the Hawks. The balance we have preserved hangs by the thinnest of threads.

When the battlefield dispatches arrived, I understood the inevitable: people will now march willingly into chaos, blind to the storm gathering beneath their feet.

### 111023 · Heartfelt Blessing

**To Marianne**

Marianne,​​

I hope you'll understand why I couldn't say goodbye properly, as I understood when you did the same. By the time I finally learned where you had gone, you were already deep underground, facing poisonous gases. Anyway, I just want this letter to reach you safely.

The "XII" required a medic, and although I sensed there was something distinct about this squad, I accepted; it would keep me on the front lines longer, and Mr. Creius's invitation could not have been more timely. Of course, this means I had to leave immediately with the squad. We're moving to the Eastern Front soon, where some of my comrades are reportedly stationed.

While I'm now part of the "XII," I've been granted permission to operate independently in the eastern region for a while. I'm hoping to locate some of my former comrades from my order to see if any might be interested in joining the Foundation.

Marianne, I know your family has an unusual constitution, and you might think overly cautious, but I have to say this: after prolonged combat in poisonous gas conditions, you really should get a full medical examination.

Wishing you all the best.

Marsha

### 1110241 · "Free" Intelligence

**Burn After Reading**

Due to unexpected circumstances, I had temporary access to an incomplete report. While the information it contained was too fragmented to understand the complete situation, it did reveal one fact: Zeno and the Foundation have been secretly working on "Storm" research. This work is entirely separate from any known research and lies beyond the scope of security clearance.

What is particularly concerning is that the majority of Foundation staff and Laplace personnel appear completely unaware of these covert operations. The mere existence of such hidden activities is, in itself, deeply concerning.

### 1120101 · Battlefield Recording

**Zeno Arms Academy Recording Restoration Report**

[File Number: ΔZENO-201124]  
Submitting Organization: The St. Pavlov Foundation (via the Timekeeper)  
Classification Level: IV (Non-Classified)  
File Status: Partial irreversible data loss  
Restoration Date: November 24, 1920

1. Technology Notes on Restoration  
    The "Zeno Audio Restoration Technique" is based on hypotheses derived from the arcane theory of resonance particles. According to documented research, sound waves converted through the communicator leave microscopic arcane vibrations in surrounding media during transmission. When environmental materials (metal oxide layers, residual electric fields, or air density gradients) are reactivated, these disturbances can be reconstructed into audible signals through phase differences at extremely low frequencies.  
    When portions of the signal exhibit semantic drift or narrative gaps due to disruption, the restored text represents a reconstructed version obtained through multiple iterative retranslations. Cross-referencing confirms the restoration achieves 99.97% accuracy compared to the original recording.

2. Restoration Report  
    No. 00-1-1 "I've been on a mission on the Eastern Front, under the direct orders of Admiral Somme."  
    No. 00-1-2 "Guess what I found, капитан?"  
    No. 00-1-3 "A Zeno outpost without jurisdiction."  
    No. 00-2-1 "As far as I'm aware, Zeno hasn't deployed any forces to this location. From the looks of it, I think it's the Igor rebels."  
    No. 00-2-2 "Details remain unclear. I'll determine the next steps after consulting with the commander."  
    No. 00-2-3 "If you're interested in Igor's movements, call me back as soon as you can."

3. Additional Notes

### 1120102 · The Profit Frontline

**A Conversation Among the Big Shots**

Inside a Foundation office, the sound of a ringing communicator interrupts a briefing.  
    Irinei glances at the device—a direct line to Zeno headquarters. He nods, signaling the committee member to wrap up mid-report. Once the door closes, he picks up.  
    "Somme here. According to Ivan's report, the cavalry has been harassed by bandits for weeks."  
    "Oh? Are these bandits connected to the Manus?"  
    "No evidence of that. More like a mix of local warlords and scattered gangs. Though a couple of the rapidly growing factions seem suspicious."  
    "Have your people maintain surveillance. Contact the Foundation the moment anything develops—we'll send reinforcements."  
    Irinei is rarely this cooperative, but Somme knows he can't stake the entire steppe's fate on so-called "reinforcements."  
    "No need. Ivan has enough manpower."  
    "Assuming the Cavalry Corps can be fully trusted—I looked into Commander Ivan. He was a trusted subordinate to that former admiral. Plus, I heard they're both from the Don River region ..."  
    "I will handle him." Somme firmly cuts off his attempt at political maneuvering.  
    "I hope you understand that the Don River region is a critical component of the Foundation's strategy. To ensure downstream stability and the safety of our new recruits, supporting the Cavalry Corps is our top priority." The leader of the Hawks leaves no room for argument.  
    "Save it. Since 1999, I've dealt with more politicians like you than I've smoked cigarettes. The Don is just a crack in the dam. What really matters is the line of battle along the Black Sea and the Baltic Sea, right?"  
    "Stay in touch, Admiral."  
    After the call ends, Irinei straightens in his chair, drumming his fingers against the desk.  
    He begins to ponder: could this small "crack" pose a risk of total collapse?

### 1120201 · Behind the Golden Famine

**A Poem Written on a Riverside Tree Trunk**

Weeping Wheat

Glittering golden plains  
A gentle breeze passes, yet no grain dances  
The earth is like a misunderstood mother  
Again and again  
Reaching out her withered hands

I see sunlight fall on the fields  
The wheat stands quiet and clean, almost cruel  
O weeping wheat!  
May you bear silent fruit, enough to sustain.  
Even if only enough to fill a child's smile

—Anonymous poet, wandering along the Don

### 1120301 · 铭印

> Imprint

???：Ah, we meet again—though it isn't much of a surprise.

???：Your move from the Western Front to the eastern one is utterly predictable.

???：Running around like a lost chick, following after her mother hen—or really anything big enough to grab your attention.

???：Will you ever learn to stand on your own? When will you grow up?

???：And then what? Watch her disappear again?

???：You're not an idiot, so why do you keep making the same mistakes?

???：You aren't the type to find joy in running on a treadmill, are you?

???：Then again—maybe you are that type after all, at least when it comes to chasing fashion.

???：What's that supposed to mean? Of course I understand family. I know what "love" is!

???：She attended to your every need when you were still wet behind the wings. You depended on her, clung to her—because your very survival demanded it.

???：Then one day, she was gone from your life. You had to fly on your own, do your own chores. You wept over the "good old days" of comfort and being cared for—oh, poor birdie.

???：But the truth is, that care and affection were nothing more than her way of feeding a sick obsession with control. That's what "love" is—shallow as a birdbath!

???：I was "loved" once. I "loved" others, too. I've experienced it firsthand, and I understand it as well as anyone!

???：Ah...

???：She's key to saving the world—that's what you're saying? I'm tired of it.

???：Tired of sick jokes like this, with self-important ideologues bragging about securing a bright future for everyone.

???：You hear any cheers or laughter? Nope—because everyone who believed in their causes already died fighting for them.

???：If you ask me, we should hang some of those "big shots" in public. It wouldn't bring peace, of course, but maybe the rest of us would feel a little relief ...

???：Come, take a seat. I've set your favorite chair facing east.

???：The view's better that way, not to mention that the river helps wash away the stench of corpses.

### 1120302 · Attached Files

**A Tea-Scented Letter**

Dear Vertin,  
    I hope this letter finds you well.  
    I'm sorry I won't able to see you off—things have piled up due to factional disputes within the Foundation. We live in such chaos that even an organization as self-professedly rational as the Foundation cannot avoid being swept into the political whirlpool. I wish you safe travels to the Eastern Front.  
    I've enclosed a copy of Commander Ivan Ivanovich Burakov's file. You're about to enter the jurisdiction of the Zeno Cavalry Corps. Ivan and Igor have known each other since childhood. Both come from an old village downstream of the Don, yet fate drove them apart along the same muddy roads. They once commanded two separate units within Zeno, nominally independent but with an unspoken understanding that lingered between them.  
    Commander Ivan is difficult to gauge. According to the records, he's plainspoken and awkward in social settings, which has earned him Admiral Somme's high praise as a "loyal and trustworthy" man.  
    However, I must caution you: don't place complete trust in him. Hearts rust in wartime, especially those of soldiers who've endured betrayal and hard choices. Moreover, the bond between Ivan and Igor may pose hidden risks. In times like these, you'll have to weigh trust and suspicion for yourself.  
    Winter is coming to the Don. The damp cold settles heavily over riverside cities this time of year. Along with the file, I've included several packets of ginger tea. Steep it in hot water and drink—it helps ward off the chill.  
    Stay in touch if you can.  
    Safe travels.  
Zhang Zhizhi  
November 25, 1920

### 1120601 · Special Times

**Ms. Galina's Wartime Recipes**

Rye Bread  
Aged flour, wheat husks, potato skins, a little salt.  
Dry and grind the potato skins, then mix them with the wheat husks into the aged flour. Add warm water and knead it into a dough, then bake beside the stove ashes.  
The Golden Famine leaves us short on oil and grain, so there's no oil for the bread. The baked loaf comes out hard and bitter, but at least it fills the stomach. Ivan loves smearing chilli paste on top—he says he's eaten it this way since childhood! I've made a note to prepare extra for him next time.

Smoked Fish  
River carp, elm twigs, one old newspaper.  
Clean the fish, salt it, then smoke it with elm twigs and newspaper until the skin turns black and dry.  
The smell carries three houses down, but don't be greedy. Save the fish oil—freeze it for soup.

Grass Roots Soup  
Ingredients: Wild grass roots, nettles, half a potato, a pinch of chili.  
Instructions: Boil the roots until soft, add chili, stove ash, and salted grass seeds to mimic borscht. The recipe came from a young man I met by the riverside wheat field. He claimed to be a nameless wandering poet.  
*Missing Ingredients: Sour cream, carrots, and onions. Can't make proper borscht. Grass roots have been dug out nearly everywhere. I can only forage along the ditches now.

Jam from Neighbor Valia  
Note: They hid two jars of real apple jam before the famine hit, and gave me one ... I've tucked it on the top shelf of the cupboard, wrapped in old cloth so the rats won't smell it.  
Her husband lost an entire wheat field's harvest ... May the Mother Earth bless this kind couple.

*I've told Ivan to stop bringing food home. The people at the front have it harder than we do.  
Besides, in the face of disaster, soldiers and civilians must be treated equally! Even with his position, I won't allow our family any special treatment.

### 1120701 · Salute to the New Sun

**Sentinel Unit Internal Notice No. 47**

To all Sentinel Unit members:  
    Intelligence confirmed that former chief of staff Captain Krutov deserted during the return voyage from the Antarctic Operation, taking critical documents and military equipment, including classified files and multiple communication devices.  
    His whereabouts remain unknown. It is highly likely that he has defected to operate as an independent force or has entered into collaboration with Manus Vindictae.  
    Admiral Igor has designated him as a top-priority threat. Any personnel who encounter Krutov during field missions or transit must report so immediately. Do not pursue or take unauthorized action.

Additionally, according to the latest announcement from headquarters, Admiral Igor will personally lead elite forces to the Bereggrad region in the lower Don after completion of the Sanctuary's infrastructure. This operation involves coordination between the front line and the intelligence department. To prevent communication leaks, subsequent personnel assignments will be communicated under separate notice.

Finally, in light of ongoing organizational restructuring, the Sentinel Unit will be renamed the "Gryphon Legion."  
    We welcome alternative naming proposals. If you wish to submit one, please deliver your suggestion to Lieutenant Moldir within seven days.  
    Note: Names must reflect our military ethos. Offensive, religious, or politically suggestive terms are prohibited.

Issued by: Lieutenant Moldir  
Date: ██ ██

### 1121001 · Where Does the Skylark Rest

???：Look at these boys. They left home with puffed chests and confident smiles on their faces—

???：Thinking they'd go to the front, pull a few triggers, kill a few enemies, and come back as heroes.

???：But when their feet hit the soil, they turned "pacifist," it seems, wounding their own hands rather than their enemies'.

???：Their mothers should have begged them to stay, not waved their handkerchiefs to see them off.

???：Ha, "misjudgment"—quite the convenient excuse.

???：Soldiers follow orders, officers give them, higher-ups make them, parents send their children off to die ...

???：And not one of them will bear the weight of their "misjudgments." Only the dead must answer.

???：Really? You think death is the only option?

???：What could possibly be the point in that?

???：Don't tell me you believe there's GLORY in dying on the battlefield? The thought makes me sick.

???：Look there—at the lifeless body lying flat in the mud.

???：He couldn't even muster the courage to flee, so he bribed a fellow soldier—a man he barely knew—with three cigarettes to surrender with him.

???：He died—killed in a moment of pure, senseless hatred. And the other lived—saved by a flicker of mercy.

???：Where should his mother aim her grief? At the officer who changed his mind just a moment too late? Hah!

???：The officer will be pardoned; they'll say he, too, was a victim of this endless, miserable cycle of hatred.

???：So tell me—please, tell me—to whom this rage should go, and I will take this kitchen knife and plunge it into their heart!

### 1121101 · 安眠

> Soothing Rest

???：Seems you've got some iron beneath that calm surface.

???：Not everyone would sleep so soundly in a stranger's house.

???：Honestly, you could've just made yourself at home and stayed the night. When I take out the trash later, I'll be sure to toss you out along with it.

???：Oh, spare me the details!

???：I'm sure it was full of enviable warmth and radiance—just enough to scorch my miserable, frozen heart.

???：Whatever you saw or did in that dream, do yourself a favor and let it go.

???：You did—and I owe you my thanks.

???：Your sleep talking added a bit of flavor to my otherwise bland meal.

???：I haven't had such an appealing appetizer in a long time.

???：The soup's gone cold. That ship's sailed.

???：You may not like the reality served on your silver platter—no one does—but you'll have to swallow it.

???：Blame this mess on the black robes if you want; those cowards keep trying to drag the world back to their "good old days."

???：And the white robes preaching the future? No less clowns for their taste in uniforms—falling off their high horses again and again, brought down by their own arrogance.

???：Who would have thought? The whole universe turned into one circus starring two troupes of fools.

???：Why didn't that blind woman just let the world burn back then? It wouldn't have been worse than these chaotic times. And who asked her to play the martyr, anyway?

???：If you're lucky enough to find her, take her home—and make sure she stays there, quietly, for everyone's sake.

### 1121102 · Sleep in the Sound of Bones

**The Lost Diary of a Captive**

[An old diary with black, viscous stains smeared across its cover. The corners are torn and dog-eared. It seems the owner abandoned it recently.]  
November 11  
    Every night since my capture, I've heard terrifying, fragmented whispers coming from outside ... and screams—not just one or two people, but many, shrieking and wailing in pain. I can also smell something burning and rotting. Damn it, are those Manus dogs burning corpses? Or performing some twisted ritual?  
...  
November XX  
    I've lost track of the date. Time and life hold no meaning in this godforsaken place. I once saved lives as a doctor ... now, they've used my medical instruments to brutally murder Boyaryshkin, Artyom, and Malyekhov right in front of me. I don't care anymore. If they're going to come for me eventually, they might as well hurry and end it now.  
...  
November XX  
    I don't know how to write this. I actually survived that nightmare—which feels like a curse in itself. We were herded onto an empty clearing where a koldunya in black robes was chanting a song. I could barely make out the words: "... Inculcator of Arcanum, let me manifest decay anew." She pulled away the shroud covering the corpse. As the black mist dissipated, a broken body lay before us.  
    She handed me my old scalpel: "Dissect it." I looked down at the remains exposed to the air. Male, early twenties. Mottled, wrinkled skin. Ribs collapsed and fractured. I didn't know what I was doing—fear made me forget everything I once believed. Standing beside her, I raised the blade and started cutting. The scraping of bone was monotonous, but I freed that soul from its mortal prison.  
    When I lowered the knife, I realized we were surrounded by a black fog reeking of death. All the other captives had died, their faces twisted in agony. The koldunya seized my filthy hand and laughed: "Thank you, Charon! You helped me unravel death and command it, master Death, and aid its power!"  
...  
November XX  
    Boyaryshkin, Artyom, Malyekhov—they came for me! I didn't mean to! I'm sorry, I'm sorry, I'm sorry ...  
[The remaining text is obscured by black, viscous stains.]

### 1121301 · Mounting Procedure and Posture

**Colonel Sternova's Operation Order Brief**

Operation Order Brief  
[In accordance with Zeno Military Security Protocol, this brief has been encrypted.]

Operation Orders: #Operation Frozen Horseshoe - Ω. "Spear" Light Cavalry Division 0500

1. Mission  
    You are ordered to form a rapid strike team with the "Shashka" and "Shield" units, hereby designated "Ω".  
    Strike Team "Ω" will coordinate with "Horse Hunter" forces and the "Old Citizen" unit to establish an "Ω Formation" battlefront. Advance upstream along the golden bank of the "Undercurrent" waterway to launch a surprise assault. Your core objective is to restrict enemy deployment space at all costs.

2. Enemy Intel  
    Our forces and "Horse Hunter" forces suffered infiltration attacks in "Southern Town." We have confirmed the enemy's new attack target. Enemy "Black Locust" forces are massing on the golden bank of the "Undercurrent" waterway. They are expected to cross in full strength and launch a general offensive against "Southern Town."  
    According to reliable intelligence, enemy "Black Locust" forces are expected to mass for attack within the next 24 hours. However, the exact strength of the troops committed to the dark bank of the "Undercurrent" waterway remains unknown.

3. Execution  
    Rapidly ford the "Undercurrent" waterway. Before enemy forces complete assembly, execute a flanking maneuver from the narrow wing. Ambush and eliminate all encountered enemy units. Penetrate enemy rear positions and sever supply lines. All combat units must reach the Old Tree riverside sector and assemble before the autumn harvest.

4. Coordination  
    "Horse Hunter" forces and the "Old Citizen" unit have been integrated into the "Southern Town" defense network and will conduct diversionary attacks from the dark bank. "Long Tail" forces will provide supporting fire and clean-up.  
    Following the extension of the offensive, your unit must immediately engage the enemy and coordinate with friendly forces for encirclement. If you encounter an enemy counterattack, you may request air support.  
    Due to logistical constraints, the "Scout Shortsword" camp will assist in securing abandoned horses and equipment recovered during operations.

5. Command and Signals  
    Maintain "Status Quo" until next classified order is received.

### 1121601 · Basic Treatment Protocol

???：I'm afraid I only have turnip stew to serve you. I will add a few sausage scraps to your bowl, however.

???：Here. Now go. It's best you leave before another battle breaks out.

???：Is there something else you want from me?

???：You know what? Since you're so fond of visiting ... take the chair you're sitting on.

???：Even through the coldest winters, that chair was spared the fire. Perhaps it was meant for you.

???：Ah, yes—a tragedy to serve such a dish to such an honorable guest, but I'm afraid I have no tears left to shed to season the thing.

???：If this farce had ended sooner, perhaps I'd have treated you to a feast—

???：Bacon, beef stew, pretzels, mashed potatoes, sauerkraut, and a tankard of beer, of course.

???：But the big men in the army disagreed. They valued shiny medals on their chests over food on their people's tables.

???：Right. Since you insist on staying, I will go to the market and see what I can find.

???：Sit here for a while longer, if you like. Prepare for a lifelong stay, even.

???：With any luck, this place will become your grave by the time the next farce ends.

### 1121602 · Open Channel

**A Telegram to Cavalry Corps Headquarters**

Subject: Enemy Nighttime Infiltration and Assault

Summary: Beginning at dawn today, enemy forces infiltrated our southwestern defensive perimeter through unknown routes, intending to sabotage rear medical infrastructure. Our forces, with support from the Gryphon Legion, successfully contained and repelled the infiltrators.

Operational Results and Loss Assessment:

—Southern District: The southern district medical supply warehouse was breached. 25 crates of medical supplies, including morphine and bandages were confirmed lost. The 7th Block Militia Company defending the facility suffered heavy casualties: 59 KIA. The facility is non-operational.

—Eastern Part of the Western District: Our counterattack succeeded. With support from Cavalry Corps medical personnel, Pokrov Children's Hospital was secured. The following supplies were recovered from enemy forces:  
        Surgical instruments and medications: 33 crates;  
        Children's clothing and bedding (winter): 4 crates;  
        Medical equipment: 3 crates of incubators, 10 crates of steam inhalers and other devices.  
During defensive and counteroffensive operations, the 3rd Street Militia Company sustained 23 KIA. Hospital medical staff sustained 10 KIA, and the Foundation medical personnel sustained 1 WIA (critical).

—Southern Part of the Western District: Enemy assault repelled by Igor's Gryphon Legion and main militia forces. The Sanatorium remains intact. Medical assets and wounded successfully secured. Detailed inventory of recovered supplies is undergoing final verification; will be submitted with a supplementary report.

Current Status: City defense elevated to maximum alert. All critical facilities have received reinforcements to security.

Request: Militia forces urgently require resupply of lost medical materials, particularly analgesics and bandages. We respectfully request that Cavalry Corps command provide material assistance and facilitate logistical transport.

Signed: ████

### 1121701 · Knowledge Without Borders

**Picrasma Tree Extract and Its Preparation Method**

Project Title: Picrasma Tree Extract and Its Preparation Method  
Abstract  
...  
Technical Field  
This project concerns the Picrasma Tree extract "Picrasmine" and its preparation method.

Background  
Picrasma is a deciduous medicinal tree, found in both wild and cultivated settings. Its roots and fruits possess medicinal value, with the primary active extract being a fat-soluble resin. The primary pharmacological agent in Picrasma is Picrasmine, which activates cell populations throughout arcanists' brains and bodies and has shown no observed dependency effects. The Laplace Scientific Computing Center Medical Technology Division has included it in the pharmacopeia.  
...  
Experimental findings indicate that standardized Picrasma extract—Picrasmine—exhibits toxicity to tissue cells in both the brain and body of individuals with less than one-quarter arcane lineage. This toxic response may correlate with specific metabolic dysfunctions in such populations. Single high-dose administration or prolonged intake of Picrasmine carries a significant risk of severe cardiac failure. Consequently, this project's findings must not be applied to general potion development and are strictly prohibited for use in pharmaceuticals intended for humans or mixed-blood humans.

Technical Content  
To achieve the project's technical objectives, primary extraction procedures are outlined below. A process flow diagram is attached.  
① Material sorting; ② Washing; ③ Drying; ④ Grinding; ⑤ Primary purification; ⑥ Filtration; ⑦ Concentration; ⑧ Drying and grinding; ⑨ Secondary purification; ⑩ Concentration and pulverization of secondary extract.

Diagram Description  
The attached diagram illustrates the process flow for this project. (diagram)

Implementation  
Case Examples:  
...  
Notice:  
The preparation method and procedures outlined in this project must strictly comply with regulations issued by the Pax Security Council and must not be applied to pharmaceutical development for humans or mixed-blood humans.

### 1122001 · After the Shots

???：The lapdogs are barking again.

???：Their masters remain quiet ... for the most part. And you know why.

???：But silence gives no orders.

???：So the masters whistle. Just a pitch. No words.

???：And so, no words are heard—only the baying of dogs.

???：Perhaps you've forgotten the words echoed in that cave ... darling. Never mind.

???：Forgetfulness can be a mercy. Especially in this dog fight.

???：If only these hounds would forget their training ... then perhaps, at last, the barking would stop.

???：How dare you! To think I should speak like some slick-talking veteran!

???：I'd slit my throat myself if I so much as uttered a word akin to those devils!

???：They're bastards—abominations! Every last one of them! I—

???：Ahem ... Please accept my apologies. Wounds are hard to heal when they are torn open time and time again.

### 1122301 · Administrative Complaint

???：I'm sorry, but I think it's time you left.

???：There will be little more to see here.

???：What difference does it make?

???：Earthquakes, floods, famine, plague, robbery, car crashes, a slip in the shower ...

???：War is just one catastrophe among many.

???：It's not worth your attention.

???：At least, this war is not.

???：Next time? Are you so intrigued by the life of a pension-drawn widow?

???：I would decline, but my upbringing demands otherwise. How unfortunate ...

???：Well ... perhaps it's better to drink dirty water beneath the eaves than to eat dust on the battlefield.

???：That is all. Hurry along to the eastern front. Check on your friend. I will pray for her.

### 1122401 · 非法入侵

> Invasion

???：Give me a minute; I'll go deal with the intruder.

???：Walking into my home with blood-soaked boots! Tsk. Such insolence. Does he think sending people to die makes him special?

???：He'll pay for this in blood!

???：That's nowhere near enough. He'll have to scrub every bloodstain off the floor.

???：I don't want to hear any excuses. This whole mess is his own fault.

???：I AM calm. He brought the blood here, so he'll pay by cleaning up every drop.

???：I won't even lend him a rag—he can use that fancy coat of his for all I care.

???：So he claims those "big shots" forced him to stain his hands, huh? How convenient.

???：And this plan to "restore everything to how it was"? Utterly foolish.

???：But the fact that he followed it to the letter makes him no different from the ones who devised it.

???：To realize their "grand vision," those so-called realists kept piling weight on one side of the scale ...

???：Sacrificing one innocent life after another just so someone could remember a name that wasn't hers anymore. Absurd.

???：I have one thing to say to those arrogant megalomaniacs wanting to play gods: anyone who has forgotten their own name is as good as dead—and forcing them to wear it again won't change a thing.

???：What happened has happened. Those bloody footprints can never truly be erased.

## 章节 113

### 1130101 · The "Storm" Downpours

**Pre-Storm Field Observation Reports**

According to field reports submitted by Marcus, Semmelweis, and other operatives across European branches, locations marked as "critical points of time" are now scattered across Europe in the current historical period. Both their number and distribution far exceed anything previously on record.  
This is a turbulent era. The territorial and political disputes left by the war have Europe redrawing its map in blood and paperwork, while many nations are still rebuilding. Famine, unemployment, the lingering effects of influenza, and other post-war wounds pervade everyday life. Rebellious movements like Dadaism take root in the spiritual confusion of the age. The invention of wireless radio and small home appliances sparks the first stirrings of a technological revolution ...  
Despite repeated reinforcements, new "critical points of time" continue to surface in cities and corners across multiple countries, and we are still unable to pinpoint where Manus Vindictae are most active. With a severe manpower shortage at the headquarters checkpoint, we can no longer spare operatives for continued observation.  
In summary, based on reports from all teams, we are unable to confirm Manus Vindictae's operational direction, mount a targeted counteroffensive, or slow the deterioration and expansion of these "critical points of time." Headquarters has decided to execute the evacuation plan immediately in order to preserve as many of our forces as possible. All field team leaders are to order and urge their operatives to return to headquarters without delay or proceed to the nearest shelter to take refuge from the "Storm."

### 1130102 · Temporary Dispatch

**Complaints From a Part-Time Guard**

"Honestly, if someone had told me that joining the Foundation would one day have me posted as a door guard, I never would have let that Creius leave the Vigiles Bureau. But it's too late now. You, I, and all these fine Foundation employees will soon be standing at the door, plastering on smiles and shouting 'Welcome!'"  
"At the end of the day, this all comes down to Creius and those 'Hawks' aggressively pushing through the Shelter Expansion Act. Ever since the Timekeeper announced the 'Storm' countdown, refugees from all over the world have flooded to the Foundation's doorstep, surrounding headquarters with no end in sight. Those lines stretch so far they could probably wrap around the entire earth."  
"Who knows how loose the Foundation's screening criteria are this time. If you ask me, if even a handful of those veterans turn out to be spies, the Foundation could be blown apart from the inside in no time. Creius knows better than anyone what those soldiers are capable of, yet he's not the least bit worried about keeping them in line—just like he always has his ways of dealing with me and 'Blackbird.' And of course, you—his loyal little dog who doesn't need dealing with at all."  
"Fine, this is why he dumped us here. On top of standing guard and welcoming guests, we're also on riot control, reconnaissance, and counter-espionage duty—and at the critical moment, we might just end up digging a trench outside the building. Let's go, heads up. Time to rotate from patrol to the front door ... Stop it. I didn't mean for you to actually smile like that."

### 1130201 · Getting Worse

**Legends of the School Watchtowers**

[The last page torn from a book, treated with a visibility potion—the invisible ink now exposed.]

This afternoon I overheard the instructor slip up—she said something like "back when the school didn't have the watchtowers ..." What does that tell us? Those five towers are no ordinary structures; they were built for a reason! Our earlier suspicions just might be right!

Which one do you mean? The one about the towers being used to subdue ghosts? Or the one about locking up students who've gone mad?

Both! Just think—why would the school suddenly put up towers that serve no purpose and ban every student from setting foot inside?

Oh please. Maybe the name says it all—they're just there to watch us. The higher-ups have teachers on duty up there around the clock, holding a long-range lightning gun, just waiting for the moment you try to slip out ... and bang, you're down.

Even if there really are teachers on duty, I don't believe they can stay awake 24 hours a day. One of these nights we'll find our chance, sneak in, and figure out their secrets for ourselves!

Have you lost your mind? What if there are machines inside that can spy on students? We'd get caught in no time.

You're just a coward! We'll cover our faces. If something goes wrong, you run first, and I'll cover the rear. Just tell me—are you in or not?

Fine ... for the sake of our friendship, I'll go.

[A report lies beneath this "secret message," with a few sharp lines on the page.]

After comparing handwriting, we have confirmed that the two students responsible are fifth-years Andrea and Oliver. I will personally reprimand them and impose a one-week detention as punishment. Given the recent rumors circulating among students regarding the watchtowers, I propose increasing patrol frequency around the watchtower perimeter and strengthening oversight of the weather-stabilizing pigeons housed within to avoid any lapses during inspections from the Pax House.  
Regarding my mistake, I can confirm that I did not mention the "Storm" or that reverse incident during the first "Storm." I will submit a three-thousand-word written apology and reflect seriously on my failures in student management.

### 1130301 · Homecoming Day

???：Such a lovely day, isn't it?

???：These noisy children are soon going to be locked behind the school gate, and I will say goodbye to you.

???：This war will be over as long as I close my eyes. It has lasted far too long.

???：*sigh* Finally ...

What are you doing standing around? Aren't you heading to school? Go on then. Children your age are SUPPOSED to be learning and playing.

???：College then. Get yourself a medical degree or a doctorate at least.

???：Haven't you realized how precious peace is after coming back from the battlefield? Make your life count.

???：I'm going to take a nap. Now, go and find something to keep yourself busy.

???：Oh, please.

???：Take a look at that tree. I think it must feed on the escape plans of students like you; how else could it grow two inches taller every year?

???：I imagine it might even serve as something of a secret hideout for them.

???：But I wonder if anyone ever bothers to visit it again after they leave; likely not. A once beloved place all too quickly forgotten.

???：Do you know why?

???：Because they are too afraid to face the difficult truth.

???：That they wasted the most carefree days of their lives trying to escape from paradise into hell, and now there's no power in heaven or earth that can send them back.

???：You'll end up just as pathetic as them, unless you can go back there and prove your courage.

???：You won't have anything like the fanfare your French classmate received—she was proud as a peacock when she walked through that gate. But you'll not chicken out, will you?

### 1130302 · Perfect Exchange

**Improved Invisible Ink Formula**

[A note in childish handwriting.]

Find a way to replace the ingredients with things that are easy to get from within the school.

Ritual Offering—This sacrificial food is mostly made of flour. Maybe a handful of dry pasta will do the trick?

Roaring Root—Fortunately, this main ingredient can be found right here at school. Spotted a few growing near the tree roots by the lake last week. The spell calls for Milled Magnesia—some salt from the canteen should work.

Trembling Tooth—Theoretically these should be molars from small to mid-sized omnivorous arcane animals, but judging by size and pneuma content, the baby teeth collected by Ms. Tooth Fairy at the clinic might be the best option. I could take cold showers for a couple of days, then if I get sick, I'd have an excuse to visit ... but should I ask her directly or just quietly steal one ...

### 1130401 · Quantifying Pain

**The Foundation's Medical Record Archive**

Classification: High  
Patient Codename: Moth  
Medical Record:  
Examination reveals that the patient, having returned from the Antarctic Operation, performed repeated identity overwrites throughout their long-term undercover assignment, exceeding the safe iteration threshold. The patient's Phototaxis now shows multiple unstable segments, which may affect overwrite efficacy and increase the risk of personality collapse.  
Brainwave activity shows an increase in fast waves, indicating compensatory neuronal overload. Neurotransmitter concentrations are imbalanced: dopamine levels have dropped to 60% of the normal range, and serotonin synthesis efficiency has decreased by 28%. Physical damage is present in the medial prefrontal cortex, with mild edema in the limbic system ...  
...  
The patient also overextended her use of arcane skills during the Antarctic Operation, resulting in prolonged pneuma depletion. Recovery prospects are not encouraging. Strict limits must be placed on her arcane skill usage in the near term to prevent irreversible damage.  
During psychological evaluation, the patient exhibited mild self-perception disorder ...  
...  
Diagnosis:  
The patient's brain damage is largely within controllable limits. Although a deep personality overwrite should be performed upon completion of the undercover assignment, given the critical enemy intelligence and knowledge obtained during this infiltration of Manus Vindictae, an unconventional approach has been approved: the Phototaxis overwrite will be suspended for the time being. The patient will instead receive regular injections of neural repair peptides to maintain brain function. During this period, the patient's psychological state must be closely monitored to confirm the results of cognitive anchoring.

### 1130601 · Low on Supplies

**Running Out of Disks**

A Brief Overview of Teleport Disk Usage in the Current Era

1. General Situation  
Headquarters's teleport disk supply across all departments is critically low. As a single-use item with a limited capacity per teleportation, emergency rationing remains in effect based on current inventory. Priority is given to frontline and core mission units. Requests for additional supplies will not be approved outside of special emergencies or critical needs.

2. Cause and Background  
The only known source of the primary materials for teleport disks is located in the core conflict zone of the ongoing war. Extraction operations have been fully suspended, and supply routes remain blocked. As a result of these uncontrollable factors, teleport disk production has dropped significantly, inventory continues to decline, and resupply remains uncertain.

3. Conclusion  
Due to the disruption of material supplies, teleport disk stocks have entered a critical shortage. The Laplace Scientific Computing Center is actively researching substitute materials. Headquarters requires all departments to strictly adhere to rationing standards and optimize usage strategy. All combat objectives must be carefully evaluated, with disks reserved for critical junctures capable of turning the tide of battle, in order to manage current and future supply pressures.

### 1130801 · Temporary Product

**Wearable Equilibrium Umbrella User Manual**

The Wearable Equilibrium Umbrella is a compact version of the standard Equilibrium Umbrella with the canopy removed. It retains the core Hofmann Knot structure, converter, and harmonizer, and is equipped with a waist-mounted nylon buckle—lightweight, durable, and secure against accidental loss. Designed for armed personnel operating in the "Storm," it reduces the burden of carrying a full umbrella while significantly improving combat mobility.

Please note the following precautions during use:  
1. To ensure the stability of the Hofmann Knot, the device retains a glass casing to maintain optimal arcane conductivity. Although the casing has been specially reinforced, it cannot withstand heavy impacts. Avoid striking it with force or exposing it to sharp edges.  
2. As development time was limited, the rain-shielding effectiveness of this canopy-free design remains unverified, and distribution has been limited accordingly. Each person must carry and wear their own unit at all times. Sharing a single device between two or more individuals risks insufficient coverage and may result in serious consequences.  
3. Armed units are advised to request backup Wearable Equilibrium Umbrellas and a number of standard Equilibrium Umbrellas when submitting their application, in case of emergencies.

### 1130901 · 不变的变化

> The Everlasting Chaos

???：What's beneath your hat?

???：Just a head, no creepy black bones sticking out from your temples?

???：Thank goodness. I'm not sure I could handle another conversation with those lunatics.

???：I do hope, if you ever find a bone stuck through your brain one day, that you won't hesitate to see a doctor.

???：But why bother covering such a pretty face? You're young and good-looking.

???：Don't be so shy to show it off. If that boneheaded lunatic doesn't bother to hide her face, you shouldn't either.

???：But seriously, if you do find a bone stuck through your brain one day, don't hesitate to see a doctor.

???：I don't call that woman a lunatic without good reason.

???：So you say, but you know what happened to her: she died and came back.

???：That is a rare privilege. How many can say they had the same gift? Death tends to be terribly final for most.

???：She was the exception, and just look at what she's made of it.

???：Life is pain. Lately it seems like every bone in my body aches ... Worse, the pain seems to come no matter the weather.

???：But I wasn't rewarded with immortality for all my pains.

???：It seems unfair that a privilege like that should be given to such a troublemaker.

???：This whole damned world is coming to an end!

### 1131001 · "New Faces, Old Friends"

**Manus Vindictae Leaders**

[A flowing handwritten note; faint scale powder clings to the paper.]

Taira  
His true form is a crab that latches onto the faces of others. Through his arcane skills, he can control the movements of his host. The death of the host does not harm him. Always be wary of the crab.

Lyra  
She can control—or amplify?—emotions through song. Her voice is highly mobile, and her wind-based arcane skills are wide-reaching and difficult to guard against.

Ustrina  
The medium of her arcane skills is possibly a shadow in the shape of a sickle. She can use it to inflict mental damage, though her victims show no visible physical wounds or signs of pain. Evade the sickle, and use mental defense arcane skills if possible.

Hypothetik  
He takes the form of a giant stitched puppet. His arcane skills allow him to implant emotions, beliefs, or fragments of consciousness into the minds of others—most commonly fear. Counter him the same way you would against Ustrina.

İlmek  
No arcane skills have been observed, but her strength and endurance far exceed ordinary limits. Avoid engaging her up close.

Prima  
Skilled in hypnosis and torture, and known for her brutal nature. Her mother, Animus, was a Manus Apostle and died during the Antarctic incident.

...

No deeper contact has been made with the remaining leaders. Physical descriptions of suspected leaders are recorded in the appendix.

### 1131101 · Whisper of Flowers

**Fragrance from Afar**

[A flower carried on the "wind." From its heart drifts the scent of honey and the soft voice of a woman.]

Prima, Igor, and his Gryphon Legion have rescued the Foundation's support convoy. I need you to stall them for as long as possible—though slowing them down is all that's required.  
Don't ask. I never agreed to take part in this operation, and I still oppose the attack on the school. Pointless fighting and bloodshed do no good for the future of arcanists ... But I still have my duty to fulfill. Whatever comes, we must give everything we have to Her cause. That's all—I'm only here to deliver this message. Don't come looking for me. I'll be gone soon.

[The sweet fragrance and the faint voice fade together.]

### 1131102 · Secret Communication

**Burn After Reading**

[A quill grows hot without warning. Drawn from a uniform coat pocket and set upon blank paper, it begins to move on its own, leaving lines of scorched writing.]

SPDM is under attack by Manus Vindictae. I'm on my way to assist. The attack came without warning. Amid the Foundation's containment expansion, headquarters is crowded with unfamiliar personnel. I suspect Manus Vindictae's plans run deeper than this. Share any relevant intelligence if you have it. Fewer than three hours remain until the "Storm." Stay cautious.

[As the admiral reads the final word, the quill's glow dims. The writing catches fire along with the paper, consuming it in an instant, leaving not even ash.]

Dear and wonderful Ms. Nautika, Laplace Scientific Computing Center has taken a keen interest in your new body. We are writing in hopes that you will agree to participate in our experiments and research. Rest assured, we remain fully committed to humanitarian care and professional research ethics—which is why you are welcome to check off only the projects you are willing to take part in. The research projects regarding "No Longer Poles Apart" are listed below:

√1. October 13 - December 20, Research Theme: Biotic Force Field and Arcane Cell Compatibility in "No Longer Poles Apart"  
     Projects: Blood draw (a significant volume of blood samples is required—you are welcome to decline if you are sensitive to pain), lab analysis, Picrasma Candy sensitivity test, triathlon comparative study (humans as control group), epidermal cell stress test, unknown mucus DNA sequencing ...

√2. November 4 - January 29, Research Theme: Psychological Study of Self-Identity and Special Socialization Based on the Reconstructed Body of "No Longer Poles Apart"  
     Theme: Social anxiety assessment in the tiered lecture hall, avoidance-attachment relationship test in the auditorium (you are welcome to share your mythological archaeology), physical coordination test in Uluru Stadium, emotional and psychological clinical evaluation ...

√3. December 15 - January 30, Research Theme: Spatial Compatibility of the Photon Dynamic Holographic Dress with "No Longer Poles Apart"  
     Content: Human-form photon holographic dress design study, personal aesthetic preference research (we're very curious!), compatibility study of photon dress materials with mucus and bone, cell photosensitive spectrum determination range study, height measurement, weight measurement, arm—or wing—span measurement ...

### 1131601 · 行业竞争

> Competitive Business

???：*sigh* I'm surprised, I must say.

???：Why would anyone else want to talk to you in riddles the same way I do?

???：It's kind of a chore being this vague, to be honest. He has to be getting paid for this.

???：If only.

???：All I've received in compensation for our little talks is a dry tongue. I've already poured three pots of tea today.

???：Do you think you're all that interesting a conversation partner?

???：I'm not some retiree who has nothing else to do.

???：The only reason I talk to you is because you won't leave my house.

???：What else should I do? I'm not strong enough to throw you out.

???：My only defense is a razor-sharp wit, though it seems to never cut you deep enough to matter. I swear at times I think you've been glued into that chair.

???：*sigh* Never mind. Sometimes we just have to accept the little disappointments in life.

???：That old dust whines about the plaster figures intruding in his room. But that's all he's done so far.

???：If he wants to take over for me, do let me know. If it means not having to come up with any more riddles, I'll even let him sit in my chair.

### 1132201 · Pure White Folder

**A Hint of Unease**

Arcana Invasion Assessment Report

The following is a strategic analysis and operational assessment of the recent Arcana invasion:

I. Assessment of Strategic Intent  
Arcana's invasion was not aimed at territorial or resource expansion—it was a strategic probe. Her core intentions are assessed as follows:  
1. To draw out the Pax House and force it to expose its operational movements under pressure.  
2. To test the Core Guard's combat effectiveness and collect critical data on our core security forces.

II. Current Situation and Response Assessment  
The Core Guard has demonstrated loyalty and effectiveness, but its premature entry into direct combat has resulted in the following consequences:  
1. Our strategic cards were shown too early—the Core Guard's true capabilities and tactical characteristics may now be known to the enemy.  
2. Strategic initiative has been partially ceded. We will face targeted countermeasures going forward, while the enemy still holds options in reserve.

III. Conclusions and Recommendations  
This confrontation has allowed Arcana to partially achieve her strategic objectives. We recommend the following:  
1. Reassess the threshold for deploying the Core Guard to avoid depleting strategic reserves in response to low-cost probes.  
2. Apply tactical deception and upgrades to the Core Guard—projecting a false profile alongside the real one to mislead the enemy.  
3. Establish a tiered response framework that clearly defines the escalation order of the Foundation's defense forces, Zeno forces, and the Core Guard.  
...

### 1132401 · 追悼会

> Memorial Service

???：It seems that just about everyone has accepted that death must inevitably follow the rain.

???：But not you. How many loved ones have you lost? You keep count, don't you?

???：The same tragedy has played out over and over again ever since your "Acting Principal" first brought you into that stark gray building. Do you really think anything can be done for them?

???：It won't—not yet; this isn't the last golden thread you'll follow. More will come. More upon more until you can't see anything else.

???：Then you'll wind up just as blind as she was ...

???：But I don't say that to scare you. You're lucky. Someone will always be there to show you the way.

???：I've said it before: anyone who has forgotten their own name is as good as dead.

???：But that might not always be the case, if that's any comfort.

???：It's far easier to help someone remember what they've once forgotten than it is to bring the dead back to life.

???：But that just means the burden on your shoulders is all the heavier.

???：You'll carry that weight until it breaks you. So, why not just save the world instead?

???：Dead or alive—drag them all into that magical Suitcase. It'll save you some trouble, and you'll get to be the savior you've always wanted to be.

???：But if you can't save them all, the least you can do is remember their names for them.

???：The rain has fallen on some more than others; each time it washes something away. In the end, who they are and who they become will be up to you.

### 1132402 · Ambush Strategy

**Investigation Report from Ms. Stranger**

[Top Secret|Authorized by the Pre-Storm Dispatch Center & Ad Hoc Investigation Committee]  
[Case ID: ███-██-████]  
[Date: █-█-█]

Case Briefing and Jurisdiction Statement  
    This report documents the attack carried out by Manus Vindictae (hereafter "the Attacker") against Foundation headquarters and the SPDM (hereafter "the Targets") prior to the 11th "Storm." The investigation was conducted by the Pre-Storm Dispatch Center and the Ad Hoc Investigation Committee under the relevant clauses of the Emergency Defense Code of St. Pavlov Foundation to analyze and determine the attack's route of entry and the cause of the internal defense's collapse.  
...

III. Attack Method and Scene Reconstruction  
    The Attacker used an unregistered arcane array (technical codename: "Windweave") to bypass all surveillance and defensive measures at the target site, striking directly at the facility's interior.  
    Phase 1: A small advance unit led by "Lyra" arrived at the target site and began infiltration, lowering the guard of personnel inside through the following means:  
    1. False Support: A Manus Vindictae member (suspected to be "Lyra") used a false identity to approach multiple Foundation members and their close contacts, earning their trust. During the "Storm" countdown, she successfully manipulated several headquarters staff and individuals with prior refugee experience into following her instructions and opening access to the facility. Manus Vindictae members also used arcane skills to attack the personnel and facilities of the defense surveillance department, rendering the surveillance systems inoperative.  
    2. Defense Breach: Under arcane control by Manus Vindictae, captured personnel were turned and used as inside operatives, guiding the Attacker past the main defensive network and destroying the base defense systems, weapons stocks, and alarm systems.  
    3. Temporary Line: The advance unit and inside operatives set up the "Windweave" array within the target interior, completing preparations for the second wave of attack. Once the array was in place, the main force of Manus Vindictae arrived at the target ...  
...

IV. Witness Testimony and Evidence Record  
    Witness #09 Testimony (Engineering Department, Senior Researcher): "I saw Nancy Sur standing in the doorway. It wasn't her shift, so I went over to ask about it. She started saying things like, 'Such beautiful weather, and yet we're still here—history really did get it wrong,' and 'Rewrite the score, sound the anthem, welcome humanity's rebirth' ..."  
    Witness #47 Testimony (Human Resources Department, Clerk): "These traitors were among the finest when it came to justice and order ... but then again, maybe that's why they'd rather admit they were guilty than admit that surviving was just luck."  
...

### 1132502 · A Loss of Civilization

**A Debriefing Meeting Record**

Debriefing Record: Missing Items in the Display Room

I. Basic Information  
    This loss incident occurred in the headquarters display room and was triggered by uncontrollable factors, including the attack on headquarters. The affected items include one Italian portable typewriter from 1969, one set of 1980s disco music cassette tapes, one sword hilt of unknown age recovered from the lake bed of Snowdonia, and 42 era relics in total (see appendix "Lost Era Relics Inventory").

II. Timeline  
    Incident: During the attack, the display room was temporarily used as part of the defensive structure, serving as an emergency shelter and wartime storage facility. In the process, multiple areas of the display room sustained severe damage, and a section of the wall in the "1960s-1970s" exhibit collapsed.  
    Discovery: Following the attack, the logistics department carried out post-"Storm" cleanup in accordance with the "Storm" Clearing Protocol. During a subsequent review, the administrator cross-checked the era relics inventory and discovered that items were missing.  
    Response: That day, the administrator reviewed the arcane surveillance records and confirmed no entries related to lending, requisition, damage, or maintenance. The following day, investigators completed a preliminary value assessment and transferred the case to the internal debriefing process.

III. Cause Analysis  
    Logistics Department: Due to the lack of arcane value in the missing items, cleaning staff were less vigilant during the post-"Storm" cleanup process.  
    Arcanum Containment Department: The "Storm" Clearing Protocol lacks clear criteria for distinguishing between "era relics" and "historic debris."  
    Security Department: Manus Vindictae may have seized the opportunity to steal items during the attack.  
    Conclusion: The cause remains undetermined.

IV. Improvement Measures  
    Logistics Department: Strengthen the disposal prevention mechanism—for example, by establishing a "72-hour holding period"—and incorporate this case into the "Storm" Clearing Protocol.  
    Arcanum Containment Department: Strengthen the classification standards for the collection and put all non-arcane items in the display room under unified management.  
    Security Department: Strengthen the missing items tracking system and enhance security measures.

V. Recommendations  
    The tracking level for this batch of historical relics is classified as "Low." Arcane items (including those currently on loan and not yet returned) take priority.

Appendix: Lost Era Relics Inventory

## 章节 310

### 3100401 · 小径① 目所能及的雨

> Trail① Rain Within Reach

???：If you look at the ground after the "rain," you can often see a wet, long trail like this one.

???：At the end of the trail, there may be a snail slowly crawling forward. Yes, "forward." The snail marks the direction for us.

???：But, what if we don't see any snails at either end? How are we going to distinguish the direction to the future from that to the past?

???："Criterion," well said, darling. To figure something out, you always need to make deductions based on facts and logical reasoning.

???：What you see is not necessarily true, but what is logical is, because we haven't witnessed or experienced anything illogical so far. If something like that actually happens, it must be because we are too ignorant to understand it for the moment.

???：This point was firmly believed by those researchers who worked in the house like canned sardines.

???：It remained true to them until the first drop of rain rose. The sardines suddenly found that the knowledge they possessed couldn't support the point anymore. The can was opened, revealing a completely different thinking pattern to them.

???：Whatever they used to mend the can, there was still a crevice left between the inside and the outside, and it could be ripped open again at any time.

???：Oh, you just figured out how those researchers handled matters: face the ignorance and do everything they could to find the answers.

???：When the rain rose, they struggled to collect every component left, hoping to preserve the castle built by science. Unfortunately, the effort was in vain. Another wave washed away the castle and restored it to quicksand.

???：Even so, they still worked hard in that house like canned sardines to record what was happening, because they believed at least the can holding them was a safe place. Yet in the end, they found that the can was only another sandcastle on the beach.

???：Everything they were familiar with disappeared when the first drop of rain rose.

???：The chaos didn't last. If it really was the rain that swept away the snail and messed up time ... then, we should look at the silver lining.

???：Does it mean we will find out the truth of time once the secret of the rain is revealed? There has never been a shortcut like this one, since time is only an intangible concept.

???：So then, observation stations were built all over the globe. The staff learned how to collect and keep the valuable samples efficiently in no time. The little boxes they used to store the raindrops are still being upgraded even now.

???：Order and silence were brought back to the labs. Every piece of gear was placed back on the right track.

???：It was a grand feat, and no one could have accomplished it inside this huge, cumbersome machine except another machine. She took no sides and sought only predictable results.

???：That's why she made a decision as soon as she read that report and its attachment on her desk. The former was about a delicate component which flapped like a butterfly in that huge machine, and the latter was a piece of paper full of circles and dots.

???：Then she stored the information on her hard disk and destroyed the report.

???：And the paper which claimed to have recorded the locations where the "Storm" might happen—yes, the one with the butterfly-like drawings—was retained for data comparison.

???：She seems satisfied with the result.

COLLECTION OF SUSPICIOUS INFORMATION**

has very poor manners. He

### 11101031 · "Missing Person"

**A STATEMENT OF APPRECIATION**

{p}Klaus, the cutest bulldog  
of all London, and I were  
separated when we walked  
by Kensington High Street.  
I looked for him in many places,  
but he was nowhere to find.  
When we got home, a handsome  
border collie showed up at our door. In this good gentleman's mouth there was Klaus' collar, and our Klaus was standing next to him.  
It would have made us very happy if this clever black-and-white border collie stayed longer with us. Sadly, he left our house quietly when we went to check Klaus.  
We hope to find this dog and his owner, and send them 10 extra large boxes of "Chewing Fun" cookies as a thank-you.

Please let us know if you know them. Thank you!{img}

乌卢鲁运动会的体育项目清单。

**List of Uluru Games Sporting Events (Provisional Version)**

Arcanum-Themed Events

[Track and Field]

The use of arcane skills, such as vaporization, blink, and flying, is permitted.

Potions and special vehicles are strictly prohibited. Requires implementable drug testing procedures to be designed. For example, random sampling before the start of the competition and standardized drug testing procedures for the top six athletes in each event after the competition.

The world record for this event was set by Carlos Castillo, the Cuban flying mouse, in 1924. By the time of his retirement, he was still unable to break his own record.

Mr. Pavia once surpassed this height in training—I witnessed it with my own eyes! I'm really looking forward to seeing him perform out on the field.

The individual event rules will provide more detailed information on this situation to avoid any potential complications regarding the referee's decision-making duties.

[Combat Events]

The competition venue needs to be set up next to a medical station.

Guardrails and warning lines need to be placed around the arena to prevent excited spectators from entering the field of competition and disrupting the competition schedule.

Competitors must tame strange water beasts, mount them, and race them. Druids will be allowed to participate.

Algae needs to be planted at the water area's endpoint to maintain the physical condition and hemoglobin index of the beasts.

### 15103002 · 《叮咚快报》周末特刊

> The Nagger Weekend Special

来自本地报刊的恶意造谣。

**The Nagger Weekend Special**

Exclusive Exposé: Revival Squad and Bunny Girl Cause Commotion at City Hall! Civil Servants Suffer Mental Breakdown Due to Extreme Intimidation

The Uluru Games are on the verge of revival, igniting the flame of competition in arcanists everywhere.

I'm sure many of you have heard the news already—perhaps you've even passed by the "Revival Squad" in the street, led by that precocious female student—and needless to say, everyone is very curious about them. They are, of course, a flame, a ninth-grade student, a fungus researcher, and a prominent public figure backed by the entire St. Pavlov Foundation, making for a motley crew fascinating enough to make anyone's heart burst with excitement.

Just like the Bunyips, who have been seen lurking in the city's dark alleys recently, these denizens of society's dark underbelly have crawled out of the sewers and thrust themselves into the spotlight, and are fast captivating the entire nation's imagination and attention.

"Following closely behind a bunny girl, a mad scientist, giant brown mushrooms, and Pixel Emu in tow, does the Melbourne shuffle by the fireside."

Where could you possibly hope to see such a scene? In a science fiction movie? At the circus? In your dreams, perhaps? No—this all happened at Alice Springs City Hall. This scene of pure absurdity was also the first impression a bystander (who has asked to be referred to here as "Ms. Kate") had of the Revival Squad.

We... We have undertaken all manner of large events and functions, all on behalf of guests with etiquette and a sense of propriety. But they... they're just too much! They... Oh my goodness... My upbringing doesn't permit me to repeat the things they did, please understand."

The aforementioned chaos wrought by the Revival Squad completely destroyed "Ms. Kate's" day. When recounting this experience, she choked back tears multiple times and even broke out in incoherent sobs.

Let us return to that glorious event and reflect on what our ancestors had to say about the "Uluru Games." Do we really need these games? Or did someone tell you that you need them to awaken the love for sportsmanship and fair competition lying dormant in your heart? What is the point of them? Selling tickets? Is this just another case of sensationalism gone mad?

Go and take a look at the page you just tore out of your calendar this morning—as we stand at the end of the century, who truly wishes to exhume those long-dead and decadent traditions once again? Whose pockets are being lined with the money from these tickets that seem to be on sale everywhere you go?

—Yes, we have all the answers you've been looking for!

Reporter: Makower  
                                — Nagger Weekend Special Edition #136

Australian Nagger — Your personal private eye!

### 15103003 · 《澳大利亚生物行为学报》

> Australian Journal of Biological Behavior

生物学期刊的研究报告。

**Australian Journal of Biological Behavior**

A Study on the Effect of Changes in Water Temperature on the Bunyip's Lie Detection Mechanism

[Experimental Samples]

1. Twelve adult Bunyips, numbered YP-01 to YP-12, each approximately ten feet long, divided into two groups, A and B, with six in each group and with a balanced gender ratio.

2. Three human volunteers, numbered V1, V2, and V3.

3. Two large enclosed aquariums capable of accommodating a total of twelve Bunyips.

4. Three color picture cards placed in a sealed, unlit box depicting apples, bananas, and pears, respectively.

[Method]

1. Place the 12 Bunyips from groups A and B into their corresponding aquariums while ensuring freshwater quality and oxygen quantity are consistent. Ensure that Group A's water temperature is set to 14 degrees centigrade and that Group B's is set to 32 degrees centigrade.

2. Direct V1 to enter the room and select one of the picture cards from the sealed box at random without showing it to the two groups of Bunyips.

3. V1 then describes the selected fruit on the picture card to the two groups of Bunyips. This description can be true or false. To avoid the personal traits of individual volunteers affecting the results, the voices of the volunteers must be altered and the volume unified.

4. Record the physiological responses of the two groups of Bunyips, including dorsal fin vibration frequency, heart rate changes, and body temperature.

5. Replace the volunteer and repeat steps 2-4.

6. Determine the authenticity of the content described by volunteers via hidden camera recordings.

7. Compare the differences in physiological responses to true and false descriptions between the two groups of Bunyips.

[Results]

1. When volunteers provided authentic descriptions, both groups of Bunyips remained calm.

2. When volunteers provided false descriptions, dorsal fin vibration frequency and heart rate in both groups of Bunyips increased significantly.

3. Both groups of Bunyips could sensitively distinguish between the behavior of humans when lying and when telling the truth through physiological responses, and the higher the water temperature, the more sensitive the Bunyips became.

[Conclusion and Prospective Optimizations]

The individual YP-07 in Aquarium B showed significant physiological deviation in its responses. Through retrospective analysis, we found that regardless of whether the description provided by the volunteers was false or not, YP-07 was more likely to believe that the picture cards drawn depicted bananas. Whether this is because the individual concerned prefers bananas or because the high water temperature affected YP-07's judgment in a way that made YP-07 more willing to believe that the fruit on the card was of a tropical nature has yet to be determined. Follow-up research will expand and optimize the current experimental setup based on these two assumptions in order to study the correlation between them.

### 15103004 · 《热土运动编年史》节选

> Excerpt from Indigenous Sports

一张本地体育杂志的扉页。

**Excerpt from Indigenous Sports**

Running tracks, ball courts, and lawns—these places are where key memories in an individual's journey of growth are born.

At every moment—every second throughout history—people have been competing, running, striking, and jumping somewhere in this land. It was through friendly competition and childish games, throwing stones and tree seeds, that children learned the basics of hunting. As they grew older, they would climb tree trunks to pick wild fruit and hone their nimble movements and footing. The youth walked upon redwood branches, placing stones on their knees, then sought to catch the most dazzling sparks from the fire.

"Tree seeds, trunks, and branches. It is from the top of a eucalyptus tree that our lives can be seen." This is the old saying that accompanies the tradition of the arcanists.

Arcanists journeyed through dense forests and rivers, hiking to reach the belly of the earth—Uluru.

### 15104004 · 研究笔记

> Research Notes

一页关于白乳菇的研究笔记。

**Research Notes**

Name: White Milk Mushroom

Its introduction in the guidebook states that it is also known as the spicy milk mushroom. Is it really that spicy? I don't think I want to find out.

Discovery: This mushroom was discovered at the foot of the Dandenong Ranges, in the shade beside a stream. To prevent it from drying out, I placed it in a cloche and brought it home with me.

External Appearance: The cap is semispherical in shape and has a diameter of approximately 3 centimeters. Its appearance is very unique, with soft gills and a cap and stem as white as snowflakes. Unfortunately, it only grows at the foot of the ranges, not on their sometimes snowy peaks. Beneath its cap lie many small spore sacs, which, when opened, reveal spores of a creamy hue.

Tissue Sample: I cut off a small piece, and when observing it under a microscope, could see that the pileus was tender and the curved hyphae were thick and wavy or annular. The structure is very similar to other milk-caps, with differences mainly reflected in color and mycelial morphology.

Function:

1. Edibility

Being a wild mushroom, it can be eaten raw or used as a cooking ingredient. Even after being sliced, its milk-white juice retains its inherently spicy flavor, stimulating the appetite and thus making it suitable for use as a seasoning.

I'll take good care of it—whether for research or as an emergency food reserve, should the need arise.

2. Medicinal Value

White milk mushroom extract boasts anti-inflammatory and antioxidant pharmacological properties. Its polysaccharides and peptide components are used in anti-inflammatory drugs, and their inhibitory effects on tumor cells are also used in adjuvant therapy for malignant tumors. White milk mushrooms can enhance the body's immune system and enhance athletic performance. *Does this mean arcanists can use it as a nutritional supplement?

I'll make a note of this—I need to gather more data.

3. Environmental Indication

The white milk mushroom is highly sensitive to its growing environment; thus, its growth status can provide a preliminary reflection of a forest's ecological health. This has caused it to become one of the ecological indicators for environmental assessment.

Humidity, nitrogen, and pH level are all indicators that correspond to the growth status of white milk mushrooms.

*This was an oversight on my part—I only recorded the growth location but forgot to test the environmental indicators. Treat this as a lesson and be more careful in the future.
