### 15105001 · A Work Log

**A Work Log**

When they gave me this civil service job, I never thought I'd end up working with an emu one day.

Or perhaps I should say that when I was drowning in those irrigation project application forms I couldn't finish last month (there are always so many applications I can't seem to finish), I never thought an emu formed of pixels would be of any use to me...

But... But "Plappy" really is something else. I'm not even sure if that's its name; I just heard that girl with the ponytail call it that.

It's quiet, strong, and has a strangely trustworthy air about it, so when I noticed it flipping through unapproved forms and trying to help with them, I didn't think to stop it.

To be honest, and I'm not sure if I'm just imagining this, but I think I can sense an aura of guilt radiating from Plappy.

It's like the kind of guilt a mother clad in a polka dot skirt feels after her child's soccer ball smashes through a glass window—the sense of guilt felt by a grandmother wearing a woolen beret after her grandchildren ride their bicycles over a beautiful flowerbed. Anyway, I can always count on it to bring me a basket of golden apples, appearing at my door, then bowing down—all the way to the ground.

I know this all seems a little over-the-top, but whether it's true or my imagination, I have managed to calm down the "Revival Squad" formed by those rambunctious children.

Oh, that's right! I mustn't forget to return Kate's backpack. She left it at my house when she came to the housewarming party earlier. She took her sick leave immediately after and won't be at the office for two weeks straight. I saw her today—she looked good and seemed to be preparing for an interview... It's strange... Who would want to interview a civil servant? Maybe I should ask her the next time I see her.

???：They're Schneider's sisters. It's hard for the Grecos to tell them apart. But Schneider is different.

???：But Schneider is different. She was born in an earthquake, all covered in blood, and didn't let out her first cry until two hours later. It wasn't until she was one year old that her father noticed that she had never been baptized.

???：She's Schneider's biological mother. You see her furrowed face, like a hard stone wall, while inside the wall is all about a pious farmer's life.

???：The Grecos are not good at dealing with gang affairs. They can't even figure out when their daughter has grown—grown into the backbone of the whole family.

???：When Schneider took out 500 dollars from her pocket and put them on the table, they were completely shocked, and that's just the beginning.

???：Schneider ... Schneider, the youngest daughter they seldom cared about. It's impossible to keep every child well-fed. Schneider could not even get a piece of bread in the Eucharist.

???：But a good daughter would not let anyone worry about her. She sat on the bench outside the church and hummed. She found a way out for herself. She walked to the underground market, fascinated by what she saw …

???：She announced her "new identity" one day.

???：The identity you know now.

???：In the process, she lost something old but gained something new, no matter if she wished for it or not.

???：The bread provided in some sacred religious ceremonies. According to different teachings, it's sometimes flatbread, sometimes fermented bread, or sometimes any kind of bread.

???："Now," Mrs. Greco said, "we live, or we die. If Schneider comes back safely tonight ... we will leave when the moon rises."

???："The doors are closing in front of us, one after another. But the benevolent Maria will give us the ultimate shelter. My children, remember today forever."

???：Her words speak for her status. But Mrs. Greco's eyes never moved away from that small Madonna on the table. The hem of her clothes were soaked in tears.

???：4 p.m. A family is determined to start a new journey.

Slicky was also remarkably unprofessional in this instance. Did they really think that printing "Employee Ticket" directly on the front of their counterfeits would make people think they were actually real? Moreover, the workmanship is of laughable quality.

January 21st, 1991

Little Scissors Jerry: 240 Tickets Seized

An increasing number of fake tickets are floating around on the market, and all I can do is try my best to confiscate as many of them as I can to prevent them from making their way into the hands of unsuspecting spectators.

The gangs have already put a target on my back. I need to make sure I draw less attention to myself next time.

## 章节 18101

### 1811402 · 加快脚步

> Walk faster

加快脚步

Resident I：We should take these components to the appropriate comrade. With my years of experience in mining, I can assure you that they are still usable and will be put to good use.

Resident I：Maybe they contain traces of runium, explaining their pristine appearance. In these times of steel shortages, every little contribution counts ...

Resident II：I imagine Comrade Vila is with the children right now, making sure they don't miss their lessons.

Resident II：Could it be that the miners have returned to the abandoned mine? Maybe we should take the shorter route to the hotel and let Comrade Evgeni investigate.

Resident I：Alright, let's do that.

...

Resident I：Shh, see those people walking in front of the hotel? Are they from Zeno? Let's not talk about them behind their backs.

Resident II：Yeah, I see them. Isn't Comrade Evgeni negotiating with Zeno? They must be looking for him.

Resident I：They left. It looks like Comrade Evgeni isn't there. We'll have to come back later.

Resident II：Tomorrow or the day after, perhaps.

...

Resident II：Honestly, this is not the way I want to leave this town.

Resident I：Ugh, I still have the scars from shoveling snow on my legs, and we just fixed the windows at home. Who wants to leave just like that?

Resident II：But what Comrade Evgeni said was reasonable, and he is always truthful ...

Resident I：You're afraid he'll hear you! We may be close to the meeting room, but—

Resident II：I'm not afraid, or I wouldn't have made the decision to come here. We weren't afraid in the pioneer days either. What Comrade Vila says is exactly what everyone said back then.

Resident II：The town has been built, and we have been living here for a while now. Our days are not as hectic as they used to be, but it is invigorating to see the resurgence of the pioneer spirit after such a long time!

Resident I：Hey, alright! That's the attitude we need! If we walk a little faster, we can still make it back to work on time!

### 1811501 · 分歧点

> Point of Divergence

分歧点

Bertolt：Hello, gentlemen.

Resident I：Hello, comrade of Zeno.

Resident II：Comrade.

Resident II：If you're here to see Comrade Evgeni, we can wait a little. Or is there something else important you'd like to discuss?

Bertolt：We talked, and Ms. Windsong was there.

Resident I：Comrade Evgeni is doing what's best for everyone, we all know that. But I'll tell you, I personally don't want to leave this place. Even if he ...

Bertolt：No, the lady turned down my proposal. And Comrade Evgeni didn't seem too invested in it either.

Bertolt：Isn't Zeno's help good news for all of you?

Bertolt：I can't comprehend the reasoning behind rejecting an abundance of food, a suitable climate, and comfortable housing.

Resident I：It's not easy to leave something you've built with your own hands, comrade. I doubt you've ever experienced living in a home you built yourself, let alone living in this way!

Resident II：Chances are you have never even touched freshly mined ore in your life!

Bertolt：That's true, gentlemen. But why do you insist on doing everything yourself?

Bertolt：It seems that our conversation has come to an end. I will stay here until Evgeni arrives and gives an answer, which should be soon.

### 1811503 · 昨日的滋味

> The Taste of Yesterday

昨日的滋味

Patrik：Identical long tables, matching chairs, and potatoes, potatoes, and more potatoes.

Patrik：In the past, we had access to tin cans and even jam and flour. It was a challenge to transport supplies, but fortunately the weather was cold enough that nothing spoiled during the trip. Time was not an issue in those days, we had an abundance of it.

Patrik：The little ones used to come and ask for Alenka. While we couldn't indulge their every whim, a treat now and then was fine. Being too strict does no one any good.

Patrik：Had I known the true value of Alenka, I would have been more careful about giving them away. Maybe I could have kept a few. Oh well, too late to regret now.

Patrik：At first, we gathered around a single table to portion out the canned food, meticulously sopping up every last drop of broth. But now, there are more tables, but a lot less variety in our meals. And it's not like we can go hunting for critters to supplement our meals.

Patrik：Why can't people live off ore like they do?

Patrik：I have an urge to give a detailed account of my past and present experiences because Rayashki is exceptional; it is the most remarkable town I've encountered, although my travels have been limited.

Patrik：But I'm worried that it will one day disappear.

Patrik：That's for sure. If Comrade Evgeni agrees to Zeno's proposal, this place will soon be a land of snow again. The factories, the machines, everything will be covered with snow, just as it was when we first arrived ...

Patrik：I have to remember to write down all the details in case the kids ask me. I want to be able to tell them about everything that disappeared. Of course, it's not going to be written down in any textbooks ...

Patrik：There are countless unsung heroes in Rayashki, a place filled with ordinary people just trying to make ends meet. That's the reality of our lives here.

### 1811601 · 被留下的

> A Heartfelt Legacy

被留下的

Knut：You're here, Comrade Vila.

Vila：Comrade Windsong and Comrade Raisa helped me take the children home; it took some time.

Vila：What did you find?

Knut：Comrade Evgeni once mentioned that he kept some things in the cabinet in the meeting room.

Knut：He said to take them out when the time was right.

Knut：...

Knut：Here. This is all of it.

### 1811604 · 针脚与面包

> Needles and Loaves

针脚与面包

Raisa：Comrade Vila frequents this place, always looking great in her poneva dress.

Raisa：I taught her how to sew, and other comrades would bring piles of torn coats and socks for us to mend. This is something I have always done.

Raisa：No matter how catchy our slogans are, we still need clothes to wear and bread to eat, right? The rest is not within my realm of concern.

Raisa：Thankfully, Comrade Vila has been diligently caring for the children. My main worry was that once the teachers were gone, there would be no one to check on them and ensure they were looked after.

Raisa：Leaving children to run amok on the streets will not lead to their healthy development.

Raisa：I'm very grateful that she's here.

Raisa：...

Raisa：This place has become a second home to me. Despite the challenges and endless tasks, I feel content knowing that our efforts benefit our fellow comrades.

Raisa：This experience is priceless to me. Anyone who has ever sat at a long table, sharing butter and conversation with people from different countries and working together to form friendships would understand the value of this way of life.

Raisa：It has a powerful effect, my friend. It reminds me of dark bread - rough, but filling.

Raisa：Whether it is Comrade Vila or ... Comrade Evgeni, I trust in their abilities. This town will not simply disappear.

Raisa：Rayashki will always be here, steadfast and unwavering.

### 1811605 · 广场聚会

> Town Square Meet-Up

将一肚子的话儿都倒在这里，别担心它们结冰。

**Side by Side**

Miners, covered in a layer of dust and wielding their picks, hurry over as others join them after washing off their bandages. In the square, some take shelter under the statue and read books, while children are led by loving hands through rows of coats and boots. A strong sense of camaraderie unites everyone in the square; they are all part of the same tight-knit community.

The conversations begin and spread like a hurricane, sweeping from one end of the square to the other as everyone tries to engage with those around them. But then another voice joins in. The town's teacher has arrived, embodying the ideals we hold dear. She is like a character from a legend who has come to our town from a faraway place. Because of our strong will, even legends can become reality here. If the teacher had not said, "See you tomorrow!" this would have gone on all night.

As a community, we each have a voice in shaping the future of our town. It's inspiring to see so many people come together to make a difference. There is a sense of pride that radiates from within, uniting us all towards a common goal.

Oh, how powerful it is when we come together as one!

### 1811701 · 一如既往

> Same as Before

一如既往

Elena：I've always believed it! We can dig through the frozen layers and find ways to survive here. Even when the harsh northern winds gnash their teeth, we can overcome any challenge!

Elena：Zeno will have its own specific guidelines that I doubt I'll be able to adapt to, but just like this particular component, we are already refined and capable enough!

Elena：Yes, we can live on our own without relying on other leaders.

Elena：...

Elena：Let me be honest, I am not much of a fighter. The only tools I know how to use are a wrench and pliers. But when push comes to shove, I will make do with whatever I can get my hands on. We need weapons to defend ourselves, and we are not afraid to use them.

Elena：It is crucial for children to explore the world. I came here at a young age, on a whim with a friend. We had no experience in shaping workpieces or handling raw materials. Now, my skills are highly valued in this community.

Elena：We did not come to Rayashki to have fun. They've got it all wrong.

### 1811801 · An Instant

Knut：I stumbled across some components. Lately, it seems like everyone is looking for them in their spare time. These little things can definitely come in handy.

Knut：The little thing was hidden just behind the base of the statue. It seemed that our resilient bear had found a new home here. The old statue had to be moved to make way for it, but its base remained. I suspected it had left some of its components behind.

Knut：Comrade Evgeni ... he did not notice the changes in Rayashki, but change is inevitable and ongoing. He was still holding onto memories of when things were tough and rough.

Knut：In those days, this square didn't even exist. We painstakingly dug it out with shovels and carried each stone ourselves.

Knut：No one expected Rayashki to end up so beautiful. We were never ones for making plans or dreaming big; we simply rolled up our sleeves and got to work.

Knut：Now, Rayashki might change once more. What will it become? Everything is changing so fast. We have a pool and a school; it all seems to have been built in the blink of an eye.

Knut：As soon as a project is completed, the difficulties and sacrifices made along the way are quickly forgotten. The immense sense of accomplishment makes us believe that all the hard work was worth it.

Knut：I don't want to leave. I'm afraid I'll change faster than this town did. Those who come back later feel like strangers themselves.

Knut：It's incredible, isn't it? How things can change in what feels like an instant ...

### 1811802 · The Factory Spirit

Adrian：Factories, ores, and Rayashki!

Adrian：To me, Rayashki seems almost like another name for Runium. Perhaps it is because I am the one responsible for sifting the ore.

Adrian：While most people came here with grand ideals, I simply signed my name on the list without much thought. It wasn't long before I found myself stepping off the train and into this unfamiliar land. Fortunately, aside from the wild Critters roaming about and the stubborn terrain, life here has been relatively simple.

Adrian：Regardless, it's all laborious work. And I understand that even Critters have to eat and survive. I forgive them.

Adrian：But back to the here and now. The factory is finally complete, and we have surpassed our wildest expectations. These accomplishments are not gifts from some higher power, but of our own making!

Adrian：These are true miracles, not bestowed by any divine entity but created by our own hard work and determination. Just take a look around!

Adrian：There was a time when I felt nothing, just going through the motions of everyday life. But now I finally feel like I am making a difference. It may not be extraordinary, but it's more than most people achieve in their lives.

Adrian：Yes, I couldn't have done this alone. It's a rare opportunity to find others willing to work alongside you, swinging hammers and building something meaningful together.

Adrian：I hope to turn our experiences here into a legend, like the stories of Comrade Evgeni. With a little exaggeration, of course. Young people need these legends to inspire them.

Adrian：So let's raise a glass to the new factory and all that we have accomplished here together!

### 1811803 · A Promise

**Vibrant Snowy Square**

Take another good look at the square. It hasn't changed much—a polar bear stands in the center, outlined by a faint light, while children eagerly reach out to touch its paws. The snow dusts the bear's head, giving it a serene quality that contrasts with the lively setting. Flowers bloom amidst the white snow, covering most of the stones on the ground. The bear appears to be at peace against the mustard yellow background.

This is an important place in Rayashki, as the town kitchen cannot accommodate such a large number of people. It is often used for public discussions and will continue to serve that purpose. Recently, it was decided to allow children to venture outside and explore the world beyond. The road leading there is bathed in sunlight, decorated with red ribbons that sway in the gentle breeze from the trees.

People find familiar images here that evoke nostalgia and memories of past lives, yet those who come here rarely speak of their homeland. This is the true essence of Rayashki—everything they hoped to see in their homeland, but never did. It's like the distant horizon that Rusalka dreams of as she braves the icy waters, or the intricate illustrations created by traveling ley hunters, or even the homes of Foundation visitors who have long since disappeared. It's what previous generations promised the next: a glimpse of their idealized homeland.

The more impossible an ideal seems, the stronger it becomes—unbreakable in its perceived perfection.

Despite this, there is still so much to strive for and achieve in life.

### 1811911 · Damp Paperslip

**Lost Export Plan Found at Port**

Raw Coal:

Commonly used as a fuel for combustion. Suitable for heating and power generation. High priority for direct export (but can cause resource waste and air pollution).

Coal Gasification:

Can be converted into clean fuel gas for synthesis (related technology and facilities are expensive – consider collaborative production methods).

Coal Ash:

Produced from boiler combustion. Can be recycled for use in road construction. No need for export.

Coking Coal:

Important material for iron smelting (perhaps the ironworks could be rebuilt?).

In addition: It is reported that there is technology to produce refined coal after it is washed, and there is a high demand for refined coal in the south. Should be prioritized.

What is the source of this report? Who could have predicted that we would uncover a coal mine, or that we had been to the south?

—Comrade Evgeni listed more than a dozen common uses and export directions for energy in his letter, and we found one that we could use.

### 1811915 · Hey, Tomorrow!

Illia：Kiril, it's time to get back to work! Please remind Bogina to come down from the forklift and stop playing Sudoku. We have to deliver the wood for reinforcing the mine roof by tonight.

Illia：Oh, Knut! What brings you here? It's not yet time for the shift change.

Knut：I'm not here for the shift change. I'm actually just dropping off some parts and a toolbox we found. Take a look, it's fully stocked.

Knut：I couldn't help but notice Comrade Kiril standing off to the side, spinning in circles. It almost made me dizzy when I first arrived.

Illia：He's been acting like this since the lunch break started, while Bogina is engrossed in her usual game. I wonder what's going on with Kiril ...

Kiril：Illia, you haven't had the chance to read Comrade Evgeni's letter, so you don't know.

Knut：...

Kiril：Looking at the stains on my hands from the coal dust, I can't help but think of his letter again. I find myself pacing back and forth, imagining how my comrades will lay me to rest when my time comes. It would be a great honor, but also ...

Kiril：I can't help but feel that Comrade Evgeni deserved a different fate than this.

Kiril：He did not deserve to end his journey in this place.

Illia：Kiril ...

???：Comrade Knut was the first to find the letter. Later we all took turns touching it, like passing around a flag, remember?

???：You shouldn't talk like that to Illia, and you shouldn't think like that about comrade Evgeni.

Kiril：But you said the same thing earlier, Bogina.

Bogina：Yes, I said that. But I also said—

Bogina："Comrade Evgeni ended his journey here, but we must go on."

Bogina：With only one square left to fill in the Sudoku, it's clear that this is the only way to go.

Bogina："Keep moving forward, to a better tomorrow."

Illia：This is not the end of our journey, Comrade Kiril.

Knut：Right ... I may not be good with words, but I wholeheartedly agree with comrade Bogina.

Knut：This must have been Comrade Evgeni's true wish.

### 1811921 · Snow

**Snow and Other Possibilities**

To the town, she is a familiar figure. But to herself, everything is so drastically different.

She's used to walking in the snow. The poplar fluff floating through Moscow's streets reminds her of Rayashki; it's like snow, but it doesn't make her cheeks turn red or her hands and feet cold; it doesn't melt on the skin, nor can it hide anything. Canned goods, even sausages and fresh meat, are available in stores, and one doesn't have to count the days just to exchange for everyday items ... The first time she made soup in her new home, it felt surreal. Another vivid memory was when she got off the train, holding her child's hand in fear. Outside the small town, in Moscow, there were so many people, all seemingly full of hope.

But she boarded the train back to Rayashki anyway.

Rayashki had changed, too. There were faces in the town she didn't recognize, and the statue in the center of the square had changed. The school was still the same, and the children played games in the small playground. The townspeople's greetings were a bit awkward; no one mentioned her hasty departure as if it was not important, as if anyone could leave at any time and also return whenever they wanted.

Snow fell on her hair and collar, turning her fingers and nose red—this was real snow.

### 1811922 · The Path Ahead

Vila：It's good to see you again, Comrade Ulyana. I heard that you have returned.

Vila：Did you get a chance to go to see the children? They've been going on about you for so long.

Ulyana：It's been a while since I've been back to this town. How are the little ones doing? With you watching over them, I'm sure they're doing fine. You've always been such a caring teacher.

Vila：But there is only so much that one person can do. It would be great to have another comrade to lend a hand.

Vila：So, will you stay this time, Comrade Ulyana?

Ulyana：Right. I wanted to talk to you about that ...

Vila：We can take our time to talk, no need to rush. By the way, did little Vasya come with you?

Vila：Now that our supplies are fully stocked, we can take the children to the square tomorrow for some sketching ...

Ulyana：It's just me this time, Comrade Vila.

Ulyana：...

Ulyana：Back in Moscow, my thoughts kept returning to Rayashki. Those who have chosen to stay here are incredibly determined and tenacious. They are genuinely sincere and kind, but they are very stubborn in their beliefs. Does that make any sense?

Ulyana：In Moscow, you don't have to worry about cold temperatures or lack of resources. If children need pencils, they can simply go to the nearest store and buy them.

Vila：That sounds like a dream come true! We're doing everything we can to make Rayashki just that.

Ulyana：It was really beautiful, but when I sat next to Vasya's bed, when he was fighting his asthma, with the poplar fuzz in the air ... And then children's gravestones and coffins, so tiny and fragile, almost like toys ... I could hardly believe they were real.

Ulyana：I clung to the hope that life would get better in Moscow. I believed that if I just endured and persevered a little longer, things would turn around.

Ulyana：But that was not the way it turned out.

Vila：My sincerest condolences, Comrade Ulyana.

Ulyana：It's alright, I brought it up myself. When I was in the house in Moscow, I couldn't stop crying. But eventually I realized that I had to pull myself together and do something.

Ulyana：Is it still possible for me to reunite with everyone? Looking back, I regret leaving in such a selfish manner.

Vila：The future is unknown and unpredictable. All we can do is take one step at a time on the path that lies ahead of us.

Vila：You will always have the ability to move forward on this path, with all of us.

### 1811923 · Welcome Home!

Vila：Don't all crowd around us at once, kids. Give us some space so that Miss Ulyana, your teacher, can greet you, okay?

Nina：Hooray! We're super happy you're teaching again, Miss Ulyana! Tee-hee!

Pasono：Miss Ulyana! There are soo many new things in town that I can't wait to show you!

Pyotr：Me too! I know this town like the back of my hand, and I've got a sneaky shortcut to the mine to show you!

Ulyana：Of course, my little bears, my patient little comrades.

Avgust：Here. I received this as a gift from the square, and I want to share it with you. Vila always tells us to share with others.

Ulyana：What a beautiful sunflower. Thank you, Avgust. Say, you seem much happier now.

Avgust：I notice that my mom and everyone else around me are smiling, so I can't help but smile too.

Avgust：You're smiling too, Ulyana.

Ulyana：Haha, yes. I, I really missed you all.

Vila：Aw, don't rub your eyes with your hands, kids. Here's some handkerchiefs for you ...

Vila：It took you kids a long time to come up with that welcome. Don't forget to actually say it to Miss Ulyana.

The Kids：—Welcome home, Miss Ulyana!

Ulyana：...!

Vila：The welcome and the celebration, all arranged on the same day ... Rayashki is slowly gaining momentum again, but there's still so much to do. I hope you don't mind.

Ulyana：No, not at all. How can I be of help? Just let me know.

Vila：Would you mind taking care of the children for a while? I have to go check on the port soon, and some friends from the Foundation are coming over for their regular lectures. Please make sure they are held to the same standards as our agreement. And finally ...

Vila：Welcome home, Comrade Ulyana!

### 1811931 · Toward the Distance

Nina：I've finished folding it, Comrade Pyotr. It's time to make our paper planes fly. Can you move a little? Just a bit!

Pyotr：Alright! Let's see whose plane can fly the farthest!

Nina：Three, two, one!

Avgust：The swallow dives into the snow pile. Pyotr wins.

Nina：He hasn't flown his yet, Comrade Avgust, maybe ...

Nina：Hey, Pasono, are you not going to play with us?

Pasono：... I've been thinking about what Miss Vila said.

Pyotr：Are you scared? Don't you want to go out and see? After all, our parents agreed, and adults always know better than we do.

Pasono：I'm not scared! But... Miss Vila said she will not go with us, neither will Miss Ulyana.

Avgust：Vila has a lot to do. After the slow turtles left, many new friends came to town. Vila wants to talk to them.

Nina：I don't think we need to worry so much. The teachers have allowed these friends of the Foundation to attend our classes, play with us, and take notes ...

Nina：They don't seem like bad people. If they were bad, Miss Vila would shoo them away.

Pyotr：That's right, Pasono! Even Avgust isn't as anxious as you are!

Avgust：I'm not nervous, I just want to learn how to transform things. Like turning a sunflower into a towering soldier or a bear with round ears ...

Avgust：We could turn snowmen into us to keep Vila company. Mom always said that when we miss her, we should build a snowman.

Avgust：—And Vila will definitely miss us when we're gone.

Pyotr：Yes, we should leave a gift for Miss Vila! Let's go make some snowmen!

Nina：Alright!

Pasono：Yes, let's find the prettiest twigs to decorate them with!

Pyotr：Let's go, everyone. Split up to find materials and come back as quickly as we can!

### 1811934 · Children's Creation

**Circle Dance**

Snowmen of various sizes were arranged in a circle on the playground, and the children lovingly linked their twig arms together so that the snow friends were holding hands. Each child focused on decorating their little creation, staying close together, and when one arm brushed another's shoulder, there was a cheerful laugh.

The little girl on the far left lowered her head, her braids curled up like haystacks in the sun. She carefully placed sunflower petals on her snowman's head. Nearby, two boys were trying to make their snowman's chubby body wider and sturdier so it would last longer and Ms. Vila could greet them more often.

The little boy's snowman was a bear. It was only through his explanation that the head and paws of the arctic fox could be seen on its back. Foxes don't usually have long ears like rabbits, but he gave it those in order to hear his mother's footsteps, Vila's footsteps, and his friends' footsteps. Finally, he carefully placed the mined ore on the fox's chest, just as he wore the beautiful stone his father had left him.

"Okay, my frosty comrades! You can all stay here and do the circle dance with Ms. Vila. Remember, no running around or making a ruckus; just listen to her instructions ..."

### 1811941 · The Workers

**Caring Crowd**

Among the long tables sits a group of people.

The one in the middle maintains her usual politeness and friendly smile. If you weren't looking at how her hands are resting uncertainly on the table, you'd miss the emotion beyond the politeness. Yes, this young scholar is stunned that the theory she has been focusing on is back in the spotlight. After being relegated to the pile of old papers in most places outside, it has found a place in Rayashki.

As for the people around her, most are still wearing their work clothes, with coal dust clinging to their collars and pant legs. They are aware of this and keep their distance from the scholar until she reaches out to them. This group of makeshift teachers and students shake hands, determined to spend time understanding a fading discipline. Those most dedicated to the work are also the most serious pragmatists. The study of ley lines has played such an important role in the rebuilding of the town! When the scholar first entered the research institute, this is what she had hoped for. Is the study of ley lines omnipotent? No. But it may eventually bring about change.

Perhaps it will be the moment the coal mine was discovered, or perhaps it is this moment.

### 1811944 · To the Horizon Line

Windsong：Wow. I'm getting a little nervous.

Windsong：There are a lot of new faces here, quite unexpected. Well, hopefully a midday vodka will calm my nerves.

Windsong：May I have your attention, please? Comrades and visitors, welcome to today's lecture on the study of ley lines.

Windsong：I am Windsong, your speaker for today.

Windsong：Now, I will present some practical examples on how the Study of Ley Lines can be applied to the mineral development in Rayashki ...

...

Laplace Researcher：This is undoubtedly beneficial to our research. Who cares about other people's opinions? Personally, I find Ley Lines very useful and relevant.

Laplace Researcher：We definitely need to try this out and tell Ms. Lucy about it. I have no doubt she would be intrigued by this type of presentation ...

Laplace Researcher：I'll take thorough notes. I'll be prepared in case there's a Q&A session later.

Vila：Hello, there!

Laplace Researcher：Uh? Oh. Hello, ma'am.

Vila：If you need a seat closer to the front, I can take you to the visitor's seats over there.

Laplace Researcher：No thank you, I'm not a typical guest like them. I'm here as a researcher.

Laplace Researcher：Is there somewhere I can hear more clearly? Does Ms. Windsong have any other business to attend to? I have a list of questions written down, such as methods for identifying lines ...

Vila：There will be a Q&A session after the presentation. Follow me and we'll see if there's a better location for you that's closer and more spacious.

...

Windsong：Phew, I really need a drink. This is as tiring as any physical labor.

Windsong：I hope the Q&A session won't be met with dead silence. Just in case, I have prepared a couple of basic talking points ...

Vila：Comrade Windsong, here.

Windsong：Perfect. This glass of water couldn't have come at a better time.

Windsong：The lecture went smoothly, and I still have time to make it to today's performance. As I promised to Avgust and the others, I have a little trick with ley lines that will surely amaze them.

Vila：The Study of Ley Lines is much more than just a little trick, Comrade Windsong. We all know this to be true.

Vila：The woman from Laplace has technical inquiries for you. You might want to finish your drink, I have a feeling she has quite a few questions lined up.

Windsong：She can ask all she wants about the subject. The truth is, I'll be researching Ley Lines for a long time to come.

Windsong：What are your thoughts on this lecture? I welcome constructive criticism, so feel free to share your honest opinions, Comrade Vila.

Vila：It brought back memories of a "line" I had seen before, although it's not exactly the same as your ley lines.

Vila：One morning I was floating on the surface of the water, admiring the distant shore. The horizon line seemed impossibly far away, with a soft glow radiating from it.

Vila：At that moment, I decided to go and see for myself. And so I arrived at Rayashki, my footprints leaving marks in the snow as I watched it transform into what it is today.

Vila：... It's remarkable how our dreams are gradually becoming a reality.

### 2010201 · Cosmic Zen

???：Let's rewind the time to a few years ago.

???：It's for your own good. Lying in front of you is a girl full of mysteries. She makes your mind spin like a top. You scratch your ears, trying to understand what happened exactly.

???：And all these damn things can be traced back to a long, long time ago, when the monkeys decided to stand on their feet …

???：Awesome! Your lofty ambition that a boxer would have is exactly what we need. But unfortunately, I can't take you to the future. Oops, I could, but now I can't.

???：Now, another person is going to do this. But before we solve this big mystery, I think it's necessary for us to sort out the principal contradiction—

???：Trust me, you won't regret listening to this story.

???：I will never be offended by your humor. Okay. The past is waiting for us to look back.

???：But I will never be tired of you, baby.

???：I respect your decision. When you're ready for a story, go find your friends. They will tell you exactly what happened.

???：... While I will wait for you at the next destination, humbly and patiently.

???：It's winter in the early 20th century. The gloomy rain never stops. The square on West Jackson Avenue is alive with people. There is an Italian Renaissance-styled basilica. You rarely see so many people get together without making a sound.

???：This is a black requiem mass. The priest is chanting the requiem, and the people mark a cross on their chests.

???："He was a father. He dedicated to a sinner of ... Lord shall give him eternal peace." A woman in a black robe turns to you and whispers.

???："What is power?" A solemn lady shakes her head at you, "Only Lord can forgive us for our sins and judge the right and the wrong, but he does not want power."

???："Neither does the father." "He was our father. He dedicated to a sinner of ... Lord shall give him eternal peace."

???：She turns back. Explaining this makes her unpleased. More and more people are queuing for the funeral. The square is alive with chants here and there.

???：The Grecos are among them. They're covered by the dark cloud of long-handled umbrellas. Soon, their voices are replaced by whimpers. You can hardly tell whether they are pleased or sorrowful. But you can't find Schneider.

???：Look in the direction of Mr. Greco's broken left palm. Yes. Look from the bandage-wrapped end at his cuff.

???：3390 feet away from the crowd, in the shadow of the church, stands the girl you want to see.

???：She is pale and thin, as if she has just recovered from a serious, long illness.

???：But her fist clenches, her eyes burn, and her figure is an open defiance of the ubiquitous chants. You don't know why she looks so—so furious. Is she ... what, 11 years old?

???：And the mass is about to usher in the ultimate climax. It rains heavier. The priest opens his arms to embrace the sky, "The Lord be with you."

???："And also with you." Schneider responds in a voice that could hardly be heard. She puts her hand on her heart. This is the first time she responds to the Lord. And it will be the last.

### 2010208 · Paulina's Diary

**Paulina's Diary**

I got a very cute stuffed animal for Christmas once when I was five years old, I think. Oh, if I remember correctly, there should be an old photo! I was sitting next to the Christmas tree with snowflakes on my head and a big grin on my face. Next to me was a particularly well-behaved little bear with a tail longer than any other teddy bear's. It was incredibly cute and I took it with me wherever I went.

On every sleepless, hungry night, on every night my parents fought, on every night when the cold was unbearable, I'd hug it tightly because it was so warm and soft. As long as I could hold it, everything didn't seem so bad. It was so important to me; I always thought it was my first and last friend.

Until the day my mom held my hand and led me, nervous and fidgety, through the doors of the Velvet Restaurant.

It was then that I realized I might have something just as important as the bear in my arms—a delicious cake, a soft, thick blanket, a home that sheltered me from the wind and rain.

Today is the eighth Christmas of my life, and unlike past years, I received another carefully prepared Christmas gift. Even though I know that the bow on the wrapping paper wasn't tied by the big guy himself, I'm still very happy! He gave me a blue scarf, saying it matched the color of my eyes. Considering how pretty the scarf is, I'm willing to forgive his cluelessness in not knowing blue from green. But who asks someone what they want for Christmas next year? No sense of romance at all.

Of course, I already know what I want for next Christmas! A bigger, warmer home that can fit a bigger family, one that's even bigger than Haight Street!

???：Suddenly, a certain jinx woke me up, saying, “I have no trouble. Don’t come over.” How wonderful! I was like a stray dog sleeping on a square. You’ll see, this old man is going to shovel all his complaints into your pure, innocent ear.

???：How do you feel about the structure of the Foundation? I can see your cockily raised neck, showing your attitude of rejection. Well, perfect! Let’s start with this intriguing topic, then!

???：The Foundation is a mess. Listen up, it drifts along the chaotic swirl between order and disorder, enticing countless idealists and realists to come over.

???：The “Storm” exacerbates the chaos. Those arcanists who were snorkeling are now surfacing—You see, Ms. Z is a great example. She is even sitting at the same conference table with the human elites.

???：The undercurrents, however, are still lurking. Natural Resource Committee, Mankind Caucus, Culture and Education Department ... Their members are hard to differentiate given the intricate backgrounds. They bustled for benefits and mired in countless trivial matters.

???：You see? You need to divide them into groups, and divide and divide ... until you break a huge entity into small groups, as small as possible. That’s when they are able to clean the vomit on the living room floor effectively and emotionlessly!

**A Brand New Age**

As the New Age Market awakens,  
And the Order of Enlightenment fades into obscurity,  
Embrace the raw, unadulterated cosmic energy,  
This is not a mere faith, but a true knowing.  
Release the shackles of societal norms,  
Savor the liberation of self-reliance,  
You need not conform to any doctrine,  
For peace shall guide us with celestial grace,  
And let love and the universe be our compass.

### 2010222 · The Pursuer and the Pursued

???：Ping, pong. Ping, pong. Ping pong ping pong ping, pong. Ping. Pong.

???：Yup. Agreed.

???：It's too noisy. You were looking at the little ball made of plastic. It hops from left to right, making an annoying sound.

???：But all we can do to it is look at it, just like all you can do to me is tolerate my existence, for both of us have nowhere to go.

???：Yes, an imitation show. A ball imitation show, little thing.

???：You're imitating the Messiah, while I'm imitating an orange ball made of plastic.

???：Being pushed back and forth by two rackets, I have nowhere else to go. Once they apply a force on me, I can't help bouncing to the sky. You can see how the air flows across my dry, wrinkled skin ...

???：Oh, what a coincidence! Aren't you in the same situation as mine now!

???：Ha! Look at your frowning face—

???：Your face wrinkles from the eyebrows to the nose tip, but people can barely see those light furrows. We know what that means. You have a poker face, and it's never your fault.

???：But for now, your face is not the thing that matters. Well, maybe for someone it is, but still, it's not.

???：You know what really matters?  
The game! Yes, it's the game, of course the game matters!

???：Everyone loves the game. They gather at the stadium, yelling or roaring vehemently. They choose one team to become its fan and spend good money on a team uniform or an autographed ball.

???：Yet, it's just a ball. You see where I'm coming from?

???：Just a ball. Its outline, a circle, could be found in any geometry textbook and anywhere in this world.

???：When the first hominid picked up a coconut and threw it to the sky—and caught it—and threw it—and caught it—and threw it again, when she felt joy and yelped, had she ever thought of the future?

???：The future where a simple ball has become so complicated and enchanting, now we call it the present.

???：Hahahahahaha! Yes, sure! Complicated and enchanting!

???：Use your silly and smart head to think about this ball game carefully.

???：The complex scoring rules, the harsh requirements for reactive agility, the countless possible foul points ... Your fingers, your wrists, and arms, a correct way of using them will lead to victory!

???：What matters more is, like every ball game, it focuses on how you serve the ball and hitting it back.

???：It's not an easy job as it sounds. When you are in the game, you need to concentrate. Where will the ball come from, and how will you return it to your opponent—you need to figure that out within half a second.

???：Use your power in a proper way, move your feet in a stable pace, inhale and exhale at the right timings.

???：Ping. Pong. Ping. Pong. Ping. Pong. Ping. Pong ...

???：The sound will last forever and ever until that bouncing little thing falls to either side of the court.

### 2010223 · Small Goals, Big Goals

Ostrich Radio：Good afternoon, San Francisco! Good afternoon, all you wet-behind-the-ears punks out there!

Ostrich Radio：Let's skip the rest of the intro because what comes next is out of this world!

Ostrich Radio：The Hubble telescope is in good working order, and scientists estimate that it could orbit the Earth for another 40 years.

Ostrich Radio：In light of the recent Challenger disaster, this is some much needed good news!

Ostrich Radio：But you might be thinking, what does a busted telescope floating outside the atmosphere have to do with you?

Ostrich Radio：Well, take a look at that magnificent sun of ours! Doesn't it remind you of a sparkling disco ball?

Ostrich Radio：Doesn't it make you want to party it up on Mercury, Venus, or any other planet out there? Let your imagination run wild, my listeners!

Sputnik：I do! I do! I want to fly out of the atmosphere and be a disco ball that lights up the Earth!

Nurse：Please keep quiet when visiting patients!

Sputnik：My apologies …

Becket：It turns out Madam Sputnik has a big dream—that's good news.

Gio：The cost of going to space would make even the filthy rich gasp, give it up already.

Becket：Can you not be such a killjoy?

Sputnik：Ladies and gentlemen, please don't argue, and no need to worry about my dream. I've actually been saving up.

Sputnik：In about five years, I should have enough! And with any luck, I'll even get an employee discount!

Gio：Saving enough to fly to space? An employee discount?

Becket：... Does a disco ball get such great benefits?

Gio：... Do they really?

### 2012213 · The First Companion

J：What's a mannequin doing all the way over here?

J：Look at you, shouldn't you be in the window of some fancy store?

Mannequin：Greetings, sir.

J：Whoa, you can talk? Wild, didn't see that coming.

Mannequin：I suppose so, yes … It is rather unusual. I'm still getting used to it myself.

Mannequin：Sir, as you may have noticed, my arm is currently stuck in this pile of trash. Could you kindly lend a hand and help relieve my pain?

J：Sure, buddy, but the next time you ask for help, drop the phony politeness.

J：Is this what you are looking for?

Mannequin：Exactly! Sir, the feeling of having all four limbs free is wonderful. How can I repay you?

J：Tell me how a talking, moving mannequin ended up in a trash heap—that's what I'm curious about.

Mannequin：Ahem, kind sir, storytelling is not my forte. This tale, I'm afraid, could take three days to tell, and it may be far less interesting than you think.

J：I'll be the judge of that.

Mannequin：In that case, we shall begin with last Christmas ...

J：You really are gonna talk for three days, aren't you?

J：I have no intention of standing around while you do. Come with me.

J：I know of a great restaurant where we can chat over a meal and maybe even help you find a new job.

Mannequin：Thank you, sir. You truly have a kind heart. May I ask what I should call you?

J：Joe, but the folks on this street call me J. And you? What's your name, my mannequin friend?

Pioneer：Pioneer, sir. I think people will call me Pioneer from now on.

Market Diviner：So as long as they line our pockets with cash, that's all that really matters.

### 2010121 · “新纪元集市事件”述职报告

> "New Age Market Incident" Report

越危险，越褒扬——赞颂你的敌人，亦是赞颂自己。

**"New Age Market Incident" Report**

Relying on the help of Ms. Maich, a citizen of the New Age Market, the support team successfully entered the "Underground Market." During the investigation, smoke from a fire suddenly rose from a forest 1.3 miles away from there. At the site, we found an injured Foundation investigator (Matilda Bouanich), a critically injured adult male arcanist (Joe Brown), a male human corpse (Harvey Hollick), and a female enemy suspected of belonging to Manus Vindictae (identity unknown).

This woman's mystical abilities far exceeded our expectations. The spells she used were extremely dangerous and difficult to understand. We were engaged in an unprecedentedly fierce battle ... Fortunately, we can confirm that the battle with Matilda Bouanich, Joe Brown, and their group had significantly drained her energy; otherwise, we might have faced a deadly confrontation ... After a second round of attrition, her arcane skills weakened, and with the help of the people summoned by Joe Brown, we gained an initial advantage. We planned to carry out a feasible capture plan to extract important information about Manus Vindictae. However, Manus Vindictae retreated much faster than usual, leaving behind a few mindless followers who hindered our plan. Additionally, due to the fog and some unknown rare incantations, the tracking arcane skills we had prepared in advance were rendered ineffective at the site ...

"Razor" Squad

Reporter: Todd Smith

## 章节 21101

### 2110101 · Tales and Journeys

**The Hitchhiker's Column**

Since our last column, we've been inundated with letters, tapes, and voice recordings from our passionate readers. I must say, the impact of the "Piranhas in the Sewers" story exceeded my expectations. One anonymous reader compiled 23 different urban sewer predator legends she picked up during her travels and asked when I was going to write about "The Country Witch Who Turned Into a Hare." She fondly recalled how her grandmother's amusing witch stories, tinged with a touch of gentle fright, would lull her to sleep as a child.

I'd like to extend my heartfelt thanks to this reader—these contributions are invaluable to my writing, and I'll soon delve into these childhood tales. Stories of mysterious transformative arcane skills are truly captivating, and their prevalence might be more widespread than I initially thought.

As we continue with the last issue's theme, sewer legends intertwine with conspiracy theories. For example, in a tape sent in by a Mr. K from New York City, a relative of his staunchly believes that the government secretly hired a team to feed albino fish living in the sewers. In previous investigations, a teenager shared his thrilling experience—he encountered human-sized, upright-walking reptiles and rodents in the city sewers. These stories often end with the line, "The government knows they exist, but the higher-ups are keeping it all hush-hush ..." More radical speculators suggest that city sewers can easily be used as experimentation sites, with piranhas, alligators, giant snakes, and all manner of other dangerous creatures being secretly bred by the government or mysterious organizations for unknown purposes.

...

In our next column, we'll be returning to Texas—where this series began. As you read this, I'll most likely already have set off on a new adventure. Perhaps our paths will cross on the dusty roads of Texas.

### 2110102 · Periodic Report

**St. Pavlov Foundation Report**

Report Number: XXXXXXXXXXXXX

From: Field Investigation Team Leader Andreas Sylvester

To: Office of the Vice President, St. Pavlov Foundation

Dear Madam Vice President,

After a month of surveillance, my team has identified several Manus Vindictae agents operating in the southern United States. At least three have been traveling between Dallas, Austin, and Houston; they are likely establishing information and supply channels for the Order of Enlightenment. Several others are active along Route 77, frequently using aliases and disguise potions to keep their exact identities and numbers hidden; however, at least five false identities have been confirmed. Please find detailed information on these agents attached to this report.

I believe these findings further substantiate our hypothesis that the Order of Enlightenment is planning to carry out an unprecedented arcane ritual.

Additionally, informant reports indicate that a spy known as "Ms. Grace" is active in Texas (see attached document). We await your directive on whether to pursue further surveillance of this individual.

May the peace be with us.  
  
Field Investigation Team Leader Andreas Sylvester

Reply:

To the Field Investigation Team,

Thank you for your diligent efforts in the pursuit of peace. The aforementioned intelligence has been documented.

Regarding "Ms. Grace," I am fully aware of the situation. Given the unknown intentions of this spy, I recommend a strategy of non-interference. Allow her to proceed in her actions for further observation.

May the peace be with us.

Vice President Constantine of the St. Pavlov Foundation

### 2110104 · Small Town Tales

**Leather-bound Diary**

February 5th

Well, ain't that odd? Pierce came back early this morning, scared out of his mind. I had never seen the kid so freaked out. He sped through town and only slammed on the brakes when he got to the front door, as if something was chasing him. That nut almost ran over Marita's little bulldog!

Only God knows what he ran into.

February 15th

The birds were chirping the whole night long. I had never seen so many of them gathered together. Oh boy, they were so noisy; I barely slept a wink.

Ruth was scared, she thought it was some kind of omen. What a joke.

Those long-beaked birdies weren't afraid of shotguns, that's for sure. I fired a shot at them, but only a few got spooked enough to fly away.

Father Morgan suggested hiring a "bird control team"—if such a thing exists in the big city, that is. But I still think we should assemble all the gun users in the town and kill all those birds once and for all, rather than scare them off.

March 27th

Had a nightmare, a very vivid one, though I can't for the life of me remember what it was all about now.

Ruth woke up scared too; I comforted her. It was just a nightmare, nothing more.

March 30th

Pierce didn't come back from Houston, but the police sure did. They said Pierce's employer in Houston reported him missing, thinking he ran off with their money.

I wouldn't put it past that sly fox.

April 10th

All night long, the birds were making that racket again. When I pulled back the curtains in the morning, I thought I was still dreaming, but it was real. There were so many birds they were blocking out the sun.

It was like a waking nightmare.

April 12th

The birds scattered.

Everyone looked kind of strange. It had been ages since I saw Marita walking her dog, and Ruth started bawling over the most trivial things. It's unnerving.

April 17th

Johnny's back in town, looking like his soul got swallowed up by the devil. I saw him running, screaming like a madman, then he just collapsed on the lawn all of a sudden, crying and begging for something to spare him.

Father Morgan came, but he had this strange look about him.

I heard what Johnny was saying. He was mumbling on and on about a "motel."

May 13th

I talked to a realtor. Yeah, it's come to that—I'm finally thinking about moving.

Why? I don't know. I guess you could call it a ... gut feeling? Maybe my mind can be eased a bit somewhere new.

### 2110106 · 20th Century Miracles

**Experiment Report**

Methods

Through the aid of voluntary donations, the research team extracted a nucleus from a female arcanist (Genetic Mother A) and an enucleated egg cell from a female human (Genetic Mother B). The nucleus was cultivated and processed to enhance its receptivity to new genetic instructions. After injecting the donor cell nucleus into the enucleated egg cell, an electric pulse was used to fuse the nucleus with the egg cytoplasm, initiating cell division and forming embryonic cells. During this process, the ratio of arcanist to human genetic material was adjusted to be nearly equal.

In Experiment No. 277, the embryo developed well, meeting the criteria for transplantation, and was implanted into the uterus of a Finn-Dorset ewe after formation.

Observations

0 Months Old

The subject was born through natural delivery, with 60.05% arcanist genetic material and 39.95% human genetic material, not achieving the expected complete balance, but still proving that the adult somatic cells of an arcanist can be programmed and adjusted through cloning technology.

In appearance, the subject exhibits characteristics of both Genetic Mothers A and B; simultaneously, for unknown reasons, its head is highly similar to that of a Finn-Dorset, but brain development has not been affected.

2 Months Old

The subject's development is slower than that of contemporary human and arcanist infants. Mild symptoms of lung infection have been found.

4 Months Old

The subject's development is slower than that of contemporary human and arcanist infants, but it showed significant responses to music and human language, with consistent reactions to specific sounds.

The cause of the lung infection was identified; the subject's lungs tested positive for the JSRV retrovirus, commonly found in sheep living indoors for extended periods. It was suggested that a veterinary team be brought in for consultation.

6 Months Old

The subject's physical development is slower than that of contemporary human and arcanist infants, and may stop developing prematurely.

Sterile room staff reported that the subject began consciously mimicking communication behaviors, as expected. Educational classes and moral guidance videotapes were initiated.

The lung infection worsened, and the subject showed a strong allergic reaction to chemical fiber products.

...

10 Months Old

The subject can engage in rudimentary communication, exhibiting good brain development.

Multiple tests have confirmed that American country music is effective in alleviating the subject's allergic reactions, the cause of which is yet to be determined.  
...

22 Months Old

The subject performed excellently in communication and intelligence tests, possessing the intelligence level of a 7-year-old human child.

23 Months Old

Bone tests indicated that the subject's physical development will cease completely by the age of 12.

### 2110107 · Chance Interview

**UTTU Special Interview**

Tuesday: An interview, huh? Well, I do declare, that is new ground for me, especially being interviewed by a child like you.  
Tuesday: So, what do you want to know?  
Barbara: You're no stranger to the realm of fear, and undoubtedly hold a unique perspective on the concept. That's why I want to hear what you have to say.  
Tuesday: You're a curious little thing, aren't you? I wonder ... is the rest of your flock this nosy? Look, darling, for me, fear's just a little hobby. Like, some people enjoy fishing, some like brewing their own beer, and ... some even like combing the knots out of sheep fleece—no offense.  
Barbara: None taken. Please, go on.  
Tuesday: Appreciating fear takes finesse ... you have to ease into it gently, like nudging a glass along the edge of a table with just your fingertip.  
Tuesday: Then, you hear that nice, crisp—clink.  
Barbara: So, you see yourself as a fear facilitator, not a direct participant, correct?  
Tuesday: You're a smart child.  
Tuesday: How do I put this ... It's more like the scared are scaring themselves. Those poor lambs will do the hard work for you, you see. Their imagination is my greatest tool.  
Tuesday: People start to make choices for themselves after experiencing true fear, based entirely on their imagination and of their own volition. And that's the most interesting part.

### 2110110 · Tuesday's Motel Incident Investigation Report (Excerpt)

**Tuesday's Motel Incident Investigation Report (Excerpt)**

Subject of Supervision: An unknown arcanum entity, at the current stage identified as a living consciousness. Commonly referred to as "Tuesday's Motel" (hereafter referred to as the "motel").

Level of Self-Awareness: Medium to low, lacking gender, age, or other cognitive attributes, but capable of responding to certain human linguistic cues.

Arcane Skills: Abilities include but not limited to influencing cognition, creating illusions, generating enclosed spaces, and creating aggressive arcanum entities.

...

Tests indicate that the subject responds to Tuesday's voice and actions approximately 60.15% of the time, and can follow certain simple commands such as "defend," "allow," and "forbid." It is less responsive to researchers and exhibits a certain level of aggression. Tuesday denies manipulating or training the "motel," claiming that their relationship is one of "nurture" or "partnership."

According to Tuesday, the subject was discovered in the Texas countryside, dyanmic in its form and size, currently mimicking the motels along Route 77. The subject's size correlates with the amount of fear it absorbs.

The subject can create tangible arcanum entities that implant illusions in affected individuals and cause real damage. Witness records show that these entities can exhibit characteristics associated with popular North American myths and legends, including but not limited to Bloody Mary, the Mothman, and poltergeists.

...

Handling Recommendations: Given Tuesday's influence over the subject, it is recommended that the "motel" is kept under Tuesday's stewardship, but Tuesday must be monitored at all times. Alternatively, consider transferring them to Team Timekeeper.

### 2310102 · A Human ... Report!

**Between the Pages**

- What's this?

- Rubbish. Stop poking around.

- Rubbish? Ain't this a book? Don't ya want it? Mind if I take a butcher's?

- You wouldn't understand it.

- Hmm ...

- You're right, I don't understand it. So, what's it about then?

- Don't you have anything better to do?

- I ain't done any of today's chores, I haven't taken that package to me sister, or helped the lady next door with her little one like Mum asked ... but I don't wanna do any of those things. I just wanna stay here a little longer.

- ...

- I ... I-Insects. "Insects ... are ... herbs ... with leaves, and ... purple flowers." Maybe it's an adventure book? About the adventures of a little creepy-crawly and its friends. Can I draw a story for it inside?

- ...

- Can I?

- You just don't stop do you? Can't you find someone else to entertain you, or maybe I just have "mug" written all over my face? Fine, do whatever you want with it, stay here, for all I care. Just leave me alone, understand? Leave me alone!  
- I can do whatever I want with it? Thanks, but I don't have a pen, do you ...

- Here. Draw. Bog off.

### 2310103 · Guide on Emergencies

**Two Alternating Handwriting Styles**

- "Do not touch any plants with barbs."

- Barbs?

- The thing I had to pull off of your leg yesterday.

- "If a plant cuts you and the wound turns purple, or the blood isn't bright red, knock on my window."

- At any time? Even if you're dancing?

- At any time.

- "In the evening, the Critters will lower their top buds to the ground. Try not to step on them."

- Got it! So, can I touch the top buds?

- Do whatever you want. Just don't come crying to me when they bite you.

- "Stop squeezing in through the damaged parts of the fence. Just use the front gate."

- Righto! Didn't know I weren't supposed to ...

- I'm teaching you Arcane Skills so you can fly, not crawl.

### 2310104 · That Thick, Stubborn ...

**Notes on a Weather Chart**

- It is half past ten in the morning, yet even by the window, the page cannot be read without a lamp. I set the journal by the sill, lit the lamp, and returned to find it layered in fine coal ash.

- Best to refrain from keeping the windows open for now.

- On the Thames, visibility is so low that feeding the swans is a hopeless task. I hear their mournful calls but cannot see them. Their wings beat against the smog like warnings to retreat.

- Records suggest fogs like these are unusual for this season.

- Cases of tuberculosis are on the rise. While I have no firm statistics, the overcrowding in hospitals and sanatoriums tells its own story.

- By tea time, visibility's improved slightly. The bus lamps barely pierce the fog, their faint glow the only evidence of their passage.

- Not good. Now my chest is starting to burn too.

- At the hospital, there's talk of a "Black Fog" capable of flattening buildings. Supposedly, one hospital was claimed in such a disaster. A tiny tornado? No, didn't look like it. Either this is a macabre joke, or someone should call up an arcanist to investigate.

### 2310105 · Life's Essentials

**Life's Essentials**

Lubricating Ointment

Snail mucus, acacia sap (scraped), swiftlet saliva, vulture feather powder.

—Vulture feather powder is hard to come by, and from the looks of it, it is not an essential ingredient.

—Swiftlet saliva, however, is absolutely required.

—Lanolin snails yield the best results.

Sleeping Draught

Hairy agrimony and elm leaves (crushed), oranges (juiced), winter bear's paw (fur dried in blue twilight), coarse salt.

—Remember to buy hairy agrimony seeds Bought.

—Too much salt drastically affects both the taste and sleep induction.

Special Pain Relief Balm

Olive oil, Ead's tongue (soaked in cold water), petrified resin (softened), white crystal, dragon bone powder

—Dragon bone? For everyday use?! Of course I can afford that! I'll just trade up fifty bottles of regular balm for one meager ounce of dragon bone powder, why bloody not? It is such a bargain!

—Alternative substitute: ... Or, just use fifty bottles of regular balm and endure the pain until it subsides.

This victory not only crowned her this year's champion but also broke a two-century-old record, making her the youngest winner in the event's documented history, with the previous record being set in 1802 by "Greenfingered Anna," who was 13 at the time.

The O'Hagan lineage is steeped in the traditions of ritualistic Arcane Skills. Charlotte's great-grandfather, Douglas O'Hagan, who famously once entered the hidden stone gate at the Giant's Causeway, and authored the arcanist text The Gate of All Gates: Rituals of Unlocking and Summoning. ### 2310107 · Research Log

**London Air Pollution Auto-Detect Cleaner Research Notes**

Improved Suction Device - Third Test

Unsuccessful again. Not surprising—a mere cleaning device cannot possibly hope to replace the role of the Fogwalker lineage. Amplifying the family's Arcane Skill may hold some promise, but ... we've only three weeks left.

Truth is, my goal is not to solve the unsolvable mysteries of the industrial era—I simply need to ensure that breathable air is available to all for the event's duration. That means faster filtering, broader reach, larger capacity—just for a short period of time, not forever.

Arcane Skill Boosting Device - Fourth Test

I could be off the mark on this one. Boosting the potency of arcane skills showed very limited effects. This line of thought failed to meet expectations, miserably so. It was a bloody waste of time!

Air Pollution Purifier - First Test

Thanks to the St. Pavlov Foundation for their ritual inscription techniques, the device works!

I was on the right track, somewhat. The Fogwalker's Arcane Skill may be irreplaceable, but this essentially doubles the number of hands we've got. By inscribing incantations onto a device like this, it's like having a duplicate of myself, working in tandem with me. Sadly, I'm not capable of supporting more than one of these, and the purification efficiency is still subpar ...

Air Pollution Purifier MK. III - First Test

A complete catastrophe! I'm a fool, an idiot, a nincompoop! Curse it, curse it!

Advanced Air Pollution Purifier - Third Test

What an astounding discovery by the Foundation! I've never heard of enchanting arcane skills onto everyday, ordinary items before; their developments in this area are revolutionary. Though I lack the expertise to perform true enchantment, following their instructions to combine specific tools has improved my Arcane Skill's integration into the device! And by integrating components of human technology ... I've achieved success! I shall name this creation the "London Air Pollution Auto-Detecting Purifier."

London Air Pollution Auto-Detect Cleaner MK. III - Seventh Test

Phenomenal! Maintaining the purification output puts an extraordinary strain on me, but at least it is sustainable for several hours. I pray that tomorrow's demonstration goes off without a hitch.

### 2310108 · The Foundation's Note Slips

**Unofficial Work Logs (Not Suitable for Formal Submission)**

Note 1

It's hard to say which is more miserable—the pea soup in the air or the pea pudding on my table.

I didn't finish today's report concerning the Fogwalker. He wasn't in his office, and I only happened upon him while wandering the streets at dinnertime. Trying to find someone in the fog is the worst game of hide-and-seek imaginable. The Fogwalker is piecing together a device he claims to be some sort of smog filtration machine ... I hope he doesn't truly believe he can handle all this fog on his own.

Note 2

Almost had my leg smashed in by a car. An Uluru leaflet dive-bombed my face, and with the Foundation uniform on, I couldn't get cross about it and tear the thing up.

HQ sent new tasks. Annoying. Frankly, under these circumstances, it feels inevitable that the London qualifiers will be canceled.

Note 3

Never thought I'd be drafting up paperwork to get the qualifiers canceled so soon. I'm oddly excited about the whole thing.

Note 4

Yes, it's all so strange. Everything here contradicts what we've managed to preserve in our records. But so what, why couldn't it have just been recording errors, bad recording practices, or outright falsified data that were passed on to us?

Meanwhile, I'm choking on coal dust that is falling as heavy as snow and putting up with a colleague who keeps getting lost in the fog. A stellar job experience.

Note 5

It's done, though there's a heap of follow-up work ahead. Honestly, I'm hoping that magnificent contraption will actually succeed. That way all these decorations won't go to waste, the tourists and athletes won't have wasted their time, and I won't have wasted all my pre-drafted paperwork.

Waste, waste, it was all a waste ... but I suppose things could have been so much worse.

### 2310109 · The Aftermath

**To: The Foundation's London Branch**

Subject: The "London Air Pollution Auto-detect Cleaner" Project: Progress and Future Collaboration

To the Honorable Head of the Foundation's London Division,

Allow me to extend our greetings on behalf of the London Atmospheric Clean-up Committee Office and present a thorough progress update on the "London Air Pollution Auto-detect Cleaner" initiative.

...

Though the research has faced setbacks and failed to resolve the pressing issues of the Uluru qualifiers, we greatly appreciate the open-minded approach and collaborative spirit exhibited by your department. Without your material enchantment technologies and the protocols for incantation inscription, progress would have been far more limited.

As indicated in earlier communications, we are committed to sharing the outcomes of this joint research endeavor in both mechanical engineering and arcanum studies. Although the device has not yet achieved its intended results, we will honor this commitment. As per standard collaboration practices with the Foundation, since your team provided only technical support while all material sourcing and manufacturing were conducted by us independently, the physical device will not be included in the shared materials. However, we will share its design concepts, manufacturing data, and complete test records.

...

Please accept our heartfelt gratitude for your continued cooperation. Should you require clarification or wish to discuss this matter further, please do not hesitate to reach out.

Yours sincerely,

Arthur Fog

London Atmospheric Clean-up Committee Office

### 2310111 · Daily Newspaper Excerpts

**Post-Qualifiers Prospects: Setting Out Beneath Clear Skies**

Following the Uluru London Qualifiers, 32 athletes from Britain, competing in 18 different events, departed for Australia aboard an Imperial Airways flight this morning. The weather was exceptionally clear with fresh air and no visible smog. Imperial Airways personnel commented that it was "an exceedingly rare occurrence, an ideal omen for their momentous journey."

Reports suggest that the rare clear skies may be related to the "Fog Monster Incident" during the qualifiers. Many claim that Willow's floor ritual routine banished both the fog and the phantom beast, while skeptics argue that the Witch staged the entire event, using a sinister spectacle to manipulate public sentiment. Others have even claimed to hold insider knowledge indicating this to be part of a century-long conspiracy orchestrated by the British government ...

Whatever the truth of the matter, the Uluru qualifiers have brought tangible benefits to London. Hospital admissions for respiratory conditions have dropped by 60%, with many residents reporting easier breathing and air quality "reminiscent of centuries past."

## 章节 24101

### 2410101 · Unofficial Map

**Excerpt from "A History of Arcane Ethnicity"**

...

Due to the reclusive nature of the Nukutais, our knowledge of this enigmatic people comes only from the sparse accounts of a few trading ships that had contact with them and the legends from neighboring societies.

...

In the folklore of coastal civilizations in the South Pacific, there are often tales of Shell People or Hermit Crab People. These beings, dressed in shells and living in the water, are depicted as otherworldly ocean emissaries or guardians. They offer advice and guidance to the protagonists in these stories or help those lost at sea.

...

With the Age of Exploration, the veil over these mysterious figures was lifted, and a small island named Nukutaeao, known for its production of mystical and esoteric crafts, became a topic of intrigue among adventurers in taverns. Consequently, the intrusion of explorers and trading ships seemed to weary the Nukutais, and records from that period show they gradually vanished from the pages of history. They employed some form of arcane skill to conceal the island's exact location, ensuring their homeland never appeared on any official map.

...

Today, with advancements in maritime technology and increasing global communication, it is only a matter of time before the island of the Nukutais is rediscovered. The author can only hope that this inevitable encounter will not bring undue harm to the Nukutais, wishing that they can integrate into the global community through peaceful and friendly exchanges. May the final chapter of this Age of Exploration conclude with harmony.
