### 2410103 · Convergence of Ideas

**Fish Tank Brainstorm**

"The topic of this brainstorming session is: How did this 'Pioneer' manage to cross the 'Storm' and continuously send information to the Earth's surface?" The ferrofluid flows freely in his glass tank.

"Is it possible that this is just a prank? Like a cipher game created by one of us, accompanied by fake photos and data ..." An indifferent researcher shrugs.

"Then go and write a detailed report on who you think is pulling the prank, one every week, until someone confesses to being behind it."

"That's an abuse of power!" The researcher, who intended to remain silent, slams the table. "Alright ... I have a thought. The 'Storm' rewinds people into disappearance, but some things that inherently exist longer might not be completely reversed. This 'Pioneer' could be some kind of man-made satellite that regularly sends data, and the 'Storm' rewinds it to an older version, but doesn't affect its operation."

"We've already experienced time periods as far back as the beginning of World War I; no man-made satellite should even exist at the time."

"I've got it! It's aliens! Aliens are crossing the 'Storm' and sending us some kind of signal! It's not surprising at all that they have technology immune to the 'Storm' among their advanced space tech, right?" A researcher with a fanciful imagination raises his hand.

"Damn it, we don't have time to verify baseless hypotheses such as these!"

"The man-made satellite theory might just be a reasonable hypothesis. Maybe the internal structure of the satellite just happened to form a knot-like structure? Many of the satellites launched by Laplace have adopted arcane techniques, theoretically capable of activating the immunity spell." The researcher, feigning seriousness, rests his chin on his hands.

"That's a good direction, but your reasoning is too idealistic. Let's proceed on the premise that there exists a satellite capable of gaining immunity to the 'Storm' and investigate further, regardless of the principle behind all this. Now, everyone, spread out and check every satellite in Laplace's history. If this hypothesis is confirmed, it could be another significant breakthrough in our 'Storm' research."

### 2410104 · Budget Crisis

**Procurement List - Project Regulus**

...

Dragon Tree Resin Compasses—Compasses carved from the resin of dragon blood trees, serving as the activation stimulus of the Eastern astrology arcane skill on telescopes, used for navigation in extreme environments.

Note: Procure dragon blood (the sap of the dragon tree) for re-manufacturing. To ensure foolproof production, an external contract has been issued for collecting genuine dragon blood.

Obsidian Crystals—Utilizing obsidian crystals to produce solar panels was a proposal by some arcanists of that time. The ingenious application of Aztec arcane skills significantly enhanced the efficiency of solar energy absorption.

Note: On-site collection at Popocatepetl Volcano, along with lab-produced control samples.

Wide-field Camera—The wide-field planetary camera (WFPC) with the highest resolution of that time provided by Caltech, responsible for capturing image data.

Note: Send a specialist to negotiate, requesting a camera and a design blueprint if possible. The price is of no issue.

Astronomical Spectrometer—The telescope is equipped with various sized spectrometers, integrating both spectral and photographic functions, used for gathering spectral data.

Note: A labor-intensive task; retrieve all existing spectrometer designs from Laplace, and commission expedited production from major corporations.

High-speed Photometer—An instrument used to measure the brightness and polarization variations of celestial bodies. It was decommissioned before the first "Storm" due to malfunctions.

Note: Even if decommissioned, it can't be overlooked. The museum might have replacement parts.

Space Seeds—Seeds prepared for the activation environment of certain arcane skills at that time. Each seed was meticulously selected and symbolically significant.

Note: The original conditions cannot be fully recreated, but similar substitutes can be found. Request as many as possible from Plesetsk.

"Regulus" Record—The power source of the telescope, shrouded in complete mystery as the most enigmatic component of the apparatus.

Note: The owner has been identified. The entanglement of identical names brings inextricable fate into play ... though unscientific, this must also be considered.

### 2410105 · Closing Ceremony

???：Hey, you! Yes, you, my hurried child, you rush through the street like a cyclone ... Where are you going?

???：A show, a show! So you came to Vienna for the love of stories. That's for sure.

???：Then this is just the right place for you. A place where the floors are decorated with gold, where music sings along with the stories ... Even a newcomer could be entranced here.

???：Oh, listen to me! You must not be a newcomer, but a true connoisseur! Well, before you leave, may I ask a simple question?

???：Which act in a show do you prefer?

???：Oh! Well begun is half done! You're always right to the point. We should know how a story starts and when we have stepped onto the path of fate.

???：Haha! The rising action! The most thrilling part, like biting into the filling of an apple strudel—you're about to have the sweetest part!

???：Yes, of course! The resolution, coming to its fated ending. We stand up and cheer, or stay in our seats and weep ... Our reward for not falling asleep during the intermission, worth every krone you've paid.

???：Haha, so greedy. It's a tough choice, isn't it? But you can't escape from making decisions.

???：Do you know which act I love the most? Not Act One, nor Three or Five ...

???：But Six. Oh! Act Six, my dear, that's the best part.

???：The part in which the king picks up his fallen head; the knight pulls out the sword in his chest; the bodies stand up, dust their clothes and come to the front of the stage, bow to the audience and receive their applause ...

???：Yes, it is! My child, that is precisely when the real show begins.

???：I have a good script here. Let me put on my glasses ... Hmm ... Let's start from ... from Act Six.

???：The princess, a nightingale decorated with diamonds, an opera singer taking center stage, steps into a building of gray-and-white diamond patterns.

???："And the curtain falls forever. The smile on her face seems too serene. She is still dreaming."

???："Her madness keeps them all alert. But in the court, she seems so meek and mentally steady, like a docked boat on calm waters."

???："Nodding elegantly to every gentleman she meets, she makes the shackle on her neck look like a piece of dazzling jewelry."

???：Next page ... oh.

???："In a transparent room, under an unyielding limelight, her dignity is stomped on, like a silk handkerchief thrown on the ground. They smash her crown, forcing her to pay for her madness."

???：No ... not anymore.

???："'Sleep, she said to me, sleep. If that's what she said, then I don't need to wake up.'"

???："That's when the star falls into an ever-lasting dream. The blinding light becoming a spotlight on the stage; the empty room carrying the best reverberations. She has never been so calm. Everything beautiful in her is there forever, frozen in time."

???：Or found eternal peace.

???："Her world has become the performance. A show that never ends."

???：I'll say she's a great actress, isn't she?

???：Hahaha, my dear. You are indeed a great connoisseur.

???：Only one act of a play can't tell a good story by itself.

???：Oh, forgive my ramblings. The golden curtain has risen.

???：Fix your collar, darling, and keep moving forward.

### 2410106 · 身份危机

> Identity Crisis

星锑在模仿摇滚海盗的比赛中荣获第一名。

???：Now, we've learned some manners in Apeiron, haven't we? Namely, to abstain from beans, never parcel off a loaf, and stay away from the white roosters on the road.

???：It's been so long since we last talked about these things. How time does fly! This rusty brain of mine is struggling to keep up ...

???：We should have waited until dawn to light the candles. The sun would have mistaken the day for night and delayed its appearance in the morning. That way, we would have had more time to talk.

???：Indeed. You possess a far greater understanding of the laws of nature than your ancestors. It's only natural that you wouldn't relate candlelight to sunrise. But, for the people of earlier times, there was a commonly understood connection between the two.

???：Did the trees not bear fruit after the Horn of Plenty was filled at the harvest festival? Did the field not wave with grain?

???：Does the lighting of a match not illuminate the moon?

???：It's human nature to try to understand the world. Of course, at that time mistakes were made, and some phenomena were attributed to the wrong causes; some theories were developed from false facts.

???：It was a time of symbols, you see.

???：The flower represented blessings and harvest, the ouroboros the eternal cycle, the hawk wit and astuteness, and the soil safety and protection.

???：Aside from nature, the circle symbolized protection, the triangle stability, and the triple helix ascendance, change, and the unity of mind, body, and soul.

???：As time went on, things became associated with one another, and more and more symbols were created.

???：You can see the cause and effect with your own eyes, dear. It's clear when something is related and when it isn't. If the outcome isn't what we expect, these symbols must be incorrect.

???：But it's not their physical form that's wrong. Physical objects never lie. It's the meaning and concept given to them that are wrong, you see.

???：Objects are just objects. A match is just a match, not the illuminator of the moon. An animal is just an animal; the circle and triangle are only shapes ...

???：... and "you" are just "you".

### 2410108 · Last-Second Scramble

**"Encyclopedia of Nautical Lore—Sea Monster Edition"**

Serres

Common Names: Pig-toothed Ray, Anchor Fish, Deep Bass Disc, Hammerhead Ray.

Back home, we call this thing a Sea Bat.

That's not right! Sea Bats are a different species and they definitely don't smash your portholes at night!

Classification: Subclass: Aquatic Primal Critter, Order: Oceanic, Family: Ray-form, Genus: Flying Ray.

In my opinion, it should be classified as a bird.

Distinctive Features: Broad wings, long tail, a pair of clawed fins, and curved ivory-white fangs.

Hey, I just thought of a business idea! Could we pass these fangs off as ivory?

Serres typically live in groups and feed on large fish, seabirds, and drifting debris. While they may look similar to Olitiau, their behavior couldn't be more different. While Olitiaus are generally non-aggressive, Serres are known for their fondness for challenging creatures larger than themselves—including sperm whales, baleen whales, megalodon sharks, and passing ships. Sometimes, they even mistake protruding rocky outcrops and coral reefs for foes. When a Serres spots a ship, it leaps from the water, attacking the vessel with its sharp wings and calling upon its companions. They seem to have a competitive nature; each Serres strives to soar higher and leave deeper marks on its target.

Stay away from the windows. You never know how deep their wings can slice.

Despite their large numbers, Serres communicate through a single method: sound. They can produce a wide range of calls within the 10-180Hz frequency range, ideal for underwater communication, and they can sense each other's vibrations through their wing membranes. The best way to deal with Serres is to disrupt their hearing. Shouts, bangs, and music can confuse them, while enough volume can physically irritate their auditory systems, causing them to flee in discomfort.

Now that's more like it! My former captain tried using anti-Olitiau measures against them, which is why he's my "former captain."

### 2410109 · 学术交流

> Scholarly Exchange

交流归交流，竞争归竞争，不然还怎么进步？

???：Be ever so careful, dear. The fiery thirst for knowledge is blazing bright within you; be careful not to put it out.

???：Once, many fire thieves sat here, sipping coffee, as they tried to decipher the best angle to hold up their reeds to jam the burning wheel of Helios and steal his flame.

???：The gods are selfish. They're stingy with their might.

???：That's easy to say when the power's not yours to give. Knowing how to share is a rare virtue. Knowing how to share knowledge is a rarer virtue still.

???："How can we transfer our wisdom into mortal minds?" The gods pondered.

???：Can inspiration be duplicated from one brain to another? It comes and goes like a gust of wind, so what can be done?

???：True. I'm sure the early customers of the bakery share the same philosophy.

???：The line must be respected, however hungry the latecomers may be.

???：There are many fire thieves in the field of science—many more than the bread buyers in the bakery. Take a look at these geeks in uniforms made of aerospace materials.

???：Among them are arcanists in love with science who spend most of their time studying diodes and machines, and humans who are passionate about the future, always finding new inspirations ...

???：Elements, lightning, atoms ... Perhaps you've forgotten their counterparts in arcanum, but you must be familiar with them as scientific discoveries.

???：These discoveries are the milestones of progress, sweetheart. The fire thieves firmly believed that they would be able to bring about many more technical revolutions: the fourth, the fifth, the 65535th ...

???：Not yet, darling. We've only finished the prologue. However, there is a "but" waiting down the road.

???：A sudden rain dampened people's enthusiasm for the future. The floppy disks and many clever minds perished in it.

???：Some of the fire thieves grieved the loss. They screamed and swore to retrieve what was lost.

???：What about the rest of them?

???：They returned to primitive chaos, like coelacanths crawling back into the ocean after thousands of years of evolution. Once again, they became the proteges of arcanum.

???：Perhaps they made the right choice by leaving that useless science behind. Who knows?

???：The story of the fire thieves has only gone this far, darling. At this point, no one knows what will happen to Prometheus or what's inside Pandora's box.

### 2410112 · Medical Records

**Shipboard Medical Log**

X/X, Wednesday

Charlie, 43, seasickness. Administered dimenhydrinate.

Janice, 29, severe seasickness. Administered dimenhydrinate.

Kalaea, 17, seasickness. Patient refused medication, advised to open a window and get some fresh air.

It's really trying how afraid some 17-year-olds can be of taking medication.

X/X, Thursday

Alessio, 33, diarrhea from consuming excessive seafood. Administered diphenoxylate, advised bed rest and adequate hydration.

Kalaea, 17, scalded by a water heater. Accompanying personnel were agitated and cursing; calmed them down, cleaned the burn, and applied ointment.

These two never cease to amaze me. Last time I spent ages explaining seasickness meds, and now they talk about 'water-fire boxes' and 'heat-pots.' Where on earth are they from?

X/X, Saturday

Received an emergency call from the crew informing me of an altercation involving five passengers in the dining area. Treated four with minor injuries and one with a light injury on the spot.

The captain tells me that it wasn't caused by those shell-wearing lads this time. Still, with their bizarre and smug demeanor towards others, it wouldn't surprise me if they got into trouble one of these days.

X/X, Tuesday

Eira, 22, bloating, diarrhea, vomiting, high fever. Administered IV fluids, kept under observation.

During observation, the patient showed signs of delirium, claiming the cruise had reached the 'New Era' and demanded to disembark and return to Nukutaeao. The shell on the patient's chest turned from white to red and developed numerous cracks. When questioned about this, the patient hastily hid the shell, and the accompanying person sternly rebuked us and changed the subject.

### 2410115 · Evaluation Meeting

**Accountability Measures**

"We can't allow failure and betrayal to run rampant indefinitely, don't you agree, Ms. Grace?" The shadow of the serpent twists under the light, appearing several times larger than its true form.

"Yes," replies another shadow, trying to lower itself as much as possible.

"You quickly identified that the toy was a contract item used to control the succubus, which is impressive. It also shows you're different from Matheus and possess wisdom befitting an Apostle." The stirring rod clinked against the teacup, producing a melodic sound.

"I am sorry that others fell to Kimberly's betrayal; I did overestimate the potency of the contract item."

"If you'd shown less mercy to that succubus and given her stricter orders, this tragedy might have been averted. Likewise, if you had been more ruthless in Texas, you wouldn't have wasted so much time there and would not have lost the contract item in the chaos. Although, in the end, losing it was Igor and his subordinates' fault, not yours."

"Thank you for your guidance and understanding."

"Steel yourself, merciful little Grace. Showing compassion to a viper only gets you bitten. Remember this in your next mission."

"The Nukutais ... they will present us with their 'Spirit Shells.'"

"Your capabilities are undeniable. I still hold some faith in you." At last, a hot cup of tea is stirred into a cold one, and the serpent's shadow vanishes out the door without even flicking its tongue. "Oh, and I shouldn't need to remind you—any unforeseen events during the mission must be reported, especially anything related to Vertin. This is also according to Their guidance."

"...Yes, it's all for Their return."

### 2410122 · Thoughts on the Solar Eclipse

**Report on the "Free Breeze" Incident**

According to the Timekeeper's statement provided to our personnel, the recent hijacking of the "Free Breeze" was orchestrated by Ms. Grace, a member of Manus Vindictae. Her involvement included the abduction of the Nukutais and persuading passengers to wear Manus Vindictae masks. Considering this, an upgrade in our risk assessment for Ms. Grace may be necessary, along with issuing a formal warrant for her arrest.

Additionally, with assistance from Regulus, a member of the Timekeeper's team, Researcher Ulrich of Laplace was able to complete his 'Artificial Storm' experiment on the "Free Breeze". Regulus's record was identified as an immune material, however its overall efficacy is weak, providing only a minor delay in the onset of Era Sickness, and critically lacks reproducibility; none of the copies of the records show the same level of efficacy. Regulus attributes this to her record's 'unique rock spirit,' claiming it possesses a soul that its imitations lack, this claim has not yet been rigorously assessed.

**Your Close Companion is Here**

"Close Companions," everyone's best friends. The earliest of these companions can be traced back to the East, where the "Zhenzi" befriended the powerful "Xiandao Beast." Together, they battled against evil and maintained peace and harmony. To this day, the "Liang" family still takes pride in the bond their ancestors shared with "Qiangliang the Xiandao Beast." The Xiandao Beasts protect the world, a story familiar to every child, often whispered by their mothers to soothe them to sleep ...

But it's not just a legend; close companions also exist around us. Your grey-headed chickadee poet, your imaginary wolf friend, your changeling childhood playmate—they've long been a part of our lives, and have become our most loyal friends ...

Now, it's your turn to find your own close companion—or perhaps get one for the kids! Thanks to the NewBabel Critter Rehab Center, critter companions are coming to every home!

Special thanks to the St. Pavlov Foundation for providing the companion footage featured in this program.

### 2510103 · Spot Check

???：Here is a well known question you must have heard: "What goes on four legs in the morning, two in the afternoon, and three in the evening?" it's so well "known" that no one finds it novel anymore.

???：But please do me a favor and answer me yourself.

???：Correct! This is the same answer Oedipus gave to defeat Sphinx. Thus becoming a hero, but also starting him on his ill-fated destiny.

???：Raise a question, give an answer. This is the most fundamental ritual. Through this, we get nearer to God.

???：The Sphinx, an embodiment of mercy and nobility in Egypt, was seen as a mystery to the Greeks: a dangerous being that kept secrets, and delivered the punishments of the gods.

???：When the question is answered, the ritual is finished, and the answerer is granted the right to reach the treasure it guards.

???：Yes, a ritual. Whether it is by pouring out wine; slaughtering a calf; or circling round a fire with a baby in your arms... It is an agreement; a question that demands an answer.

???：Every desire has a price. Whether it is paid in wine, blood, or fire, but sometimes that sacrifice is taken from you without it ever being noticed.

???：And the treasure you seek might be the undoing of your own desires.

???：Let us take a look at another question: "Stranger, what's your name?" Cried Polyphemus.

???："Nobody!" Odysseus answered, and it allowed him to flee the cyclops' cave, safe and sound.

???：A human being and nobody, two answers that could not be more alike, yet both were bound to fate by their answer.

???：Alright, dear, time to call it a day. As for you? You'll get the answers you seek, you've already paid the price.

### 2510108 · Pieced Together

**Reconstructing Idealized Memories**

Although I don't usually keep a journal, I had to record today's encounter—I met the great Miss Matilda Bouanich!

I still remember that summer. My mother lectured me for an hour before we left and then another hour during our journey. I thought I was following her instructions perfectly: I was dressed perfectly neat; my greetings were memorized word-for-word. But the moment I met the Bouanichs, I froze solid, too nervous to say anything. Even adults can get stage fright, so how was it fair to give her own daughter the cold shoulder for it? A smile or a pat on the back from mother was all I needed to finish my greeting without fumbling.

Thank goodness for Lady Beryl. She saw what happened, and her gracious words salvaged the situation. I didn't disappoint my mother after that. I'm sure of it because the rest of my memories of that visit were pleasant—Lady Beryl's warm smile and Matilda's energetic chatter. Matilda could be as noisy as she liked and still win her mother's approval, how I envied that so much.

But Lady Beryl … for some reason, I can't remember what she looked like. Now I understand Latham's frustration. Being Matilda's mother, I could imagine a matured, refined version of Matilda—minus the childish energy and, oh right, without the hooked nose, that's my mother's trait.

### 2510111 · Calculated Maneuver

**A Vigile's Correspondence**

This letter was tucked inside a folder of interrogation records, it appears to have been exchanged silently between two Vigiles agents during an interrogation. The two distinct handwritings reflect their unspoken dialogue:

"She's tight-lipped about it."

"But she seems unaware that we've already figured out everything."

"Fascinating, like a mouse in a lab experiment—arrogant yet clueless."

"There's still one thing left to figure out: how exactly did she learn to crack the anti-theft arcane seal?"

"All her lies and disguises fall apart under scrutiny, but she hasn't given up a single clue about this. Even our artificial somnambulism couldn't uncover anything …"

"Could it just be sheer dumb luck?"

"Disappointing. I've taught you better—never rely on million-to-one coincidences. Always consider the more plausible scenario."

"You mean … she's being controlled by someone else? And they've erased her memory, so she can't betray them?"

"Exactly. That slippery merchant gave up Scott, but there's no way a minor employee could pull off something like this. Rejoice, we've hooked a big fish."

"So … we're letting her go?"

"Yes, but not without loosening her strings first."

"Understood. I'll notify Mr. Pedra. I trust he'll ensure Miss Scott feels completely at ease after some … subtle adjustments."

"Good. You're a reliable assistant, Billy."

The second writer seemed about to reply but left only a hesitant pen stroke.

### 2510112 · Syncing Up

???：My dear child, look at you, you're exhausted. I couldn't be of help then, but it's ok, the "party" is over now. At least let me brush your hair, darling.

???：Or your hair will get all knotted, and it will hurt you when you try to untangle them.

???：I'm not surprised. An old friend of ours, "knot." Often we learn our knots before we even know how to read and write.

???：A knot could be an obstacle that breaks the consistency of a flow, and also a reminder of forgotten things.

???：You can use just about anything on hand to make a knot: leaves of grass, the fur of an animal, even fallen hair ... But the sturdiest knots are tied with rope.

???：Knots are to be found in many stories. Signify promises, counting and measuring, an instrument of salvation and one of execution.

???：Oh, I could go on all day! The influence and power of knots can't be overstated...

???：It is said that it was in the cutting of the Gordian Knot that Alexander became "the Great"; just touching the meters-long rope provides blessings to the faithful in Belem; and tiny knots on strings can carry the history of an entire people.

???：The ancient Inca called it "Quipu." The intricate variations of hundreds of fabrics, colors, and lengths of strings were used to record their stories through the ages. And to think, Shakespeare only had 26 letters to create his masterpieces.

???：Just look around you: don't sailors use knots to mark depths and mend sails; or coachmen tying knots to rein their horses; and any respectable lady or gentleman knows the knots they must tie about their necks to stay in fashion.

???：Or consider the knot-like helix structure of DNA, and the changes in states of matter ... Invisible to the naked eye, these knots carry some hint at a universal law.

???：That there might be a string, like the Quipu, an endless rope that records in its knots everything in the world. One that can't be seen, yet there it is, everywhere, all at once.

???：Maybe you can tie this knot yourself. Use your hair, then form a loop, pass the end through it, and tighten it up. There you go.

???：I hope you keep this knot as a record of the words we shared here.

Our reporter brings you an exclusive frontline update:

"As the Dushuo Festival begins, Los Angeles's Chinatown is once again alive with the excitement of the Shehuo Parade. Humans and arcanists will soon come together to pray for a year of peace and prosperity."

"We are currently at the Chinatown Community Center, which houses 12 sacred arcane idols, marking the starting point of the parade."

"Ah, the summoning official is responsible for reciting the summoning rites and inviting the idols. After lunch, the rotating God of Shèti, 'Xieqia,' will be placed on the palanquin. Some of the parade members have already gathered outside."

"That's right, we can see people carrying lanterns and flags preparing for the parade. Principal Huang, what's that large animal lantern over there?"

"That's a Lùshǔ, a Xiángruì. Later, it will join the Beast Lantern Team. The parade will be led by the Five-Colored Banners and the Yingge Troupe, followed by the Xiandao Beasts and Xiángruì creatures—quite the spectacle!"

"The Xiandao Beasts, are those the legendary creatures said to ward off evil plagues?"

"Exactly. During the 'Plague-Banishing' segment, General Qianmo will shout the command, and three young men wearing Xiandao Beast masks will sing the Plague-Banishing Song, imitating the beasts to drive out evil."

"The musicians will accompany them with gongs and drums. The parade will go on until late at night—quite the festivity!"

"Thank you, Principal Huang. Though the parade has yet to start, we can see crowds waiting already. Stay tuned as we bring you live coverage of the Shehuo Parade."

???：No need to start panicking just yet. Things are still under control. Your lady friend has stepped up to solve this mess, and the veteran knows what he's doing.

???：This city is unlike any you've seen before. You must learn its secrets to survive—remember which street kids are from which alley and what intel they can be relied on for, learn the dates when the dealers bring their "medicine" into port, and figure out which water tanks hide the key to the local gang's safehouse.

???：Don't look at me for answers. It's none of my business.

### 271010501 · An Unexpected Encounter

**An Unexpected Encounter**

Surrounded by light but feeling no pain. The screams and footsteps around her are crystal clear.

The first footsteps she hears are Kozlov and Natalya's. Their voices are more familiar than her own heartbeat.

The clatter of goat hooves belongs to Elijah, still midway through his riding performance.

The particularly light, quick footsteps are Fracios's, a habit left from his juggling practice.

"Everyone stay calm. Her Arcanum has protected her very well." A strange voice. Must be an audience member stepping onto the stage. "Relax, girl. Try not to think too much. Just tell me, what you see right now?"

"This is unbelievable. I see a star, a shooting star is flying right toward me."

The stranger crouches beside her. The light dims just a little. "I'm going to use Ley Lines to dissolve your Arcanum. Stay relaxed. Talk to me about anything. What hobbies do you have?"

"Maybe they're not really hobbies, but ... I like to listen to the radio and weather reports. I also like exploring strange places. To keep from getting lost, I memorize all the unusual-looking trees and rocks along the way ... That star is still twinkling. That's not normal, right?"

"Don't worry. Maybe everything today happened just so you could meet that star. Let's keep talking. Would you like to study Ley Lines with me? You have a real talent, I'm sure of it ..."

### 271010502 · The Great Utrennyaya Circus

**The Great Utrennyaya Circus**

We have the best stage on Earth, each show as unique as a flake of snow.

We bring the most spirited performances in the world.

Elijah can turn any animal into the finest steed alive.

Fracios's performances will make you laugh until you cry.

And Kiperina, our tiny and adorable aerialist, walks on a wire so thin no one can even see it.

Ringmaster Kozlov has folded his entire life's reputation into this little tent.

From now until next month, this new world welcomes you!

### 271010601 · Have You Heard?

**Have You Heard?**

"That kid seems to have disappeared. You know where he went?"

"Who?"

"The talkative one. Obsessed with Roman myths."

"Are you describing yourself?"

"Name Day. I'm talking about Name Day."

"You were closer to him than I was. Why ask me?"

"The only thing I ever talked with him about was those myths."

"Huh, our conversations were usually limited to German folklore. He seems to always keep people at a distance."

"Nonsense. He hated German mythology."

"Stop projecting. As far as we talked it seemed like he loved German culture. We talked about it all the time."

Two Foundation staff glare at each other in the cafeteria, on the verge of fighting over a man they thought they knew, until a third person sits down between them.

"You're talking about Name Day? He took two months off. I think he's on vacation."

"That workaholic actually went on vacation? I thought his life was nothing but work and collecting trinkets."

"Where'd he go?"

"No idea. We only worked together on one mission, but he was a decent guy."

"Did he ever chat with you about anything else?"

"We talked about folk legends."

"Roman?"

"German?"

"Gothic."

### 271011101 · To Camille

**To Camille**

Dear Camille,

How long has it been since we last spoke? The long tug-of-war with the cold drained all my energy. Only today did I manage to warm up enough to dip my tail in ink and write to you.

Sylvie is doing well. Maxime and Alexandre always help her think in parallel, and Monique, as usual, keeps things under control at critical moments. Serpentes has been showing more and more signs of thought lately. I think it's only a matter of time before he speaks. Hélène is sleepier than ever, staying in heated rooms around the clock.

As for Chantal and Pierre, they're the same as always.

And me? I didn't just hibernate. The local literature has filled the gaps in my collection that Sylvie once ruthlessly discarded.

The Melusines were deeply moved by these authors' vivid portrayals of suffering and death, yet even within the darkest themes, the authors' exploration of new paths brimmed with vitality, dreams, and a defiant sense of romanticism.

Whenever I read the literature to her, her memories become unusually vivid, and she recalls one line in particular with striking clarity from Pasternak's February: "The truest verses arise by chance, tempered in the fire of pain."

Of course, there's no way to verify this and Sylvie would never admit it, but I believe she's the most romantic in our family. Or maybe the second-most. After me. I wish you were part of the Dubois family too. That way, you'll truly understand what Sylvie's memories feel like when she sees a shooting star.

I truly hope we can talk about all of this someday. Maybe after Sylvie's little challenge is complete, you could visit Plesetsk. I promise Sylvie and I will both be warmly waiting to welcome you.

Your dear friend,

Estelle

### 271011401 · Cabinet of Wishes

**Cabinet of Wishes**

Become Director. Drink endless wine. Succeed in projects.

World peace. Conquer the galaxy. Go home for a visit.

Live to see next year. Touch Mars with your own hands. Establish relations with alien life.

Grow flowers in Siberia. Free yourself from material needs. Become a true engineer.

Defeat death. Marry Anastasia. Witness history.

### 271011701 · The Story of Cepheus

**The Story of Cepheus**

Cepheus learned a wonderful way to live.

Always keep your eyes on the future. Keep walking forward. It believed that was the right thing to do.

Cepheus moved quickly. And so, it fell hard. After that, it donned a metal shell.

Inside that metal shell, the ghost of Cepheus witnessed countless beautiful moments—

A near-eternal hearth being lit. Man-made stars launched into the sky. Earth's songs reaching beyond the Solar System.

But it all vanished in the rain. Its dreams ended.

And Cepheus felt regret for the first time. It realized, perhaps, that it didn't really know how to live after all.

Cepheus believed itself to be a guiding pointer for millennia to come, leading the way for ten thousand years. But in truth, it realized it didn't even understand the road to the next hundred years. It couldn't even light the paths of the Southern Hemisphere.

What happened to Cepheus afterward? It returned to the space base and continued its research for the sake of those still living.

Yes. I am Cepheus.

### 271012001 · Party Highlights

**Party Highlights**

Excuse me, ladies and gentlemen. May I ask which one of you is Miss Hissabeth? I bring greetings from Constantine. I heard you speaking just now—please don't all play dumb and pretend to be snakes.

...

Greetings, Miss Windsong. The Foundation has heard about the outstanding role that Ley Line Theory played in this aerospace experiment. Am I here to grant you Funding? No, I'm here to deliver Constantine's formal commendations, which surpass any material support. Is this your apprentice? The greatest contributor to the success of this space experiment! Constantine specially instructed me to offer Kiperina the highest congratulations, and to wish her a brilliant future at the LSCC.

...

Hello, Ms. Pointer. This teleport disk really is a miraculous once-in-a-century invention. Constantine sees it as having immense strategic value.

Your colleagues are already grumbling that I've ruined the party atmosphere—I hope you won't mind my delivering her greetings. Are you offering me the stage to speak? Then I'll take just five minutes.

...

Help! Anyone! Save me from Medicine Pocket!

I didn't know this table was their turf! They're still biting me! Hey, who's playing music at a time like this?! Somebody, help!

### 271012002 · Teleportation Ritual Seminar

**Teleportation Ritual Seminar**

...

As many of you have read in academic journals, the theoretical feasibility of teleportation incantations is not determined by distance.

However, the farther the distance, the more interference factors there are, and to this day, we still don't know exactly why. Thus, long-distance teleportation has always been considered the realm of pure fantasy.

However, during the extreme conditions just before the "Storm," we gathered a vast amount of arcane energy fluctuation data from outer space to the Earth's surface.

After repeated cross-analysis, we finally discovered a stable arcanum waveform capable of anchoring teleportation incantations. My teammate, she gave it a misleading name—"Pulsar Waveform." I suppose that was her way of being poetic or something.

...

Yes, I hear some of you murmuring that this is purely experimental, lacking theoretical proof and impossible to replicate. That's true. But the fact is, over the past few weeks, we've tested it repeatedly. As long as you simulate this waveform near the destination and there's enough arcane energy, you can achieve teleportation with up to 95% accuracy.

...

That's all I wanted to share today. I believe that one day in the distant future, with a complete theoretical framework, teleportation could become a field that outpaces aerospace itself. But even now, what we've uncovered is a stable, practical technology—perfect for food delivery.

## 章节 31101

### 3110101 · Top-Secret Mission

**St. Pavlov Foundation External Field Report: The "Danube Dawn"**

Mission Number: XXX-1912-XX-13  
Location: The "Danube Dawn"  
Security Level: Top Secret

Intelligence Summary:

Key Report 1: Multiple investigation records indicate that during the past three operation cycles (exact dates in encrypted attachment), abnormal arcanum energy traces were consistently detected inside the "Danube Dawn," the international train running between Istanbul and Vienna. These energy patterns closely match the activity signature of a known Beyond entity—the vampire (see encrypted attachment for detailed characteristics).  
Key Report 2: Meanwhile, a series of unexplained deaths and disappearances have occurred along the train's route.  
Preliminary examinations show that the victims suffered blunt-force trauma and displayed tissue mutations of unidentified form, differing from the pattern of known vampire "Infected." The upper division suspects direct contact with a new strain of vampire. Threat level has been classified as severe.

All evidence suggests the presence of an anomalous vampire aboard the "Danube Dawn"—either active on a periodic cycle or as a vampire "Infected" with atypical traits.  
Squad members are to prioritize personal safety during the operation.

Mission Objectives:

Based on prior performance, Level Four Investigator Semmelweis is hereby assigned to the "Danube Dawn" investigation team. Objectives include:  
1. Board the "Danube Dawn," confirm the authenticity and number of vampire entities, observe their activity pattern, and assess passenger threat level.  
2. Investigate whether vampire activity correlates with newly observed vampire "Infected" incidents along the train's route. Record any newly discovered strains, their traits, and behavioral patterns.  
3. Collect direct evidence of collaboration between "Manus Vindictae" and the vampire.

Investigator Semmelweis is to rendezvous with four members of the Istros Operation Squad 77 after the "Danube Dawn" docks at Platform 3, Station G, 6:20 PM local time, November 30, 1912. They will supply all mission-critical resources and equipment.

St. Pavlov Foundation

November 2, 1912

### 3110102 · Legend's Tale

**The "Danube Dawn" — Rumor 1**

The "Danube Dawn" was once the Versailles Palace on rails, gliding along the banks of the Danube River. From Istanbul to Vienna, silverware clinked a harmonious tune, vase violets glistened with dew, and even the stokers carried themselves with the poise of Habsburg courtiers—at least, that is what they believed.

But the glory days didn't last. Once the roar of cannons had grown louder than the whistle, it was abandoned—forgotten in a warehouse.

Later, Vienna-Pannonian Railways pulled the train from the warehouse and refurbished it, stripping away the last threads of velvet and cramming in seats hard enough to turn your back straight. The silver tableware disappeared, French cuisine vanished from the menu, and flavorless beef stew took its place. The train now resembles an aging gentleman in an ill-fitting tuxedo, clinging to the last shreds of his dignity.

And then, there was the missing conductor, Mr. Hermann. With a pale face, hollow eyes, and a drooping mouth, his very presence reeked of morbid neuroticism. His dress, however, was pristine in every aspect: from his uniform to his pocket watch chain aligned perfectly with his buttons, to the gloves which never left his hands—not even while eating. None ever saw him sleep. Like a ghost, by midnight, he would wander the train, inspecting every carriage. The "Danube Dawn" was his home. He knew every passenger's quirks, every worn cushion, every stain on the windows. Or so the passengers claimed—that is, those who remembered him.

One night, three years ago, while passing through a fog-drenched stop near Budapest, the conductor vanished—swallowed by the mist, gone without a trace.

Yet a story still circulates among the passengers: the vampire on the train took him. His body was hidden in one of the cars, and his eyeballs embalmed and nailed to the ceilings of cars one and ten so that even in death, he may keep watch over his beloved train.

Forevermore ...

— UTTU Editor: Verity

### 3110103 · Whose Nightmare?

**Excerpt from the Vampire Beyond Entity File**

File No.: XXX-1912-B (The Beyond - Vampire)

Clearance Level: Top Secret

The vampire is an extremely rare Beyond being bearing a strong resemblance to humans, yet with several defining traits: its skin is notably pale, its irises typically crimson—varying in depth with its tolerance for blood intake. Anatomical studies confirm that blood functions as one of its key energy sources. Vampires exhibit a compulsive craving for blood, particularly that of highly active animals.

Key Traits:

1. Bloodthirst

A vampire's obsession with blood often triggers bouts of extreme aggression and heightened tracking ability (showing acute sensitivity to the scent of blood). However, certain reports suggest blood is not their sole means of sustenance. A rare few can resist Bloodthirst altogether or survive on alternative food sources without dependence on blood.

2. Photophobia

Vampires exhibit a marked physiological aversion to sunlight. Prolonged exposure to strong daylight causes irreversible tissue damage. These entities cast no visible shadows or reflections—whether upon water, glass, mirrors, or polished metal surfaces.

3. Vulnerabilities

Ordinary physical attacks are largely ineffective. Only bullets forged from metals refined through specific alchemical techniques (most notably pure silver) can inflict permanent injury upon them.

4. Vampire Infected

When a vampire bites or exchanges bodily fluids with an individual possessing innate arcanum affinity, there is a high probability of "conversion."  
The infected undergo █–█ hours of violent physiological and psychological mutation, with a 93% mortality rate and over 96% incidence of mental instability among survivors. Those who endure the process ultimately become "the Infected," gradually gaining the creature's biological traits and mastering its arcane skills.

Note: Intelligence suggests "Manus Vindictae" is conducting ongoing experiments to convert ordinary humans directly into vampires. The success rate and stability of such transformations remain unknown.

Disclaimer

Both vampires and their infected derivatives are officially classified by the Foundation as "the Beyond," distinct from both humans and arcanists.

Last Updated: November 15, 1912

### 3110104 · Innocence Sealed Away

**Annabelle's Diary**

Outside, it's as dark as if someone had spilled a whole bottle of ink. The glass window reflects me and little William sitting opposite each other. I bet he's not really asleep! The kerosene lamp on the table casts our shadows on the wall. I stare at them, hold my breath, and clutch my teddy bear tight.

The train clatters along the rails, like someone's chasing after us, chasing us away from Istanbul, away from Uncle's house. When Ms. Dorothy was packing our clothes, her hands were shaking so much she couldn't even fasten the buttons on my shirt. Her face was pale as paper, lips pressed tight, as if the moment she opened her mouth, all the awful words trapped in her throat would come flying out like crows. She just said, "Children, hurry! Come with me." Everything happened too fast. I didn't even have time to pack all my dolls—Jenny, Jack, Nina ... I'm sorry, I couldn't bring you with me!

Where is this train taking us? Budapest ... Father's home. Will there be a warm fireplace there? Sweet Dobos cake? The smell of roses? I've already forgotten what Father looks like ... but he must be tall ... tall enough to protect us! Like the heroes in bedtime stories ... the powerful, brave, amazing vampires who only appear at night and vanquish everything in their path!

If only ... if only a vampire would really come! They'd drain the blood of all the bad people and protect good children who behave. So they'd surely scare away whatever's hiding in the dark, whatever it is that makes Ms. Dorothy's face go pale.

I keep praying silently, again and again. I believe the great vampire will come to save us!

### 3110105 · News or Rumor?

**Notice of the "Danube Dawn" Resumed Service - Vienna-Pannonian Railways**

Vienna-Pannonian Railways announced yesterday that after a full year of maintenance, the "Danube Dawn" will officially resume service next Wednesday. The new train is said to drastically reduce travel time between Istanbul and Vienna, strengthening connections throughout the Danube region.

Rumors, of course, soon followed. An engineer—who wished to remain anonymous—claimed that the train's remarkable performance might not be entirely due to human work. He reported glimpsing peculiar, Critter-like entities near the engine room and wheel bearings, somewhere between a beetle and a firefly in size, dark brown in color, with two slender antennae that glowed faintly red. The workers nicknamed them "Scarlet Nightmares."

"They seem... very fond of staying close to the engines," he said. "When the machinery runs smoothly, they're lively; when it doesn't, they bunch together and start ... glowing, bright red."

Vienna-Pannonian Railways declined to comment. However, several arcanobiology enthusiasts have suggested that these beings are far from ordinary Critters. They might, in fact, be familiars of vampires—social, volatile creatures said to feed on the blood of laborers.

"This isn't a metaphor," they insisted. "It's real."

"People have long said the disappearance of the 'Danube Dawn''s former conductor was connected to a vampire. Now these Critters show up, and somehow it's the same story again ... maybe the train itself is a familiar of the vampires ..."  
"Hah! If tickets weren't sold out, I'd get on board myself, maybe even catch a few to show everyone!"  
"Don't joke about that ..."

...

Whether the so-called "vampire familiar theory" holds any truth or not, the "Danube Dawn" will depart on schedule. Will it truly reshape the Danube region's transport network as promised by Vienna-Pannonian Railways? Has it truly been touched by supernatural power? Only time will tell.

### 3110106 · Measured Words

**Passenger Information Log**

...

Sonya, 60 years old  
A noblewoman, sharp-tongued and fussy, seemingly fleeing from the war.  
Sixty years of pride might have made it hard for her to face reality.

She was supposed to travel with her butler, but tickets for the "Danube Dawn" were nearly impossible to get.  
It seems war treats nobles and commoners alike, or perhaps her family's status isn't quite as lofty as she makes it seem.

After ten minutes of complaining about having to travel alone, with no butler to set her table or press the creases from her skirt, she finally boarded by herself.  
Do people really spend their whole lives learning to place their cutlery at a perfect forty-five-degree angle? In any case, she's the archetype of a noblewoman straight out of an oil painting, gown in hand. Let's hope that on her road of escape, she learns what the world is really like.

......

...

Arbuthnot, 45 years old  
Middle class. A decent, respectable gentleman.  
Nothing remarkable about his demeanor—just an ordinary man who fits neatly into his social standing.

A businessman with investments in Eastern European railways and oil.  
Vital infrastructure and strategic resources. He's well-connected and has an eye for opportunity.

The war cost him dearly, and he worries for his future.  
Special times demand special measures. What troubles him isn't death or hunger—it's the thought of losing a life of luxury forever.

......

...

### 3110107 · A "Song" Without an Owner

**The "Danube Dawn" — Rumor 2**

Passengers from Car No. 3 often come to me with their troubles—not over the tasteless stew or the unbearably chilly nights, but a sound: a crying that seeps through the partitions and burrows straight into the brain. It feels as if the train itself has come alive, endlessly recounting all the pain its body has endured through the years.

And so, tonight at 11:51 p.m., I went to Car No. 3, sat by the window, and quietly waited for the "cry" to come.

When the hour and minute hands aligned, I heard it—that delicate, indescribable rhythm. The tone rose and fell, turning in on itself, resonating at a frequency barely perceptible. To me, it was more like a ... song, one that no throat could ever produce—something primal, pure, flowing unconsciously from the soul.

Once the word "song" took shape in my mind, it stirred a memory. Along the Danube, there is an old legend of "the Song of the Spirit." They say it is the most beautiful melody ever imagined by humankind, able to pass through any physical barrier and reach the listener's innermost consciousness.

Could what I heard be that very song? Or was it the faint cry for help from the frail former conductor, Hermann, his decayed vocal cords vibrating through some supernatural force?

Then, without the slightest warning, the singing stopped.

The carriage sank into an absolute, suffocating silence ... as though the "Danube Dawn" itself, along with every living and dead thing within its belly, was holding its breath ... waiting for an unseen baton to fall again, or for whatever the song had briefly subdued ... to reawaken.

— UTTU Editor: Verity

### 3110108 · Call of the Blood Moon

**Nameless Nursery Rhyme**

Listen, the wind is wailing in the distance.  
Look, the crimson moon is rising into the sky.  
Child, don't linger here.  
Don't touch the berries wet with dew.  
For that tempting red hue  
already carries the smell of rust.

...

Listen, the wind weaves endlessly through the forest.  
Look, the bloodthirsty heralds wander here, their forms faint and shapeless.  
They have no hearts, no shadows, no bodies,  
only blood-red lips, blood-red eyes, and blood-red fangs.  
They will use those fangs to suck your little fingers,  
as if they were the ripest berries ...

### 3110109 · Echoes of the Past

???：...

???：Alright, moment of silence over.

???：Oh, I'm not a believer—not an atheist either. But I do respect her sacrifice.

???：Even if it was stupid and meaningless.

???：But, as I said before, there's no greater proof of loyalty than to follow a fool.

???：At least she died for a cause—her family legacy, her spirit of exploration, even that children's fairytale.

???：She was stupid, but she was faithful.

???：Were you expecting a warning first? I thought you'd know better after what you've been through.

???：Sorry, but you won't hear any praise from me for a sentimental commander.

???：If I were your superior, I'd tell you to rip out your own heart and let the others see what sentiment leads to.

???：Oh, come on. You and I both know this isn't the end for her.

???：For someone as devoted as her, death is nothing more than the achievement of eternal life.

???：She's not like those blind followers who cut off their tongues. In fact, at this very moment, I fear she's being confronted with her own beliefs.

???：Join her now, while the gate is still open.

???：Or she'll leave you behind for good.

### 3110110 · Before the End

???：Heavy rain will eventually cause a flood. It's an obvious deduction.

???：This is my final report. The era of mankind and birds is over, and the era of fish has begun.

???：But the fish will have to wait a little longer. Because you've delayed the doomsday by about an hour.

???：Every living person now owes you an hour of their lives. How big do you think your medal should be?

???：Certainly. Even if the umbrellas don't work, you still have an ark.

???：Just as long as you only take the "qualified animals" with you, like in old times ...

???：You should be able to continue to fight.

???：Please, let me finish my final report, even if you don't want to hear it.

???：Thanks to gravity, those on higher ground live longer in a flood.

???：Which gives you plenty of time to cuddle up with your greyhound and watch the end of the world together.

???：Or ... you could do what a soldier would and follow your orders till the very last moment.

???："Find that writer."

???：You know how important this mission is.

### 3110111 · Meaningless Questions

???：Another near-drowning. How nostalgic.

???：Take it easy. You can't move yet. Just stay right there and listen to the report.

???：Urgh …

???：Okay. You win.

???：You did it, noble savior. You turned the tide and saved the world at the last moment.

???：And I didn't see it coming. There. Are you satisfied? Can we get to the report now?

???：It's your loyal greyhound licking your wounds. She's doing whatever she can to wake you up—another metaphor, of course. I'm sure I don't need to explain it.

???：Just another reason not to wake up yet. The salt water will get in your eyes ...

???：And that'll just be embarrassing for everyone. So you just keep lying there and listen to me.

???：You almost drowned again, but not because you're a bad swimmer.

???：No, it's because you'd rather die than let that spinning wheel go.

???：But a spinning wheel is a spinning wheel, no matter how precious the thread it spins is—and you already have one.

???：What's the use in having two? You're the only spinner, after all.

???：And even if you tear it apart, its broken components can't be used to fix yours. Just because two things look alike doesn't mean they're interchangeable.

???：This is a universal truth. Not just for spinning wheels, but for animals, fathers and sons—mothers and daughters.

???：Just wait a moment longer. The greyhound's looking for your hat.

???：This is the only thing she can think to do besides first aid measures.

???：It's cute, isn't it? Seeing her so naive and superstitious. We should play along for a little longer.

???：Sure, you deserve a moment of quiet, savior.

???：Forget about the mess out there. Take a break.

???：Don't rest for too long, though. The greyhound's on her way back with your hat.

???：If you're not awake by the time she arrives, there's no telling what she'll do.

### 3110112 · No More Talks of "Humanitarianism"

**The Infected Transformation Experiment Record**

No.██

Date: ████.██.██  
Recorder: /

1. Objective:  
To study the effects of specific environmental stimuli, (particularly audio patterns, i.e. "the Song"), on the blood samples of the Infected, with a focus on their temporary mutation and fusion behavior.

2. Method Summary:  
· Collect four samples each from arcanist subjects (Group B) and human subjects (Group C), forming one experimental unit. Three units per group are used as control and stimulus sets.  
· Acquire high-activity vampire blood samples (Group A).  
· Under █████ conditions, expose Group B and C to Group A to █████.  
· Introduce "the Song" as the primary stimulus source.  
· Monitor Groups B and C in real time for changes.

...  
.........

4. Key Observations (Experiment Iterations 7–██):  
· Temporary Fusion and Mutation: Recipient samples (Groups B and C) exhibited the base arcanum traits of the source vampire post-fusion, confirming transformation into the Infected.  
· "Song" Effect: Confirmed that exposure to "the Song" significantly increases transformation probability. (Fusion rate averaged █.█% without stimulus, rising to ██.█% under stimulus.)  
· Extreme Rejection Response: All test subjects died rapidly following successful fusion and manifestation of ability.

......  
...

Note: Research will and must continue. If this succeeds, we may be able to turn ordinary humans into arcanists.

### 3110113 · Bad Luck

**Blood-Stained 0073-MV**

Mission Number: 0073-MV

Date: ████.██.██

Got the file on the new colleague today—Semmelweis. According to her record, she's a sharp, calm, and reliable investigator. With her joining us, this "odd" case should go a lot smoother. Still, before she arrives, Squad 77 has to do its part—at least handle the groundwork properly. Can't have her looking down on us. I think I've got a lead. Been stationed here for a while, and I keep hearing rumors about that so-called mystic train. You'd be hard-pressed to find another line with so many vampire stories attached to it, unless you're in one of ████'s horror novels. The conductress never replies to our messages—not to inquiries, columns, or even official investigation requests. Highly suspicious. On my list, that puts it right at the top—extremely, ridiculously suspicious! (Suspect there's a syntax error here. Recommend revision before submission.)

(I struck out your irrelevant rambling. Why do you always fill task logs with random digressions and pointless jokes?)  
(Okay, fine. Thanks. But you know this is just a draft — I'll rewrite the official report later.)  
(Then stop wasting the report forms. I couldn't even find a blank one last time and had to draw the format by hand ... I'll stake out the station tomorrow and see what I can learn about this "Danube Dawn.")

Date: ████.██.██

Did we just hit the jackpot? Officer Pyrrhos from the Nika checkpoint invited us to "talk." Guess our "covert" investigation wasn't so covert after all—or maybe the man's just sharp as hell. After getting the invite, first thing I did was review our recent operation. I wouldn't say flawless, but definitely clean ... Uh, in the formal report maybe I'll write that we "intentionally leaked intel to lure them in." Depends how it plays out.

Anyway, is it trap or cooperation? We'll only know once we go. Damn, I'm actually nervous. But the invite was polite ... and honestly, our investigation has been stalling. So yeah, let's go! I'm a proud member of Squad 77! My record's just as good as Semmelweis's, I'm just terrible at paperwork!  
Date: ████.██.██

Warehouse address: ████████████ stench heavy blood ████████ Invited by Officer Pyrrhos of Nika checkpoint we ███ him ████████ he's behind me he's searching ███████ this crate is a terrible hiding spot ████

### 3110114 · Third-Rate Novel

**An Ordinary Yet Unordinary Story**

Today's tale doesn't start with "once upon a time." It begins in "a quiet, ordinary village." In that village lived an equally ordinary little girl whose father was a vampire. But unlike him, she didn't live apart from humans. She felt a natural fondness toward both humans and arcanists. Perhaps because she was a hybrid—an "impure" half-vampire? In any case, for a long while, the girl lived peacefully with her parents.

Until a shell fell.

Yes, war. Again.

War brings death, and death spreads. Her family didn't escape it; one by one, they died. Only she and her uncle survived, wandering endlessly. But shells don't discriminate. Human, arcanist, vampire, or half-breed—none are spared. The girl was wounded. How badly? No one knows. Severe enough that she nearly died. Her uncle wanted to save her—his only remaining family. Even if it meant making a deal with the devil, he would.

He carried her to an old castle. Its mistress, as all stories go, was powerful and mysterious. She healed the dying hybrid girl. The price—if it could even be called that—was an overactive regenerative power that let her recover from any wound with ease.

Since then, the girl's body has always been wrapped in bandages.

### 3110115 · Echoes of the "Wind"

**To the UTTU Editorial Team**

Dear colleagues,

Regarding Corvus, the conductress of the "Danube Dawn." As you all know, she was once one of us and my most trusted partner. Thus, for personal reasons as well as curiosity, I have attempted to glimpse traces of the partner I once knew. I must admit, she's changed. There is barely a trace of the person I remember. The glimmer in her eyes remains, yes, but where that gaze now falls ... no longer has anything to do with me.

Still, in a way, our destinations aren't all too different. For that reason, I still cherish this brief journey we now share.

As for Ms. Pandora Wilson, it's been some time since she vanished. Since then, I've found it difficult to sense her through the wind. It's as if some unseen force quietly erased every trace of her. Yet lately, I've faintly detected something ... an echo which mimics the wind. I have tried many times to reestablish contact, but I've received no response. Even so, I still believe she is out there somewhere.

Despite this, UTTU continues to publish articles in her name and tone. I understand why. Losing her would mean losing the very spirit of UTTU—something none of us could bear. That is why I took it upon myself to handle external correspondence under her name. Yet, the unease lingers: we are all trying to imitate her—her style, her rhythm, the way she thought, the words she might have written ... but none of us truly understand the meaning behind them. For me, this imitation is like calling out a name in the dark, over and over again, knowing it will never answer back.

As for the strange happenings aboard the "Danube Dawn" and the many details pertaining to vampires ... I will explain everything in full once I have alighted.

Verity, UTTU Editor  
Written aboard the "Danube Dawn"

### 3210401 · 来说个故事吧

> Tell Me a Story

???：It was two years ago, or sixteen years hence, depending on which chronicle you follow.

???：To me, it was as vivid as yesterday. But to young minds like yours, it seemed a kind of chaos unheard of.

???：We should start from the beginning.

???：With the war coming to an end, France faced countless possibilities. Its people, weary yet filled with hope, longed for lasting peace.

???：The economy flourished for a time, and people from all walks of life came to France.

???：Still, the impacts of war inevitably surfaced. As the wartime factories closed, people struggled to survive. Their hardships gave rise to new demands, and before long, Paris became a swirl of competing ideas.

???：That very much depends on your perspective. If your only interest is in the story itself—not the context—you might think it irrelevant.

???：But I believe context matters. It keeps you from being thrown off by the details.

???：That said, neither the economy nor society is our chief concern today. What I share with you now is a story, hearsay really—

???：You presume correctly. A taxi driver caught up in the whirlpool of ideas that spun within Paris.

???：A heroine born tied to a fate that promises adventure at the cost of her life, who only ever desired a quiet life in the city she loved.

???：But fate does not ask for permission.

???：And when this journey comes to a close, regret will be all that remains.

???：It ends, as all stories must—whether that end is a happy one. You will soon find out.

### 3210302 · Under the Mask

A masquerade ball is taking place in Montmartre. It lacks a clear theme, but a mask is required for entry. So you pick up:

A polished silver mask with a polite smile

Everyone must appear proper.

A rough wooden mask carved with totem motifs

A mask can be "natural" too.

An enamel mask adorned with multicolored feathers

Beauty is always compelling.

A lifelike paper mask painted to match your face

Pretend, for now, that you are still yourself.

### 3210306 · Gaze Within

Before a shop window on the Champs-Élysées, you stop for a long time, so long you don't notice the sky changing. What you're staring at is:

A Klein-blue gown flowing with black liquid.

You have to admit, it's impeccable taste.

A restored illustration of ancient Critters.

Were they always this noisy?

A smoking pipe cut clean in half.

Makes you wonder where the other half is shown.

An astronomical clock with its hands reversed.

There must be a deeper metaphor.

### 3210402 · 说到生意

> Speaking of Business

???：I was running a crystal parlor in Paris back then.

???：People came for all sorts of products—from ordinary crystal ornaments to the enchanted ones—I had them all. I recognized a few of my customers in your Suitcase.

???：But many came seeking something I never put on the shelf—prophecy.

???：But our path through fate's garden is already set. No prophecy can change where it leads.

???：And I dislike putting a price on things that have no use.

???：So I made myself a promise: that only those I trusted would get a glimpse of what lies ahead of them—a gift, nothing more.

???：I am afraid that was never an option for me. I could never put a price on it. Whether one knows about the future or not, their fate remains unchanged.

???：A prophecy to fate is like a title to a book, and no bookseller should charge for the title alone.

???：So, I only offered the gift of prophecy to those who did not come seeking it.

???：To my surprise, it did something unexpected for my business. Word spread, and I recovered my investment soon enough.

???：Nor was I. Few things in life have ever surprised me, and this was much the same.

### 3210101 · Arcanist Observer Journal

**Welcome to Paris**

In a recent report, Paris was named the most preferred city for arcanists seeking residence. Respondents note that living in a metropolis dominated by humans is no easy feat—arcane skills, whether deliberate or incidental, often disrupt established social norms, inevitably creating confusion and conflict. Yet Paris, long known for its inclusivity toward people of many diverse backgrounds and traditions, stands apart. Parisian salons have come to regard arcane skills as a kind of marvelous and romantic art, and arcanists are frequently welcomed as guests of honor. To humans, Paris is a cradle of art; to arcanists, a haven of creativity.  
Reports indicate that several renowned arcane product entrepreneurs have opened new branches in Paris this year, their trust in the city drawing increased attention. The historic capital continues to attract arcanum talent and resources from across Europe. While human concerns about the spread of arcanum persist, many believe that more cities will soon follow in Paris's footsteps.

—Arcanist Observer Journal

### 3210403 · 预言之于人

> Prophecy and People

???：I saw doubt on Mademoiselle Brume's face. As a diviner, you encounter that look more often than people think.

???：Some have called me a schemer, a fraud, as the Vigiles Bureau did. Perhaps they are watching me even now, listening in to our very conversation.

???：Some were frightened by what I could see but kept coming back to sate their own curiosity.

???：Some showed nothing but disdain—not for me, but for the very idea of a predetermined future. I admired their courage.

???：Brume was not any of these. She was a fourth kind—one who tries to ignore the prophecy but, in doing so, only finds herself carried further into its stream.

???：Oops. Have I let it slip?

???：Well, I suppose there is no use pretending now. There was a disagreement about my decision to take a certain handful of arcane objects without consulting their rightful owners.

???：In the end, I paid all their penalties, among other not-so-charming consequences.

???：Not that it has done anything to prevent them from hounding me at every turn. To them, it seems I am only a fear-mongering schemer who knows secrets best left unknown.

???：Hmm. An excellent question.

???：Someone who walks between eras and through the "Storms," what is your view on fate?

???：Few people can imagine the nature of your position. No more than three, I would wager. And I wish I could say I was one of them.

???：Alas, I am but a puppet dangling at the end of fate's thread. My limited vision cages my imagination.

???：Though I may still see a little more than most people, I cannot do as I was meant to.

???：I have lived long enough to know better than to act against fate.

???：Though, in fact, what I would have done differs very little from what I was meant to do.

???：What do you think? Is it perhaps fate that determined my personality, or my personality determining my fate?

### 3210305 · Voices in Debate

You're just about to enjoy your Mont Blanc and a rich cup of coffee when the argument at the next table gets louder. They're bothering you, sure, but one person's view does strike you as the more reasonable one:

"Those who withdraw from society are running from their responsibilities."

Not much different from exile.

"There is no evil, only good that society has yet to recognize."

Standards are always shifting.

"A lover is the part of yourself that got lost among humankind."

Ah, the romantic flair.

"One must realize they were whole from the very beginning."

So what are we even chasing.

### 3210102 · Alarms Ringing

**A Missing Person Report**

Hello. Yes, I ... I need to report a missing person.  
My Aunt Mary. She's the youngest among the elders in our family ... She's a quiet person. She's always reading poetry we don't really get ... She doesn't drink, and she bakes the best cakes for the kids ... But now she's disappeared. We can't find her anywhere! All her belongings are gone too—her clothes, her books, her baking supplies. Who would take all that with them ...  
What does she look like? Oh, sure, she's small and thin, with neat, short brown hair. Ah, and she has a birthmark on her right arm ... No, no, we don't have a photo of her. Yes, we checked every album. I think it's strange too, but there isn't a single picture of her anywhere. She's part of our family, so I don't know why she's not in any of our group photos ...  
Wait, what do you mean you can't help? Too many missing people with no proof they exist? What does that even mean?

### 3210304 · From a Friend

A kind friend sends you a fortune-telling postcard, saying the pattern on the back reveals your fate for the week. You flip it over and see:

Soft, winding curves.

Curling around every solitary nail.

Sharp, angular lines.

Even the paper seems stung.

A square missing one corner.

Still a square, as you can tell.

A full circle.

The most complete, most flawless pattern.

### 3210404 · 说到这个名字……

> About That Name ...

???：And there she was, glowering at the empty passenger seat, yelling as if quite alarmed by someone inside.

???：I believe she called her ... Félicie? No ... Félicienne.

???：Yes. That was her name. Indeed, I once knew a Félicienne as well.

???：Well, it is far from a common name; that much is certain.

???：But then such a coincidence would border on ... theatrical, no?

???：I should like to think that fate has more elegant ways of crossing my path with Brume's. Though, of course, it is possible that fate favors the comedy of the absurd.

???：Heh heh, you do know how to make me laugh.

???：But Félicienne is no Napoleon. She never became a household name.

???：Perhaps her demeanor and her air of self-assurance gave you that idea, but I advise you to look a little bit closer.

???：That said, the Félicienne I knew would not take well to being mistaken for just any Félicienne.

???：Before the Second Industrial Revolution, the blood in her veins was more prized than liquid gold.

???：Indeed. Her family was once renowned for their command over thunder and lightning. Though their influence faded when humans learned to harness electricity for themselves.

???：Even so, the fortune her ancestors gathered would keep the family running for another two centuries. More than enough for our Félicienne to live a truly opulent lifestyle.

???：The Barons were known as fighters, heroes, Priests and Priestesses of Lightning. That legacy was passed down through generations.

???：Even a century after the last aluminum furnace bearing their name went cold, she still remembers the heat of fame, of success, of envy.

???：Precisely. When she was born, the "Storm" had not yet begun.

???：Not that it matters much, you see. In times like these, those born before the "Storm" often still cross paths with those of another era. Just like you and your friends, and myself and Brume.

???：Yes. The Félicienne I knew disappeared long ago.

???：Absent a miracle, I doubt anyone will ever see her again.

### 3210103 · Who's there?

**Stay Away**

"Are we seriously waiting all night? I'm not sure this is worth it."  
"Trust me, Paul. This is our big chance. I saw him go in right here."  
"The Catacombs? It's just a pile of bones. Everyone in Paris knows that."  
"He had suitcases. Heavy ones. I bet he's hiding something big. It's all ours when he leaves."  
"Did you hear that?"  
"Shh! He's coming out!"  
"Wait ... was the guy you saw ... this massive?"  
"No way ... look at his head. No, wait, what's wrong with his hands?"  
"He's coming this way ... Did he see us? He saw us!"  
"Run, Paul! Come on! Go, go, go!"

### 3210303 · Voices of Anger

Chants from marches and protests echo through the streets. You begin thinking about the roots of this era's unrest. Those surges of fear, anxiety, and anger—perhaps they come from:

The collapse of morals and the decay of the human heart.

Vile people with no sense of virtue.

A crisis of civilization and primal aggression.

Monkeys wearing clothes, nothing more.

A breakdown of connection and distance between people.

No one truly understands anyone else.

A world out of order and a universe out of balance.

Maybe the gods have lost their minds.

### 3210104 · Harsh Critique

**"The Sun King's Favorite"**

During Louis XIV's reign, life for most nobles of the sword was tough. They had to live at Versailles and follow elaborate etiquette to show their loyalty to the king. Earning even a small amount of royal favor meant extra rewards and yearly salaries, which helped them manage their debts.  
However, some families refused to exchange their swords for robes. Old aristocratic families with arcane bloodlines continued to flaunt their "unique" arcane skills. The Baron family was one of them. Once commanders of thunder and lightning on the battlefield, the Barons now performed parlor tricks at banquets to gain social status.  
As time passed, their wealth dwindled. After the Napoleonic era, these noble families found themselves deeply in debt, often selling off their possessions to survive. Investing in business became more common and was no longer seen as disgraceful. Fortunately, the rise of aluminum gave Monsieur Baron the chance to apply his arcane skills to this new industry, bringing his family a steady income.  
But their success didn't last. As new weapons and a new social order emerged, human technological progress made low-output arcane skills obsolete once more. Electric lights and wiring became commonplace, and the Hall-Héroult process made aluminum cheap, wiping out the Baron family's last viable business.  
What a pathetic glory of the past, what pitiful noble souls. These nobles desperately needed an opportunity, some support, a chance to change times and restore the arcanists' prestige ...

### 3210204 · Witness Report

**Elephant in the Dream**

Many mothers report that their children, scared and screaming, run back to them when playing in the park, only to have severe nightmares afterward. Each dream begins the same way: with a blurry image of a toy elephant.

### 3210105 · Depths of Myth

**A Record**

Everything is unfolding right now.  
No form of divination, prophecy, thought, or those feelings of déjà vu and dreams is just a coincidence.  
Imagine a vault that exists beyond both time and space. Inside it lies everything. Love and hatred, good luck and bad luck, the oldest archetypes and legends. Under normal circumstances, we could never reach it. But the Guiding One and the "Storm" have made this miracle possible. Now, the Archive of Imagery, known as the "Depths of Myth," has become visible to us.  
We owe a great deal to the pioneer whose research and courage made this possible. Her dedication and sacrifice deserve our deepest respect. We would all be wise to follow her example.  
...

### 3210405 · 现在伏笔都揭开了

> Now Every Clue Is Revealed

???：It is true; I have held back many important details.

???：Concerning matters like the nature of Félicienne and Brume's relationship, and Professor Roseau's true intentions.

???：But I believe it is not my place to disclose such things. People deserve to discover these things on their own.

???：And you have placed far too much importance on prophecy. The course of fate remains the same, whether I speak about it or not.

???：In this kind of business, one must remain neutral—all of my clientele understand that.

???：I take no sides, only payments.

???：The "Storm" has affected many things, including the Depths of Myth. One day, Laurent Roseau stumbled upon a rift that caused it to spill over, and his mind has been captured in its maelstrom ever since.

???：As for Felicienne—she was a soul lost within the Depths of Myth. Thus, their streams of fate inevitably crossed.

???：With the villains in their proper places, Brume's story had truly begun.

???：Ah, of course—and why not? My Matilda ... such a lovely, radiant girl. She has inherited my strength and added to it a vitality of her own. Those who speak with her often leave smiling.

???：As for her future, I cannot share it with you here.

???：But I can assure you: her path is one worth looking forward to.
